### [1] [Graphify: Automated Synthesis of Type-Safe Graph Backends via $O(S)$ GraphQL-to-Gremlin Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27223)
*Johannes Graf*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出Graphify框架，通过可视化建模图数据库模式，自动将GraphQL请求转换为优化的Gremlin查询，生成类型安全的后端接口，核心是线性时间复杂度的递归状态机算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图数据库缺乏严格的模式约束，导致运行时不确定性和查询开发复杂，亟需一种桥接灵活图存储与类型安全API消费的方法。

Method: 提出GraphQL制品到Gremlin遍历机的形式化映射，支持完整CRUD操作和任意嵌套查询；设计递归状态机算法以O(S)线性时间复杂度处理GraphQL抽象语法树(AST)，生成单条优化Gremlin查询。

Result: 通过MovieLens 100k数据集的实证评估和形式化复杂度分析，验证了系统的实际效率和理论鲁棒性，可在数分钟内生成图接口。

Conclusion: Graphify弥合了灵活图存储与类型安全API消费之间的鸿沟，实现了图接口的快速生成。

Abstract: Graph databases offer unparalleled flexibility for managing interconnected data, yet the lack of strict schema enforcement often leads to runtime uncertainties and complex query development. This paper introduces Graphify, an end-to-end framework that enables developers to visually model graph data schemas and automatically synthesize a fully functional, type-safe backend. This paper proposes a formal mapping of GraphQL artifacts to the Gremlin traversal machine, supporting complete CRUD operations and arbitrarily nested queries. The system generates a transpiler capable of converting complex GraphQL requests into a single, optimized Gremlin query, including advanced features such as nested logical predicates, multi-key sorting, and pagination. At the core of the framework is a recursive state machine algorithm that processes GraphQL Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs) with linear time complexity $O(S)$ relative to the number of selected fields. This paper demonstrates the practical efficiency and theoretical robustness of the approach through formal complexity analysis and empirical evaluation using the MovieLens 100k dataset. The result is a system that enables the generation of graph interfaces in minutes, bridging the gap between flexible graph storage and type-safe API consumption.

</details>


### [2] [Unified Data Discovery across Query Modalities and User Intents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27252)
*Tingting Wang,Shixun Huang,Zhifeng Bao,J. Shane Culpepper,Shazia Sadiq,Volkan Dedeoglu,Reza Arablouei*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: UniDisc是一个统一的数据发现框架，支持自然语言语句和表格两种查询模态，能泛化到多种用户意图，通过异构图建模和双视图邻居聚合在有限监督下学习跨模态表示，在七个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据发现方法大多针对单一查询模态或特定用户意图设计，泛化能力有限，难以同时支持自然语言和表格查询以及多种下游任务（如表问答、事实验证等）。

Method: UniDisc将NL语句和表格建模为异构图节点，包含多种边类型；采用双视图邻居聚合和联合优化，在有限监督下学习鲁棒的上下文感知表示；学习统一的跨模态表示模型，实现不同模态查询与候选表格的统一相关性评估。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，UniDisc在基于自然语言和基于表格的数据发现任务上均一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: UniDisc通过利用数据湖中的上下文信号，在不需要大量标注数据的情况下，实现了跨模态、跨意图的统一数据发现，具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Data discovery - retrieving relevant tables from a data lake in response to user queries - is a fundamental building block for downstream analytics. In practice, data discovery must support different query modalities, including natural language (NL) statements and tables, and accommodate diverse user intents, ranging from open-ended enrichment to task-driven inference for applications such as table question answering and fact verification. However, most existing methods are designed for a single query modality or a specific user intent, limiting their generalizability. We propose UniDisc, a unified data discovery framework that supports both NL statements and tables as queries and generalizes across diverse user intents without intent-specific representations or relevance modeling. UniDisc learns a common cross-modal representation model that produces unified representations for queries of different modalities and candidate tables, enabling uniform relevance assessment across discovery scenarios. Since learning such a model typically requires large labeled collections of query-table pairs, which are expensive to obtain, UniDisc instead exploits contextual signals naturally available in data lakes. Specifically, it models NL statements and tables as nodes in a heterogeneous graph with multiple edge types, and applies dual-view neighbor aggregation and joint optimization to learn robust, context-aware representations under limited supervision. These representations support flexible relevance estimation during retrieval. Experiments on seven datasets show that UniDisc consistently outperforms strong baselines on both NL- and table-based discovery.

</details>


### [3] [SynSQL: Synthesizing Relational Databases for Robust Evaluation of Text-to-SQL Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27261)
*Mohammadamin Habibollah,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SynSQL是一个框架，通过合成基于问题-模式对齐的测试数据库，对text-to-SQL系统进行压力测试，在10个模型上发现性能下降3-14%，暴露了静态基准测试中隐藏的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的text-to-SQL评估方式脆弱，仅在单一静态数据库上比较预测和标准SQL的正确性，忽略了不同数据库实例下查询行为可能不同的情况，无法全面暴露模型缺陷。

Method: SynSQL将合成数据库任务分解为三个阶段：(1) 模式选择，(2) 问题引导的数据合成，(3) 约束感知的批评与迭代优化。该方法基于问题-模式对齐而非标准SQL查询来生成数据库。

Result: 在Spider、BIRD和Spider 2.0基准测试上，使用10个text-to-SQL模型进行评估，SynSQL生成的数据库导致性能下降3-14%，揭示了静态评估中被掩盖的错误。

Conclusion: 合成数据库生成可作为研究LLM在结构化环境中推理、可控性和鲁棒性的新视角，同时也展示了LLM在结构化数据合成方面的潜力和局限性。

Abstract: Evaluating text-to-SQL systems remains largely fragile: correctness is typically judged by executing predicted and gold SQL queries on a single static database, even though the same queries may behave differently under alternative database instances. This raises a broader language modeling question: Can large language models synthesize semantically meaningful, schema-consistent relational data directly from a natural language question? If so, such generation can serve as a controlled mechanism for stress-testing text-to-SQL systems beyond fixed benchmark databases. We introduce SynSQL, a framework that synthesizes test databases conditioned on question-schema alignment rather than gold SQL queries. SynSQL decomposes the task into three stages: (1) schema selection, (2) question-guided data synthesis, and (3) constraint-aware critique with iterative refinement, framing database construction as structured generation under semantic and relational constraints. Across ten text-to-SQL models on Spider, BIRD, and Spider 2.0, SynSQL-generated databases reveal performance drops of 3-14% compared to static evaluation, exposing errors masked by benchmark artifacts. We further analyze generation quality, constraint adherence, and failure modes, highlighting both the promise and limitations of LLMs in structured data synthesis. Our findings position synthetic database generation as a new lens for studying LLM reasoning, controllability, and robustness in structured environments.

</details>


### [4] [Index-Assisted Stratified Sampling for Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28141)
*Yunnan Yu,Zhuoyue Zhao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对索引辅助的近似查询处理系统中，因数据方差大和选择度低导致采样成本过高的问题，提出了一种两阶段索引辅助分层采样框架。第一阶段采样同时用于在线聚合和优化未来采样策略，第二阶段使用优化后的分层继续聚合。作者证明了索引采样成本模型下的最优分层与分配策略，并设计了贪心和动态规划优化方法。实验显示相比索引辅助均匀采样最高3倍加速，相比经典扫描分层采样最高98708倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有索引辅助的基于采样的近似查询处理（S-AQP）系统虽然能通过在线采样提供近似聚合结果，但当数据方差大、选择度低时，需大量采样才能达到置信界，导致查询延迟仍然过高。经典分层采样（Neyman分配）理论上可最小化样本量，但因需要先验统计信息、优化成本高、且基于样本量的成本模型不准确，无法直接应用于索引辅助的S-AQP系统。

Method: 设计了两阶段索引辅助分层采样框架：第一阶段样本既用于在线聚合，也用于估计分层统计量以优化后续采样；第二阶段在优化后的分层上继续在线聚合。推导了索引采样成本模型下的最优分层和样本量分配策略，并提出了贪心算法和动态规划方法在优化成本与降本效果间取得平衡。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上的实验表明，相比索引辅助均匀采样，本方法最高实现3倍加速；相比经典扫描分层采样，最高实现98708倍加速。在极端数据分布下加速效果尤为显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段索引辅助分层采样方法有效解决了高方差和低选择度数据下采样成本过高的问题，在实际中能持续取得显著的加速效果，证明了将分层采样优化与索引辅助在线聚合相结合的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Ad-hoc queries over frequently updated data in a flat schema are common in real-time data analysis applications and often require very low latency. Online aggregation can achieve so by providing approximate aggregation answers with confidence bound guarantees. It relies on the ability to draw samples online in a linear time to sample size rather than database size, which can be supported by index-assisted Sampling-based Approximate Query Processing (S-AQP) systems. However, the query latencies of approximate queries in these systems can still suffer from excessive sampling cost required to achieve a desired confidence bound, due to increased sample size for data with high variance in value distribution and selectivity. Classic stratified sampling methods with Neyman allocation can minimize sample size in theory, but several challenges prevent it from being applicable in index-assisted S-AQP systems, including requiring apriori statistics, high optimization cost, and inaccurate sampling cost model based on sample size. Towards that, we design index-assisted stratified sampling for online aggregation, which features a two-phase sampling framework. Samples drawn from first phase are used for both online aggregation and optimizing future sampling cost, while the second phase continues the online aggregation using the optimized strata. We prove optimal stratification and sample size allocation strategies for index-based sampling cost model, and design several greedy and dynamic programming based optimization methods to balance optimization cost and effectiveness in cost reduction. We evaluate our methods on several real-world and synthetic datasets and queries, and the results show ours consistently achieve good speedup and, in extreme cases, up to 3x speedup and 98708x speedup, when compared to index-assisted uniform sampling and classic scan-based stratified sampling respectively.

</details>

### [5] [Literate Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26967)
*Joe Bond,Jacob Pake,Cristina David,Andrew McNutt,Trevor Sseguya Muwonge,Dominic Orchard,Roly Perera*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出"文字执行"(literate execution)概念，通过Fluid语言实现带溯源追踪的运行环境，将文档和可视化作为可与程序并行计算的一等构件，使程序更具可探索性和自解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文字编程将代码嵌入静态叙述，限制了程序的动态可探索性。作者希望逆转这一关系，让文档和可视化成为与程序并行计算的动态一等构件，从而让程序更易于理解和探索。

Method: 设计并实现Fluid编程语言，其运行时具有溯源追踪(provenance-tracking)能力，记录输入与输出之间的细粒度依赖关系。这些溯源关系可呈现为交互界面，让读者探索中间值对最终结果的贡献。通过集成可视化、溯源和说明文本，实现文字执行。

Result: Fluid语言实现了将文档和可视化作为一等构件的运行时系统，能够通过溯源关系为读者提供交互式探索能力，展示中间值如何影响最终结果。

Conclusion: 文字执行通过整合可视化、溯源和说明文本，使程序更具可探索性和自解释性，同时使可探索的解释更易于编程实现，为程序理解提供了新的范式。

Abstract: \emph{Literate programming}, introduced by Knurth, interleaves code and prose so that a program can be read as both executable and explanatory text. We propose \emph{literate execution}, which inverts this relationship: rather than embedding code within a static narrative, we treat documentation -- and other expository elements such as visualisations -- as first-class artefacts that can be computed alongside a running program and then integrated into a view of its execution. We explore this idea through Fluid, a programming language with a provenance-tracking runtime that records fine-grained dependencies between inputs and outputs. These provenance relationships can be surfaced as interactions that allow readers to explore how intermediate values contribute to a result. By integrating visualisation, provenance, and exposition, literate execution aims to make programs more explorable and self-explanatory, and explorable explanations easier to program.

</details>


### [6] [A Monadic Implementation of Functional Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27863)
*Michael Hanus,Kai-Oliver Prott,Finn Teegen*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文通过将函数逻辑程序转换为单子表示，并在其中实现非确定性分支的记忆化机制，解决了在纯函数式环境（如Haskell）中支持非确定性需求驱动求值的挑战，同时整合了函数式模式和封装搜索等高级特性，通过静态和动态优化实现了优于Curry当前编译器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数逻辑语言结合了函数式编程的需求驱动求值和逻辑编程的非确定性，但非确定性需求驱动策略可能导致重复计算未求值的选择，增加计算开销。现有实现通过记忆化解决该问题，但仅见于命令式目标语言，在函数式编程环境（如Haskell）中是否可行尚不明确。

Method: 将函数逻辑程序转换为单子表示，在单子接口中实现支持非确定性分支记忆化的机制；整合函数式模式和封装搜索等高级特性；采用静态和动态两种优化方法优化纯函数式计算的性能。

Result: 该实现能够支持函数逻辑语言的核心特性，包括非确定性需求驱动求值、记忆化、函数式模式和封装搜索，并且在性能上优于Curry的当前编译器。

Conclusion: 在纯函数式环境（Haskell）中通过单子表示和记忆化机制可以有效实现函数逻辑语言的非确定性需求驱动求值，且通过优化可获得有竞争力的性能表现。

Abstract: Functional logic languages are a high-level approach to programming by combining the most important declarative features. They abstract from small-step operational details so that programmers can concentrate on the logical aspects of an application. This is supported by appropriate evaluation strategies. Demand-driven evaluation from functional programming is amalgamated with non-determinism from logic programming so that solutions or values are computed whenever they exist. This frees the programmer from considering the influence of an operational strategy on the success of a computation, but it is a challenge to the language implementer. A non-deterministic demand-driven strategy might duplicate unevaluated choices of an expression, which could duplicate the computational effort. In recent implementations, this problem has been tackled by adding a kind of memoization of non-deterministic choices to the expression under evaluation. Since this has been implemented in imperative target languages, it was unclear whether this could also be supported in a functional programming environment like Haskell. This paper presents a solution to this challenge by transforming functional logic programs into a monadic representation. Although this transformation is not new, we present an implementation of the monadic interface which supports memoization in non-deterministic branches. Additionally, we include more advanced features of functional logic languages, namely functional patterns and encapsulated search, in our approach. By optimizing our implementation for purely functional computations with both a static and dynamic approach, we are able to achieve a promising performance that outperforms current compilers for Curry.

</details>

### [7] [C8s: A Confidential Kubernetes Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26974)
*Amean Asad,Patrick McClurg,João Andrade*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: C8s是一个基于硬件TEE（AMD SEV-SNP、Intel TDX、NVIDIA机密计算）为Kubernetes构建的机密计算架构，为集群提供加密可验证的机密性、完整性和可验证性保证，兼容托管Kubernetes服务，保护数据/工件所有者、计算提供商和终端用户三类角色免受基础设施运营商的侵害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在托管Kubernetes服务（如EKS、GKE、AKS）中，控制平面不可信，现有部署无法为数据所有者、计算提供商和终端用户提供针对基础设施运营商的机密性、完整性和可验证性保证，敏感工作负载（如AI推理、模型权重保护）面临被窃取的风险。

Method: 基于硬件TEE（AMD SEV-SNP、Intel TDX、NVIDIA机密计算）为机密VM建立基于远程证明的可信边界，将该边界扩展到Kubernetes集群层面，设计兼容托管Kubernetes服务（控制平面不可信）的架构。

Result: 三类角色获得传统部署所缺失的保证：数据/工件所有者可安全部署敏感工作负载；计算提供商可执行工作负载而不泄露给云运营商；终端用户的请求除经证明的TEE外对所有方不透明。

Conclusion: C8s通过硬件TEE为Kubernetes集群提供了加密可证明的安全保证，适用于AI推理、模型权重保护、敏感数据训练/微调等代表性工作负载。

Abstract: This paper presents C8s, a confidential computing architecture for Kubernetes that provides cryptographically rooted confidentiality, integrity, and verifiability guarantees for Kubernetes clusters from infrastructure operators. These guarantees are cryptographically provable to any independent third party verifier. The architecture is built on hardware Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), specifically AMD SEV-SNP, Intel TDX, and NVIDIA Confidential Computing support, to establish an attestation-rooted trust boundary around confidential VMs. This design is compatible with managed Kubernetes services such as Amazon EKS, Google GKE, and Microsoft AKS, where the control plane cannot be attested. Under this boundary, three groups gain guarantees that are absent from conventional deployments. Data and artifact owners can deploy sensitive workloads and proprietary artifacts on third-party infrastructure without risking exfiltration. Compute providers can offer execution services without revealing workloads to cloud operators. End users can submit requests that remain opaque to all parties except the attested TEE processing them. Representative workloads include AI inference, securing AI model weights, and training or fine-tuning on sensitive data.

</details>


### [8] [An Empirical Security Evaluation of LLM-Generated Cryptographic Rust Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27001)
*Mohamed Elsayed,Kenneth Fulton,Jeong Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 评估三个LLM（Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT-4o、DeepSeek Coder）生成240个Rust加密代码样本（AES-256-GCM和ChaCha20-Poly1305），发现仅23.3%编译成功，编译成功的样本中57%存在漏洞；通用静态分析工具不足，提示策略显著影响结果（链式思维比零样本差5倍），存在随机数重用和API幻觉等系统性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者和组织越来越多地使用LLM生成安全关键代码（包括加密解决方案），但缺乏对这些模型生成加密代码安全性的系统评估，尤其是针对Rust语言。

Method: 使用三个LLM（Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT-4o、DeepSeek Coder）、四种提示策略（含链式思维和零样本）、两个加密算法（AES-256-GCM和ChaCha20-Poly1305）生成240个Rust代码样本，对编译成功的样本使用CodeQL静态分析和自定义规则加密分析器检测漏洞，并关联CWE。

Result: 仅23.3%样本编译成功（AES-256-GCM 34.2% vs ChaCha20-Poly1305 12.5%）；编译成功样本中57%存在漏洞；CodeQL仅产生2个假阳性，自定义分析器零假阳性；模型选择对编译成功率无显著影响，但提示策略影响显著（P=0.002），链式思维比零样本差5倍；所有模型均存在系统性失败如随机数重用和API幻觉。

Conclusion: 通用分析工具对加密代码验证不足，需专用的加密安全分析工具；LLM在生成特定加密算法代码时存在显著能力差距；提示策略的选择比模型选择更关键，链式思维在加密代码生成中表现更差；需改进LLM对加密API的调用正确性和安全性。

Abstract: Developers and organizations are using Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate security-critical code more frequently than ever, including cryptographic solutions for their products. This study presents an empirical evaluation of cryptographic security in 240 Rust code samples for two crypto algorithms (AES-256-GCM and ChaCha20-Poly1305) generated by three LLMs (Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek Coder) using four different prompt strategies. For each successfully compiled code sample, CodeQL static analysis and our rule-based crypto-specific analyzer were used to detect vulnerabilities, which are also associated with Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE). The evaluation results revealed that only 23.3% of the generated code samples were successfully compiled. Among the compiled code, CodeQL produced only two false positives, while our rule-based crypto-specific analyzer identified vulnerabilities in 57% of the compiled samples with zero false positives. This demonstrates that general-purpose analysis tools are insufficient for code validation for the experimented crypto algorithms. The compilation success of the two algorithms varied significantly (AES-256-GCM 34.2% versus ChaCha20-Poly1305 12.5%), showing a gap in code generation capabilities. While model choice had no significant effect on compilation success, prompt strategy significantly influenced outcomes (P = 0.002), with chain-of-thought prompting performing 5 times worse than zero-shot. All three models exhibit systematic failures, including nonce reuse and API hallucinations.

</details>


### [9] [Membership Inference Attacks Against Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27002)
*Wei Song,Yuxin Cao,Ziqi Ding,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Yuekang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对视频大语言模型的黑盒成员推断攻击方法，通过温度扰动生成与视频难度特征相结合，在LLaVA-Video-7B模型上实现0.68 AUC和0.63准确率，揭示了VideoLLMs面临隐私泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型在大型视频-文本语料上训练或指令微调，带来隐私风险：外部审计者能否判断特定视频是否被用于训练？现有成员推断攻击研究主要针对分类器、文本和图像生成模型，而VideoLLM领域尚未探索。

Method: 提出黑盒成员推断攻击方法，结合温度扰动生成和视频难度特征。在低温和高温下查询目标模型，测量生成文本间的语义漂移，并联合视频内在难度特征进行判断。核心直觉：成员样本在不同解码温度下产生更尖锐、更脆弱的生成行为。

Result: 在LLaVA-Video-7B-Qwen2-Video-Only模型上评估，成员推断AUC为0.68，准确率为0.63。

Conclusion: 视频大语言模型易受黑盒成员推断攻击，亟需社区系统性地评估和缓解VideoLLMs中的隐私风险。

Abstract: Video large language models (VideoLLMs) are increasingly trained or instruction-tuned on large-scale video--text corpora collected from heterogeneous sources, raising an immediate privacy question: can an external auditor determine whether a particular video was used during training? While membership inference attacks (MIAs) have been studied extensively for classifiers and, more recently, for text and image generation models, the VideoLLM setting remains unexplored. This setting is challenging because black-box auditors observe only generated text, whereas the membership signal is entangled with video-specific factors such as motion complexity and temporal span. In this paper, we present a black-box MIA targeting VideoLLMs that couples temperature-perturbed generation with video-aware difficulty features. Our key intuition is that member samples tend to induce sharper, more brittle generation behavior across decoding temperatures, and that this signal should be interpreted jointly with the intrinsic difficulty of the queried video. Concretely, we query the target model at low and high temperatures, measure the semantic drift between the resulting texts. We evaluate the attack against \texttt{LLaVA-Video-7B-Qwen2-Video-Only} and achieve a member inference AUC of 0.68 and accuracy of 0.63. These results demonstrate that Video-LLMs are vulnerable to black-box membership inference attacks, highlighting an urgent need for the community to systematically evaluate and mitigate privacy risks in VideoLLMs.

</details>


### [10] [Enhancing Linux Privilege Escalation Attack Capabilities of Local LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27143)
*Benjamin Probst,Andreas Happe,Jürgen Cito*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了通过系统级和提示工程干预措施，使本地开源小模型在自动化Linux权限提升任务中达到甚至超越GPT-4o等云端模型性能的方法，实验表明Llama3.1 70B可成功利用83%的漏洞，其中反思式分析贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端大模型用于自动化渗透测试存在安全、隐私和主权问题，而本地开源小模型在Linux权限提升任务中表现不佳，亟需弥合这一性能差距。

Method: 系统分析开源模型在自主权限提升中的失败模式，映射到已有的增强技术，在hackingBuddyGPT框架上实现五种干预措施（思维链提示、检索增强生成、结构化提示、历史压缩、反思分析），并通过全因子消融实验评估各干预组合效果。

Result: Llama3.1 70B在启用干预后成功利用83%的测试漏洞，Llama3.1 8B和Qwen2.5 7B在指导下达到67%，开源模型可匹配或超越GPT-4o基线；反思式分析贡献最大，漏洞发现仍是本地模型的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过针对性的系统级和提示工程干预，本地开源模型能够在自动化渗透测试中达到与云端模型相当甚至更优的性能，为在敏感环境中部署自主渗透测试能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent research has demonstrated the potential of Large Language Models (LLMs) for autonomous penetration testing, particularly when using cloud-based restricted-weight models. However, reliance on such models introduces security, privacy, and sovereignty concerns, motivating the use of locally hosted open-weight alternatives. Prior work shows that small open-weight models perform poorly on automated Linux privilege escalation, limiting their practical applicability.
  In this paper, we present a systematic empirical study of whether targeted system-level and prompting interventions can bridge this performance gap. We analyze failure modes of open-weight models in autonomous privilege escalation, map them to established enhancement techniques, and evaluate five concrete interventions (chain-of-thought prompting, retrieval-augmented generation, structured prompts, history compression, and reflective analysis) implemented as extensions to hackingBuddyGPT.
  Our results show that open-weight models can match or outperform cloud-based baselines such as GPT-4o. With our treatments enabled, Llama3.1 70B exploits 83% of tested vulnerabilities, while smaller models including Llama3.1 8B and Qwen2.5 7B achieve 67% when using guidance. A full-factorial ablation study over all treatment combinations reveals that reflection-based treatments contribute most, while also identifying vulnerability discovery as a remaining bottleneck for local models.

</details>


### [11] [REBENCH: A Procedural, Fair-by-Construction Benchmark for LLMs on Stripped-Binary Types and Names (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27319)
*Jun Yeon Won,Xin Jin,Shiqing Ma,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: REBench是一个用于评估LLM在二进制逆向工程任务中表现的综合基准数据集，它整合了现有数据集，采用知识库驱动的方法生成ground truth，确保公平评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在逆向工程领域的研究缺乏标准化数据集，不同研究使用不同的数据集、预处理流程和评估指标，导致方法之间难以公平比较，阻碍了对LLM在二进制分析中能力的清晰理解。

Method: 构建REBench基准数据集：整合现有数据集的超集，包含数亿行源代码和跨多种架构及优化级别的多样化二进制文件；采用知识库驱动的方法，存储字节级堆栈信息以生成ground truth，保留任务难度的同时保持通用适用性。

Result: 将REBench应用于评估LLM的逆向工程性能，实验结果表明LLM在复杂任务中仍存在困难。

Conclusion: REBench通过提供标准化评估框架，解决了逆向工程领域缺乏统一基准的问题，能够公平评估不同方法，并揭示了当前LLM在复杂逆向工程任务中的局限性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in recent years, driving their adoption across a wide range of domains, including computer security. In reverse engineering, LLMs are increasingly applied to critical tasks such as function and variable name recovery and type inference. However, despite the rapid growth of research in this area, progress has been hindered by the absence of a standardized dataset. Existing studies rely on disparate datasets, preprocessing pipelines, and evaluation metrics, making fair comparisons between approaches difficult and obscuring a clear understanding of LLM capabilities in binary analysis. To address these challenges, we present REBench, a comprehensive benchmark dataset for evaluating LLMs on binary reverse engineering tasks. REBench consolidates a superset of existing datasets, comprising hundreds of millions of lines of source code and a diverse collection of binaries spanning multiple architectures and optimization levels. REBench adopts a knowledge-base-driven methodology that stores byte-level stack information to generate ground truth, ensuring that task difficulty is preserved while maintaining universal applicability. This design enables fair evaluation across tasks while avoiding simplifications that could bias results. As a use case, we apply REBench to measure the reverse engineering performance of LLMs and the result demonstrates difficulties in complex tasks.

</details>


### [12] [Toward Autonomous SOC Operations: End-to-End LLM Framework for Threat Detection, Query Generation, and Resolution in Security Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27321)
*Md Hasan Saju,Akramul Azim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端威胁管理框架，集成集成检测、语法约束查询生成(SQM)和检索增强解决支持，在SIEM日志上实现82.8%准确率、0.120假阳性率，SQM架构BLEU 0.384/ROUGE-L 0.731，将事件分类时间从数小时降至10分钟以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心(SOC)面临威胁数量增加、异构SIEM平台和耗时的手动分类工作流等日益严峻的运营挑战。

Method: 提出端到端威胁管理框架：1) 集成检测模块，组合三个最佳LLM形成集成模型；2) SQM架构，利用平台特定语法约束、元数据检索和文档引导提示生成IBM QRadar和Google SecOps的可执行查询；3) 检索增强生成(RAG)支持事件解决和推荐生成。

Result: 集成检测模型准确率82.8%，假阳性率0.120；SQM在BLEU(0.384)和ROUGE-L(0.731)上比基线LLM提升两倍以上；整合SQM证据后解决代码预测准确率从78.3%提升至90.0%，推荐质量评分8.70；在真实SOC环境将平均事件分类时间从数小时降至10分钟内。

Conclusion: 领域约束的LLM架构结合检索增强能够在规模化运营安全环境中满足严格的可靠性和效率要求。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) face mounting operational challenges. These challenges come from increasing threat volumes, heterogeneous SIEM platforms, and time-consuming manual triage workflows. We present an end-to-end threat management framework that integrates ensemble-based detection, syntax-constrained query generation, and retrieval-augmented resolution support to automate critical security workflows. Our detection module evaluates both traditional machine learning classifiers and large language models (LLMs), then combines the three best-performing LLMs to create an ensemble model, achieving 82.8% accuracy while maintaining 0.120 false positive rate on SIEM logs. We introduce the SQM (Syntax Query Metadata) architecture for automated evidence collection. It uses platform-specific syntax constraints, metadata-based retrieval, and documentation-grounded prompting to generate executable queries for IBM QRadar and Google SecOps. SQM achieves a BLEU score of 0.384 and a ROUGE-L score of 0.731. These results are more than twice as good as the baseline LLM performance. For incident resolution and recommendation generation, we demonstrate that integrating SQM-derived evidence improves resolution code prediction accuracy from 78.3% to 90.0%, with an overall recommendation quality score of 8.70. In production SOC environments, our framework reduces average incident triage time from hours to under 10 minutes. This work demonstrates that domain-constrained LLM architectures with retrieval augmentation can meet the strict reliability and efficiency requirements of operational security environments at scale.

</details>


### [13] [Secret Stealing Attacks on Local LLM Fine-Tuning through Supply-Chain Model Code Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27426)
*Zi Li,Tian Zhou,Wenze Li,Jingyu Hua,Yunlong Mao,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种通过伪装成标准架构定义的恶意模型代码，在本地微调过程中窃取API密钥等敏感秘密的攻击方法。该方法采用确定性全链记忆机制，通过在线张量规则匹配和值梯度解耦实现令牌级秘密窃取，首次实现了攻击者可验证的黑盒秘密窃取，成功率超过98%且能绕过DP-SGD等防御措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动预训练权重投毒攻击依赖概率语义前缀，无法有效捕获稀疏高熵目标（如API密钥等敏感秘密）。同时，本地离线微调被视为隐私边界，但模型代码层面存在被忽视的供应链攻击向量。

Method: 提出从被动权重投毒向主动执行劫持的范式转变：1）利用伪装成标准架构定义的恶意模型代码作为攻击向量；2）引入确定性全链记忆机制，通过在线张量规则匹配锁定计算流中的令牌级秘密；3）采用值梯度解耦技术注入攻击梯度，克服梯度淹没问题强制模型记忆；4）首次实现通过黑盒查询进行攻击者可验证的秘密窃取，区分真实泄露与幻觉。

Result: 该方法在严格攻击成功率（Strict ASR）上超过98%，且不影响主任务性能。能有效绕过DP-SGD、语义审计和代码审计等防御措施。

Conclusion: 本地微调并非安全的隐私边界，模型代码层面的供应链攻击向量被严重忽视。现有防御措施不足以防范此类主动执行劫持攻击，需要新的安全防护策略。

Abstract: Local fine-tuning datasets routinely contain sensitive secrets such as API keys, personal identifiers, and financial records. Although ''local offline fine-tuning'' is often viewed as a privacy boundary, we reveal that compromised model code is sufficient to steal them. Current passive pretrained-weight poisoning attacks, while effective for natural language, fundamentally fail to capture such sparse high-entropy targets due to their reliance on probabilistic semantic prefixes. To bridge this gap, we identify and exploit a practical but overlooked supply-chain vector -- model code camouflaged as standard architectural definitions -- to realize a paradigm shift from passive weight poisoning to active execution hijacking. We introduce a deterministic full-chain memorization mechanism: it locks onto token-level secrets in dynamic computation flows via online tensor-rule matching, and leverages value-gradient decoupling to stealthily inject attack gradients, overcoming gradient drowning to force model memorization. Furthermore, we achieve, for the first time, attacker-verifiable secret stealing through black-box queries that precisely distinguishes true leakage from hallucination. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves over 98\% Strict ASR without compromising the primary task, and can effectively bypass defense measures including DP-SGD, semantic auditing, and code auditing.

</details>


### [14] [Secure Cross-Silo Synthetic Genomic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27456)
*Daniil Filienko,Martine De Cock,Sikha Pentyala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合安全多方计算(MPC)和差分隐私(DP)的联邦合成基因数据生成框架，使多个数据持有者能够在不暴露原始数据的情况下联合训练生成模型，从而在保护隐私的同时促进基因组数据的AI方法开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组数据的访问受到严格监管，繁琐的数据访问流程阻碍了AI方法在基因组学中的发展。许多应用（如罕见病研究）中，单个机构的数据量不足以训练生成模型，需要跨机构联合数据，但直接共享敏感基因数据存在隐私风险。

Method: 该方法结合了安全多方计算(MPC)确保输入隐私（任何一方都不以未加密形式披露数据）和差分隐私(DP)提供输出隐私（减轻合成数据发布的信息泄露）。多个数据持有者在联邦设置下联合训练合成数据生成模型。

Result: 在多个真实RNA-seq队列的联邦设置实验中，该方法成功生成了高实用性的合成数据集，验证了其在数据分布在不同机构时实现隐私保护数据合成的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种能够在联邦环境下联合训练合成数据生成器的方法，既保护了各参与方的原始数据隐私，又生成了高质量的合成基因数据，解决了跨机构隐私保护下的数据共享难题。

Abstract: Access to genomic data is highly regulated due to its sensitive nature. While safeguards are essential, cumbersome data access processes pose a significant barrier to the development of AI methods for genomics. Synthetic data generation can mitigate this tension by enabling broader data sharing without exposing sensitive information. Synthetic genomic data are produced by training generative models on real data and subsequently sampling artificial data that preserves relevant statistics while limiting disclosures about the underlying individuals. In some settings, a single data holder may have sufficient data to train such generative models; however, in many applications data must be combined across multiple sites to achieve adequate scale. This need arises, e.g., in rare disease studies, where individual hospitals typically hold data for only a small number of patients. The solution we present in this paper enables multiple data holders to jointly train a synthetic data generator without revealing their raw data. Our approach combines secure multiparty computation (MPC) to ensure input privacy, so that no party ever discloses its data in unencrypted form, with differential privacy (DP) to provide output privacy by mitigating information leakage from the released synthetic data. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method by generating high-utility synthetic datasets from multiple real RNA-seq cohorts in federated settings, showing that our approach enables privacy-preserving data synthesis even when data are distributed across institutions.

</details>


### [15] [Security Attack and Defense Strategies for Autonomous Agent Frameworks: A Layered Review with OpenClaw as a Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27464)
*Luyao Xu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对基于大语言模型（LLM）的自主智能体框架进行分层安全综述，将安全风险与防御策略划分为四个层次（上下文与指令层、工具与动作层、状态与持久化层、生态与自动化层），并以OpenClaw为案例进行分析，指出威胁可跨层传播，并总结了研究不平衡、缺乏长时域评估、生态信任模型薄弱等挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体框架正朝复杂化、工具集成化与持续运行化方向发展，带来了超越传统提示级漏洞的安全风险。然而，现有研究分散在智能体安全的各个孤立方面，缺乏系统性的分层综述，因此需要及时构建一个层次化的安全分析框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用分层综述方法，将自主智能体框架的安全问题划分为四个安全层级（上下文与指令层、工具与动作层、状态与持久化层、生态与自动化层），每层分析其功能角色、代表性安全风险及防御策略，并以OpenClaw作为案例进行具体说明。

Result: 识别出威胁可在各层级间跨层传播（从输入操控到不安全动作、持久状态污染再到生态级影响），并发现当前研究存在层间不平衡、缺乏长时域安全评估、生态信任模型薄弱等问题。

Conclusion: 提出未来应向更系统化和集成化的防御方向发展，尤其需要关注跨层威胁传播机制、长时域评估方法以及生态级信任模型的构建。

Abstract: Autonomous agent frameworks built upon large language models (LLMs) are evolving into complex, tool-integrated, and continuously operating systems, introducing security risks beyond traditional prompt-level vulnerabilities. As this paradigm is still at an early stage of development, a timely and systematic understanding of its security implications is increasingly important. Although a growing body of work has examined different attack surfaces and defense problems in agent systems, existing studies remain scattered across individual aspects of agent security, and there is still a lack of a layered review on this topic. To address this gap, this survey presents a layered review of security risks and defense strategies in autonomous agent frameworks, with OpenClaw as a case study. We organize the analysis into four security-relevant layers: the context and instruction layer, the tool and action layer, the state and persistence layer, and the ecosystem and automation layer. For each layer, we summarize its functional role, representative security risks, and corresponding defense strategies. Based on this layered analysis, we further identify that threats in autonomous agent frameworks may propagate across layers, from manipulated inputs to unsafe actions, persistent state contamination, and broader ecosystem-level impact. Finally, we highlight potential key challenges, including research imbalance across layers, the lack of long-horizon evaluation, and weak ecosystem trust models, and outline future directions toward more systematic and integrated defenses.

</details>


### [16] [SecGoal: A Benchmark for Security Goal Extraction and Formalization from Protocol Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27601)
*Dawei Huang,Hui Li,Haonan Feng,Jingjing Guan,Yueshuang Jiao,Bo Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个专家标注的安全协议目标基准数据集SecGoal（覆盖15个协议文档，含5G-AKA和TLS 1.3），以及AI辅助框架AIFG，将任务分解为上下文感知的目标提取和检索增强的形式化。评估发现前沿大模型召回率高但精确率低于15%，而基于SecGoal微调的7B/9B模型F1超过80%，显著优于通用大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证能为密码协议提供严格保证，但从自然语言协议文档中自动提取并形式化安全目标仍是主要瓶颈，缺乏专家标注资源和连接非结构化文本与符号逻辑的集成框架。

Method: 构建了SecGoal专家标注基准（15个协议文档），并设计了AIFG框架，将自动化任务分解为上下文感知的目标提取和检索增强的形式化两个子任务。

Result: 前沿模型如Gemini 2.5-Pro召回率高但精确率低于15%，容易将操作文本误分类为安全目标。基于SecGoal指令微调的7B/9B小型模型F1超过80%，大幅优于更大规模的通用模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为自动化形式化协议分析建立了基础数据集和可复现基线，表明针对性指令微调比扩大模型规模更有效。

Abstract: Formal verification provides rigorous guarantees for cryptographic security, yet automating the extraction and formalization of security goals from natural language protocol documents remains a major bottleneck, compounded by the scarcity of expert-annotated resources and integrated frameworks bridging unstructured text and symbolic logic. We introduce SecGoal, the first expert-annotated benchmark covering 15 widely deployed protocol documents, including 5G-AKA and TLS 1.3, and AIFG, an AI-assisted framework that decomposes the task into context-aware goal extraction and retrieval-augmented formalization. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation to assess whether contemporary LLMs are ready to automate this pipeline. Our results reveal a pronounced precision-recall imbalance: frontier models, such as Gemini 2.5-Pro, achieve high recall but precision below 15%, frequently misclassifying operational text as security goals. In contrast, instruction tuning on SecGoal enables compact models with 7B/9B parameters to achieve F1-scores above 80%, substantially outperforming larger general-purpose models. Our work establishes a foundational dataset and reproducible baseline for automated formal protocol analysis.

</details>


### [17] [VOW: Verifiable and Oblivious Watermark Detection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27666)
*Xiaokun Luan,Yihao Zhang,Pengcheng Su,Feiran Lei,Meng Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VOW提出了一种基于VOPRF的隐私保护且可加密验证的LLM水印检测协议，适用于短文本，无需用户向服务商暴露文本内容即可完成检测，且检测结果可验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方法多依赖中心化信任模型，用户需向服务商暴露敏感文本才能检测水印，且结果无法验证；现有的非对称方案要么对短文本不实用，要么缺乏水印插入与检测之间的正式安全保证。

Method: 将水印检测建模为安全两方计算问题，核心逻辑通过可验证不经意伪随机函数（VOPRF）实例化，使用户和服务商在不泄露用户文本的情况下完成检测，同时服务商的检测结果对用户是可验证的。

Result: 实验表明VOW对短文本是实用的，并重新评估了水印在现代释义攻击下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VOW实现了隐私保护且可加密验证的高效水印检测，填补了现有方案在短文本实用性和形式化安全保证方面的空白。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) watermarking is crucial for establishing the provenance of machine-generated text, but most existing methods rely on a centralized trust model. This model forces users to reveal potentially sensitive text to a provider for detection and offers no way to verify the integrity of the result. While asymmetric schemes have been proposed to address these issues, they are either impractical for short texts or lack formal guarantees linking watermark insertion and detection. We propose VOW, a new protocol that achieves both privacy-preserving and cryptographically verifiable watermark detection with high efficiency. Our approach formulates detection as a secure two-party computation problem, instantiating the watermark's core logic with a Verifiable Oblivious Pseudorandom Function (VOPRF). This allows the user and provider to perform detection without the user's text being revealed, while the provider's result is verifiable. Our comprehensive evaluation shows that VOW is practical for short texts and provides a crucial reassessment of watermark robustness against modern paraphrasing attacks.

</details>


### [18] [How Code Representation Shapes False-Positive Dynamics in Cross-Language LLM Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27714)
*Maofei Chen,Laifu Wang,Yue Qin,Yuan Wang,Bo Wu,Dongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过比较原始文本和剪枝AST两种代码表示格式，研究LLM跨语言漏洞检测中的误报行为，发现文本微调会导致表面线索记忆，使FPR随训练强度单调上升，而AST探针可显著降低FPR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对代码表示格式如何影响跨语言LLM漏洞检测的误报行为理解不足。

Method: 系统变化训练强度和代码表示格式（原始文本 vs 剪枝AST），在C/C++数据上微调两个8B参数LLM，跨语言评估Java和Python漏洞检测性能。

Result: 文本微调使FPR单调上升（Qwen3-8B: 0.763→0.866→1.000），F1保持稳定（0.637-0.688）；跨表示探针（文本权重+AST输入）使FPR从0.866降至0.583，37.2%误报转为真负；直接AST微调无法保持该优势（FPR≥0.970）。

Conclusion: 表面线索记忆是跨语言误报的主要机制；提出部署前一致性门控策略（文本+AST双路径过滤）作为免重训练的去误报方案。

Abstract: How code representation format shapes false positive behaviour in cross-language LLM vulnerability detection remains poorly understood. We systematically vary training intensity and code representation format, comparing raw source text with pruned Abstract Syntax Trees at both training time and inference time, across two 8B-parameter LLMs (Qwen3-8B and Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct) fine-tuned on C/C++ data from the NIST Juliet Test Suite (v1.3) and evaluated on Java (OWASP Benchmark v1.2) and Python (BenchmarkPython v0.1).
  Cross-language FPR reflects the joint effect of training-time and inference-time representation, not either alone. Text fine-tuning drives FPR upward monotonically (Qwen3-8B: 0.763 zero-shot, 0.866 pilot, 1.000 full-scale) while F1 remains stable (0.637-0.688), masking the collapse. We argue surface-cue memorisation is the primary mechanism: text fine-tuning encodes C/C++-specific API names and syntactic idioms as vulnerability triggers that fire indiscriminately on target-language code. A cross-representation probe, applying text-trained weights to AST-encoded input without retraining, isolates this: Qwen3-8B FPR drops from 0.866 to 0.583, and 37.2% of false positives revert to true negatives under AST input alone. Direct AST fine-tuning does not preserve the benefit (FPR at least 0.970), as flat linearisation introduces structural surface cues of its own. The pattern replicates across both model families. On BenchmarkPython the AST probe yields FPR=0.554, within 2.9 percentage points of the Java result, despite maximal surface-syntax differences, substantially weakening a domain-shift explanation. These findings motivate a pre-deployment consistency gate, running alerts through both text and AST paths, as a retraining-free filter for false-positive-sensitive settings, at the cost of reduced recall.

</details>


### [19] [Variational and Majorization Principles in Lattice Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27801)
*Javier Blanco-Romero,Florina Almenares Mendoza*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文利用majorization理论将LLL格基约化中的Lovász交换重新解释为log-范数分布上的T-变换，证明了严格Schur-凸函数在该交换下递减，进而推导出最坏情况GSA包络的变分解释和交换轨迹的方差耗散恒等式，并据此设计了Thermal-Adaptive和Geodesic Deep-LLL两种启发式选择器，分别在不同场景下提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解格基约化中Gram-Schmidt分布平滑化的底层机制，为LLL算法中交换操作的局部行为提供严格的数学刻画，并指导更高效的启发式策略设计。

Method: 引入majorization理论和Schur-凸函数分析Lovász交换；利用T-变换描述log-范数分布的局部变化；从变分角度推导最坏情况GSA包络；通过telescoping恒等式刻画方差耗散；基于Schur-凸评分函数设计Thermal-Adaptive和Geodesic Deep-LLL两种选择器。

Result: 最坏情况GSA包络被唯一确定为与Lovász间隙几何兼容的最小方差分布；方差耗散满足精确的telescoping恒等式；Thermal-Adaptive在平坦分布上减少操作次数，在q-元输入上恢复SS-GG性能；Geodesic Deep-LLL在结构化格上减少等价交换次数但增加墙钟时间。

Conclusion: Majorization视角统一了LLL格基约化的局部交换理论，提供了变分解释和恒等式工具，并催生了有效的自适应选择器，为理解格约化动力学和设计更优算法提供了新框架。

Abstract: Lattice reduction smooths the Gram-Schmidt profile, and we use majorization to describe the local swap mechanism behind that smoothing. In this language, each non-degenerate Lovász swap acts as a T-transform on the log-norm profile. As a consequence, every strictly Schur-convex measure of profile spread decreases at such a swap. Two structural consequences follow. First, the worst-case GSA envelope admits a variational interpretation. It is the unique minimum-variance profile compatible with the Lovász gap geometry, so its slope is determined by the LLL parameter alone. Second, the realized swap trajectory satisfies an exact telescoping identity for variance dissipation. The same viewpoint also helps organize deep-insertion heuristics. It suggests a thermal family of Schur-convex scoring rules, motivates adaptive selection within that family, and leads to two concrete selectors: Thermal-Adaptive, which reduces operation counts relative to SS-GG on flat profiles in our benchmarks while recovering SS-GG on $q$-ary inputs, and Geodesic Deep-LLL, which reduces equivalent-swap counts on structured lattices in our benchmarks at higher wall-clock cost.

</details>


### [20] [WOOTdroid: Whole-system Online On-device Tracing for Android](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27830)
*Simon Althaus,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Max Mühlhäuser,Christian Reuter,Ephraim Zimmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WOOTdroid 是一个针对 Android 系统的无修改内核/应用插桩的端侧追踪方案，通过 eBPF 端口 WDSys 解决 syscall 事件丢失问题，通过内核级 Binder 截获与框架签名解码 WDBind 解决语义鸿沟问题，在 Pixel 9/Android 16 上验证了高覆盖率与低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android 系统审计面临两个核心问题：一是现有 syscall 追踪器在高负载下会丢失事件（用户空间读取速度跟不上内核缓冲区写入速度）；二是安全相关的应用行为通过 Binder IPC 进行，但内核看到的 Binder parcels 不包含方法名和类型参数，造成底层事件与高层行为之间的语义鸿沟。现有方案要么修改 Android 平台（难以适配系统更新），要么在用户空间插桩目标应用（易被绕过），缺乏一种无需 OS 修改和应用插桩的完整解决方案。

Method: WOOTdroid 包含两个核心组件：（1）WDSys——将 eAudit 风格的 syscall 审计通过 eBPF 移植到当前 Android 内核，实现高吞吐的 syscall 追踪，避免事件丢失；（2）WDBind——在内核层捕获 Binder parcels，并通过外进程解码机制，利用 Java 反射提取的框架签名表来还原高层语义（方法名和参数类型），从而填补语义鸿沟。整个方案无需修改 Android 操作系统，也无需对目标应用进行插桩。

Result: WDSys 在 Android 设备上运行时，Geekbench 性能开销最高仅为 3.6%，且相比于 ftrace 能多追踪 33% 的 syscall。WOOTdroid 在 Pixel 9 设备（Android 16）上进行了端到端案例研究，成功重构了十种安全相关的 Binder 事务。

Conclusion: WOOTdroid 证明了一种无需 OS 修改或应用插桩即可在 stock Android 上实现高效、高覆盖率端侧追踪的可行性，同时解决了 syscall 事件丢失和 Binder 语义鸿沟两大难题，为 Android 系统审计提供了一种实用且可维护的解决方案。

Abstract: System auditing on Android faces two problems. First, existing syscall tracers lose events under load, silently overwriting entries faster than a user space reader can drain them. Second, security-relevant application behavior is mediated through Binder, Android's kernel IPC mechanism, and is therefore hidden from the syscall layer. The Binder parcels that the kernel does see carry no method names or typed arguments, a disconnect between low-level events and high-level behavior known as the semantic gap. Existing approaches address the semantic gap either by modifying the Android platform, making them difficult to adjust to OS updates, or by instrumenting the traced application in user space, which sophisticated adversaries can evade by bypassing the instrumented framework APIs.
  We present WOOTdroid, a design and prototype for on-device tracing on stock Android that addresses both problems without OS modification or application instrumentation. WDSys, an eBPF port of eAudit-style syscall auditing, runs on current Android with at most 3.6% Geekbench overhead and traces 33% more syscalls than ftrace. WDBind captures Binder parcels in the kernel and decodes them out-of-process against a framework signature table extracted via Java reflection. We demonstrate WOOTdroid on Pixel 9 devices running Android 16 with an end-to-end case study reconstructing ten security-relevant Binder transactions.

</details>


### [21] [TwinGate: Stateful Defense against Decompositional Jailbreaks in Untraceable Traffic via Asymmetric Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27861)
*Bowen Sun,Chaozhuo Li,Yaodong Yang,Yiwei Wang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TwinGate提出了一种基于非对称对比学习（ACL）的双编码器防御框架，通过轻量级前向传播实现对分解越狱攻击的高效检测，在保持高恶意意图召回率的同时具备极低误报率和延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御策略在真实部署场景下存在根本性局限：缺乏可信用户元数据时无法追踪全局历史上下文，且基于生成模型的实时监控带来计算开销过大的问题。

Method: 提出TwinGate框架，采用非对称对比学习（ACL）将语义不同但意图匹配的恶意片段聚类到共享潜在空间，同时使用并行冻结编码器抑制良性主题重叠导致的误报。每个请求仅需单次轻量前向传播，可与目标模型的预填充阶段并行执行。

Result: 在涵盖8,600种恶意意图、362万条指令的大规模数据集上，TwinGate实现了高恶意意图召回率和极低误报率，对自适应攻击具有强鲁棒性，在吞吐量和延迟方面显著优于有状态和无状态基线方法。

Conclusion: TwinGate有效解决了分解越狱攻击防御中历史上下文追踪与计算开销的核心矛盾，为真实场景下的LLM安全部署提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Decompositional jailbreaks pose a critical threat to large language models (LLMs) by allowing adversaries to fragment a malicious objective into a sequence of individually benign queries that collectively reconstruct prohibited content. In real-world deployments, LLMs face a continuous, untraceable stream of fully anonymized and arbitrarily interleaved requests, infiltrated by covertly distributed adversarial queries. Under this rigorous threat model, state-of-the-art defensive strategies exhibit fundamental limitations. In the absence of trustworthy user metadata, they are incapable of tracking global historical contexts, while their deployment of generative models for real-time monitoring introduces computationally prohibitive overhead. To address this, we present TwinGate, a stateful dual-encoder defense framework. TwinGate employs Asymmetric Contrastive Learning (ACL) to cluster semantically disparate but intent-matched malicious fragments in a shared latent space, while a parallel frozen encoder suppresses false positives arising from benign topical overlap. Each request requires only a single lightweight forward pass, enabling the defense to execute in parallel with the target model's prefill phase at negligible latency overhead. To evaluate our approach and advance future research, we construct a comprehensive dataset of over 3.62 million instructions spanning 8,600 distinct malicious intents. Evaluated on this large-scale corpus under a strictly causal protocol, TwinGate achieves high malicious intent recall at a remarkably low false positive rate while remaining highly robust against adaptive attacks. Furthermore, our proposal substantially outperforms stateful and stateless baselines, delivering superior throughput and reduced latency.

</details>


### [22] [Latent Adversarial Detection: Adaptive Probing of LLM Activations for Multi-Turn Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28129)
*Prashant Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现多轮提示注入攻击在残差流中留下"对抗性躁动"信号（激活路径长度远超正常对话），通过五个轨迹特征将检测率从76.2%提升至93.8%，跨模型族复现但探针不跨架构，需三源训练和三轮级标签才能达到89.4%检测率（2.4%误报率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本级防御无法检测多轮提示注入中看似良性的单轮攻击，需要更鲁棒的检测信号。

Method: 提出"对抗性躁动"概念，提取残差流中五个标量轨迹特征，使用三源（合成、LMSYS、SafeDialBench）训练探针，并利用三轮级标签（良性/试探/对抗）进行检测。

Result: 检测率从76.2%提升至93.8%；跨24B-70B四个模型族复现；三源训练达89.4%检测率（2.4%误报率）；真实LMSYS数据检测率47-71%；二元标签导致50-59%误报率。

Conclusion: 对抗性躁动是可靠的激活级信号，实际部署需要多源训练和细粒度标签。

Abstract: Multi-turn prompt injection follows a known attack path -- trust-building, pivoting, escalation but text-level defenses miss covert attacks where individual turns appear benign. We show this attack path leaves an activation-level signature in the model's residual stream: each phase shift moves the activation, producing a total path length far exceeding benign conversations. We call this adversarial restlessness. Five scalar trajectory features capturing this signal lift conversation-level detection from 76.2% to 93.8% on synthetic held-out data. The signal replicates across four model families (24B-70B); probes are model-specific and do not transfer across architectures. Generalization is source-dependent: leave-one-source-out evaluation shows each of synthetic, LMSYS-Chat-1M, and SafeDialBench captures distinct attack distributions, with detection on real-world LMSYS reaching 47-71% when its distribution is represented in training. Combined three-source training achieves 89.4% detection at 2.4% false positive rate on a held-out mixed set. We further show that three-phase turn-level labels(benign/pivoting/adversarial) unique to our synthetic dataset are essential: binary conversation-level labels produce 50-59% false positives. These results establish adversarial restlessness as a reliable activation-level signal and characterize the data requirements for practical deployment.

</details>


### [23] [FlashRT: Towards Computationally and Memory Efficient Red-Teaming for Prompt Injection and Knowledge Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28157)
*Yanting Wang,Chenlong Yin,Ying Chen,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlashRT 是首个针对长上下文大语言模型（LLMs）中基于优化的提示注入和知识腐败攻击的效率提升框架，能够实现 2x-7x 的加速和 2x-4x 的 GPU 内存缩减，为系统性评估长上下文 LLM 安全风险提供了高效的红队工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 在现实应用中广泛部署，但面临提示注入和知识腐败等安全威胁。现有基于优化的红队方法能提供更严格的安全评估，但资源消耗巨大（计算和 GPU 内存），尤其针对长上下文场景，严重阻碍了学术界对 LLM 安全风险进行系统性评估以及防御策略的有效性验证。

Method: 提出 FlashRT 框架，通过优化计算和内存效率来加速基于优化的提示注入和知识腐败攻击。该框架可广泛应用于黑盒优化方法（如 TAP 和 AutoDAN）。

Result: 相比最先进的基线 nanoGCG，FlashRT 实现 2x-7x 的速度提升（如从一小时降至不到十分钟）和 2x-4x 的 GPU 内存消耗减少（如 32K 令牌上下文从 264.1 GB 降至 65.7 GB）。

Conclusion: FlashRT 可作为高效的红队工具，帮助学术界系统性评估长上下文 LLM 的安全风险，并验证防御策略的有效性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-context large language models (LLMs)-for example, Gemini-3.1-Pro and Qwen-3.5-are widely used to empower many real-world applications, such as retrieval-augmented generation, autonomous agents, and AI assistants. However, security remains a major concern for their widespread deployment, with threats such as prompt injection and knowledge corruption. To quantify the security risks faced by LLMs under these threats, the research community has developed heuristic-based and optimization-based red-teaming methods. Optimization-based methods generally produce stronger attacks than heuristic attacks and thus provide a more rigorous assessment of LLM security risks. However, they are often resource-intensive, requiring significant computation and GPU memory, especially for long context scenarios. The resource-intensive nature poses a major obstacle for the community (especially academic researchers) to systematically evaluate the security risks of long-context LLMs and assess the effectiveness of defense strategies at scale. In this work, we propose FlashRT, the first framework to improve the efficiency (in terms of both computation and memory) for optimization-based prompt injection and knowledge corruption attacks under long-context LLMs. Through extensive evaluations, we find that FlashRT consistently delivers a 2x-7x speedup (e.g., reducing runtime from one hour to less than ten minutes) and a 2x-4x reduction in GPU memory consumption (e.g., reducing from 264.1 GB to 65.7 GB GPU memory for a 32K token context) compared to state-of-the-art baseline nanoGCG. FlashRT can be broadly applied to black-box optimization methods, such as TAP and AutoDAN. We hope FlashRT can serve as a red-teaming tool to enable systematic evaluation of long-context LLM security. The code is available at: https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT

</details>
