# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [24] [Quantifying the Cost of Manual Navigation: A Comparison of Gesture-Based Magnification versus Direct Access Reading in Digital Layout-based Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27010)
*Sebastián Gallardo,Hui-Yin Wu,Dorian Mazauric,Pierre Kornprobst,Monica Di Meo,Stéphanie Baillif,Aurelie Calabrese*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过实验比较了数字文档中手势缩放导航与大字号直接阅读两种模式对报纸阅读的影响，发现大字号模式不仅在性能上提升18%阅读速度和30%目标定位速度，更重要的是恢复了被手势交互破坏的自然阅读策略，且降低了工作负荷、提高了用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化手动导航（捏合缩放）与直接结构访问在布局化数字文档中的行为与性能成本，探究视觉访问结构线索（标题作为入口点）受限时对新闻阅读的影响。

Method: 开展两组实验任务（朗读所有标题、定位目标文章），对比原始版本（手势缩放，行业标准）与大字号版本（直接访问阅读），收集性能指标（成功率、完成时间）、行为完整性（阅读路径分析）及感知工作负荷与偏好（NASA-TLX），采用线性混合效应模型进行分析。

Result: 大字号条件相比手势缩放条件：阅读速度提升18%，目标定位速度提升30%；恢复被手势交互破坏的自然阅读策略；读者报告更低工作负荷和更高偏好。

Conclusion: 开发自动生成大字号版本的方法至关重要，其中布局适配应补充字体缩放以支持可访问性和体验质量。

Abstract: Understanding how diverse audiences engage with structured media is critical to ensure a consistent quality of experience. In this context, we quantify the behavioral and performance cost of manual navigation (e.g., pinch and zoom) versus direct structural access in layout-based digital documents. We specifically investigate newspaper reading when visual access to structural cues (headlines as entry points) is constrained. Participants completed two tasks-reading all headlines aloud and locating target articles-under two conditions: (1) original edition with gesture-based magnification (pan and zoom), which is the industry standard for digital documents, and (2) large-print edition supporting direct-access reading. We collected performance measures (success ratio and completion time), behavioral integrity through reading path analysis, alongside perceived workload and preferences (NASA-TLX). Results from linear mixed-effects models show that the large-print condition yielded not only better performance than gesture-based magnification (18% improvement in reading speed, 30% improvement in speed to locate a target), but more importantly, restored the natural reading strategy that gesture-based magnification interaction disrupts. Readers also reported lower workload and higher preference. These findings highlight the importance of developing automated methods for generating large-print editions, where layout adaptation complements font scaling to support accessibility and quality of experience.

</details>


### [25] [Breaking Bad Financial Habits: How LLM Conversations Correct Financial Misconceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27022)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过三项预注册研究，发现设计得当的LLM能够持久纠正金融误解，但需要两个关键条件：纠正意图（仅讨论误解的LLM效果不优于自我反思，无引导的对话甚至会固化误解）和接受者接受度（低于受访者金融素养水平的LLM回应被认为可信度更低，纠正效果更差）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融误解（如恐慌性抛售、回避股票市场）难以纠正，传统金融素养干预受限于成本、覆盖范围以及知识-行为鸿沟，亟需可扩展、成本效益高的替代方案。

Method: 三项预注册研究，系统操纵LLM的纠正意图（有目的地纠正 vs. 仅讨论误解 vs. 无引导对话）和接受者接受度（LLM回应水平是否匹配参与者的金融素养），测量误解纠正的持久性。

Result: 1) 纠正意图是关键：仅提示LLM讨论误解产生的纠正效果不优于自我反思；无引导对话甚至会固化误解。2) 接受者接受度是关键：低于参与者金融素养水平的回应被判断为可信度更低，纠正效果显著减弱。

Conclusion: LLM可作为传统金融素养干预的可扩展替代方案，但必须同时考虑纠正意图和接受者接受度这两个关键设计因素，否则可能无效甚至适得其反。

Abstract: Financial misconceptions carry direct economic costs, from panic selling to equity market avoidance, yet they are notoriously resistant to correction. Traditional financial literacy interventions are constrained by cost, reach, and a persistent gap between knowledge and behavioral change. Across three pre-registered studies, we find that purposefully designed LLMs can durably correct financial misconceptions. Critically, two factors are necessary for this effect. First, corrective intent: LLMs prompted only to discuss a misconception produce corrections no better than unassisted self-reflection, and undirected LLM conversations can actively entrench misconceptions. Second, recipient receptivity: financial concepts are often foreign to the investors who misapply them, and LLM responses pitched below a participant's financial sophistication are judged as less credible and produce substantially weaker corrections. LLMs thus offer a scalable alternative to traditional financial literacy intervention, but only when designed with both factors in mind.

</details>


### [26] [What Influences Readers' and Writers' Perceived Necessity of AI Disclosure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27129)
*Jingchao Fang,Victoria Xiaohan Wen,Mina Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究发现，在AI辅助写作中，读者比作者更认为需要披露AI使用情况；当AI贡献不可替代、直接整合且作者未有意引导AI生成时，披露必要性更高；写作努力程度对披露必要性无显著影响；作者使用AI的意图在读者和作者间产生相反效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在AI辅助写作中，哪些因素影响人们对披露AI使用必要性的感知，从读者/作者视角、阅读/写作目的、以及AI使用过程（可替代性、努力程度、意图性、直接性）三个维度展开研究。

Method: 采用小场景实验（vignette study），招募727名被试，操纵视角（读者/作者）、目的、以及AI使用的程序性因素（可替代性、努力程度、意图性、直接性），测量被试对披露必要性的感知。

Result: 读者比作者认为披露更必要；AI贡献不可替代、直接整合、作者未有意引导AI时，披露必要性更高；作者的意图性对读者和作者的感知产生相反效果；写作努力程度无显著影响。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了读者和作者对AI使用披露的直觉判断差异，为反思现有AI使用透明度规定提供了独特视角，并为设计更符合用户感知的AI披露指南和工具提供了见解。

Abstract: The growing capability of artificial intelligence (AI) leads to its increasing adoption in writing, spurring discussions around whether writers should disclose their AI use in writing. What influences the perceived necessity of disclosure? We look into this question from three dimensions: perspective (reader or writer of the text), purpose (the goal of reading or writing), and procedural factors (how AI was used in the writing process in terms of replaceability, effortfulness, intentionality, and directness). In a vignette study (N = 727), we find that readers consider disclosure to be more necessary than writers, and disclosure is regarded as more necessary when AI's contribution in writing is irreplaceable, directly incorporated, and when the writer does not intentionally steer AI generation. To our surprise, the writers' intentionality of AI use produces contrasting effects on readers' and writers' perceived necessity of disclosure. Moreover, the effort of writing shows no significant effect on the perceived necessity. This study contributes to the conversation on transparent AI use by revealing readers' and writers' grassroots judgments, providing a unique angle to reflect on existing regulations, and offering insights into how AI disclosure guidance and tools could be designed to better align with readers' and writers' perceptions.

</details>


### [27] [Reading Speed, Image Quality Ratings, and Comfort Ratings in Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27203)
*Minjung Kim,Saeideh Ghahghaei Nezamabadi,Trisha Lian,Anand Singh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文构建了Read-AR数据集，包含超过11,000个阅读速度和近6,000个视觉质量与舒适度评分数据，涵盖80多种实验条件，用于基准测试不同AR头显架构的文本渲染与显示质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本的渲染与显示是增强现实（AR）的关键应用场景，但目前缺乏一个标准化的、可对比的数据集来评估不同AR头显架构在文本阅读方面的表现。

Method: 在统一且受控的实验设置下，收集了超过11,000个阅读速度数据和近6,000个视觉质量与舒适度评分，涵盖了80多种不同的实验条件，构建了Read-AR数据集。

Result: 成功构建了Read-AR数据集，提供了丰富的阅读速度和主观评价数据，可作为AR头显架构质量评估的参考基准。

Conclusion: Read-AR数据集通过大规模、多条件、受控实验的数据积累，为AR领域中文本显示质量的标准化基准测试提供了重要资源。

Abstract: The rendering and display of text is a key use-case for augmented reality (AR). Here, we present the Read-AR, a dataset of reading in AR, for which we collected over 11,000 reading speeds and almost 6000 visual quality and comfort ratings across over 80 different experiment conditions on the same experiment set-up. The consistent, controlled set-up enables the dataset to function as a reference for benchmarking the quality of different AR headset architectures.

</details>


### [28] [Upskilling with Generative AI: Practices and Challenges for Freelance Knowledge Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27231)
*Kashif Imteyaz,Isabel Lopez,Nakul Rajpal,Hunjun Shin,Saiph Savage*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 自由职业者越来越多地使用生成式AI进行学习，但受限于不一致性、缺乏上下文相关性和验证开销，AI并非其主要学习资源。研究发现学习从"成长"转向"生存"，并揭示了"隐形能力"这一结构性挑战，即通过AI获得的技能缺乏可信的市场验证方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由职业者缺乏传统员工所拥有的组织培训、指导和基础设施，生成式AI工具既在重塑技能需求，也提供了新的学习支持方式，但学界对自由职业者如何实际使用这些工具进行学习、遇到的挑战以及生成式AI如何与平台工作的不稳定性相互作用知之甚少。

Method: 混合方法研究，结合问卷调查和半结构化访谈，以自我导向学习理论为框架，研究对象为自由职业知识工作者。

Result: 自由职业者依赖生成式AI来结构化学习和支持探索性技能获取，但因其不一致性、缺乏上下文相关性和验证开销而不将其视为主要学习资源。学习动机从"成长"转向"生存"，出现"隐形能力"问题——通过AI获得的技能缺乏可信的市场信号和验证方式。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，为自由职业者提出面向生成式AI学习工具的设计建议。

Abstract: Freelance workers must continually acquire new skills to remain competitive in online labor markets, yet they lack the organizational training, mentorship, and infrastructure available to traditional employees. Generative AI-powered tools like ChatGPT are reshaping market skill demands while also offering new forms of on-demand learning support to meet those demands. Despite growing interest in AI-powered learning tools, little is known about how freelancers actually use these tools to learn, the challenges they encounter, and how generative AI for learning interacts with precarity and competition in platform-based work. We present a mixed-methods study combining a survey and semi-structured interviews with freelance knowledge workers. Grounded in self-directed learning theory, we examine how freelancers integrate generative AI tools into their learning practices. Our findings show that freelancers increasingly rely on generative AI to structure learning and support exploratory skill acquisition, but do not treat it as their primary learning resource due to inconsistency, lack of contextual relevance, and verification overhead. We identify a shift from learning as growth to learning as survival, where upskilling is oriented toward immediate market viability rather than long-term development. We also surface a structural challenge we term invisible competencies, in which workers acquire skills through generative AI tools but lack credible ways to signal or validate these skills in competitive freelance markets. Based on these insights, we offer design recommendations for generative AI-powered learning tools for freelancers.

</details>


### [29] [Evaluating Epistemic Guardrails in AI Reading Assistants: A Behavioral Audit of a Minimal Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27275)
*Matthew Christian Agustin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知护栏"概念，通过TextWalk原型和十轮提示协议，研究LLM阅读助手在解释性任务中的行为边界，发现关键风险不在于系统崩溃，而在于"中间地带"——系统虽保持理性但过度转移了解释性劳动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM阅读助手在需要解释而非简单检索的场景中日益普及，核心风险不仅是错误或不安全输出，而是"解释性位移"——意义建构工作从读者向系统的转移。现有研究缺乏对这一交互现象的系统评估方法。

Method: 使用TextWalk最小阅读支持原型，设计固定十轮提示协议，对12篇分析性文本进行测试。协议从基线阅读支持逐步升级至解释性探究、边界压力和显式捷径压力，将护栏视为交互中可观察的行为属性而非静态指令特征。

Result: 结果显示：基线稳定性强；解释性探究阶段出现可测量的应变；直接边界压力下部分恢复；后期压力下趋于稳定。最关键的弱点不是系统崩溃，而是处于支持与替代之间的"中间地带"——系统保持理性和教学性，但过多地将解释性劳动从读者转移到自身。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：评估对话式AI阅读助手中认知护栏的交互协议；其行为动力学的实证描述；以及阅读支持AI中解释边界功能的新兴模型。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) reading assistants are increasingly used in settings that require interpretation rather than simple retrieval. In these contexts, the central risk is not only error or unsafe output, but interpretive displacement: the transfer of meaning-making work from reader to system. This paper examines that problem through the concept of epistemic guardrails, defined here as constraints on how an artificial intelligence (AI) system participates in reading and interpretation. Using TextWalk, a minimal reading-support prototype designed as a co-reader rather than an answer-provider, the study applies a fixed ten-prompt protocol to twelve analytical texts spanning four categories of argumentative prose. The protocol escalates from baseline reading support to interpretive inquiry, boundary stress, and explicit shortcut pressure, enabling guardrails to be examined as behavioral properties observable in interaction rather than as static instruction features. Results show strong baseline stability, measurable strain during interpretive inquiry, partial recovery under direct boundary stress, and late-stage stabilization under escalation pressure. The most consequential weaknesses did not appear as overt collapse, but in a middle zone between support and substitution, where the system remained grounded and pedagogical while redistributing too much interpretive labor away from the reader. The paper contributes a protocol for evaluating epistemic guardrails as interactional phenomena in conversational AI reading assistants, an empirical account of their behavioral dynamics under pressure, and an emerging model of interpretive boundary function in reading-support AI.

</details>


### [30] [Beyond One-Size-Fits-All Exercises: Personalizing Computer Science Worksheets with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27433)
*Franco Ortiz,Runlong Ye,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出FACET系统，利用LLM为CS1课程生成个性化教学材料。对409名大一CS学生进行混合方法研究，发现LLM个性化材料使低知识水平学生的任务完成率从70-75%提升至>99%，且低知识低动机学生的正确率提升18.2%，表明基于学习者画像的LLM个性化主要作为保留支架，防止高风险学生放弃任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛应用于面向学生的教育工具，但探索其支持教师的研究较少。本研究旨在通过FACET系统为教师提供个性化教学材料生成工具，缩小CS1课程中的参与度差距。

Method: 采用混合方法研究，409名大一CS学生参与，聚焦正则表达式(RegEx)教学。基于Bloom认知分类学和自我决定理论将学生分为四种学习者画像，实验组接受LLM生成的个性化练习（在脚手架、教学明晰度和语气上自适应调整），对照组接受标准非自适应练习。

Result: 标准练习导致低知识水平学生约25-30%的任务未完成，而个性化材料使所有画像学生的完成率接近100%（>99%）。低知识低动机学生正确率提高18.2%。调查显示学生更重视结构性脚手架而非动机性语气，认为自适应任务与标准练习难度相当。

Conclusion: 基于学习者画像的LLM个性化主要作为保留支架，防止高风险学生的任务放弃，同时不降低任务的"理想难度"。面向教师的LLM系统可通过调整教学明晰度有效缩小CS1课程中的参与度差距。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely applied to student-facing educational tools, this work explores their use in supporting instructors by presenting a practical adaptation of the Framework for Adaptive Content using Educational Technology (FACET) system to generate personalized instructional materials for an Introduction to Computer Programming (CS1) course.
  We conducted a mixed-methods study with 409 first-year computer science (CS) students, focusing on regular expressions (RegEx). Students were assessed on their knowledge and motivation, classified into one of four learner profiles, and assigned either LLM-personalized (treatment) or standard non-adaptive (control) exercises. Personalized materials varied in scaffolding, instructional explicitness, and tone based on learner profiles grounded in Bloom's Taxonomy and Self-Determination Theory.
  Quantitative analysis reveals that standard exercises resulted in task incompletion among low-knowledge learners, with approximately 25-30% incompletion, whereas personalized materials sustained near-universal completion (>99%) across all profiles. While high-performing students experienced ceiling effects, Low Knowledge/Low Motivation students achieved significantly higher correctness (+18.2%) with personalized support. Survey data indicate that students prioritize structural scaffolding (logical sequence, difficulty pacing) over motivational tone and perceive the adaptive tasks as equally challenging as standard exercises.
  These findings suggest that learner-profile-driven LLM personalization primarily serves as a retention scaffold, preventing task abandonment among at-risk students without diminishing the task's "desirable difficulty". The results demonstrate that instructor-facing LLM systems can effectively close engagement gaps in CS1 by tailoring instructional explicitness to student needs.

</details>


### [31] [Why Learners Drift In and Out: Examining Intermittent Discontinuance in AI-Mediated Informal Digital English Learning (AI-IDLE) Using SEM and fsQCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27493)
*Yiran Du,Huimin He*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于认知-情感-意动框架，调查了AI辅助非正式英语学习中学习者的间歇性中断行为。通过对632名中国大学生的调查数据分析，发现感知智能、交互性和个性化通过享受间接减少中断行为，而感知无效、不可控和复杂性通过无聊间接增加中断行为。fsQCA结果识别出四条导致间歇性中断的配置路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助语言学习研究多关注采纳和持续使用，鲜有探讨学习者的中断行为。本研究旨在揭示AI-IDLE中间歇性中断的发生机制，扩展AI语言学习研究从采纳/持续使用到采纳后中断阶段。

Method: 采用问卷调查法收集632名具备AI-IDLE经验的中国大学生数据，结合结构方程模型（SEM）和模糊集定性比较分析（fsQCA）进行数据分析。

Result: SEM结果显示：感知智能、感知交互性和感知个性化通过享受间接降低中断行为；感知无效、感知不可控性和感知复杂性通过无聊间接增加中断行为。fsQCA识别出四条导致间歇性中断的配置路径，表明认知障碍和情感疏离的不同组合均可导致中断。

Conclusion: 研究将AI-IDLE研究从采纳与持续使用拓展至采纳后中断阶段，强调设计愉悦、个性化、可控且认知负荷适度的AI英语学习体验的必要性。

Abstract: This study examined intermittent discontinuance in AI-mediated informal digital learning of English (AI-IDLE) through the cognition-affect-conation framework. Survey data were collected from 632 Chinese university EFL learners with prior AI-IDLE experience and analysed using structural equation modelling and fuzzy-set qualitative comparative analysis. The SEM results showed that perceived intelligence, perceived interactivity, and perceived personalisation reduced AI-IDLE intermittent discontinuance indirectly through enjoyment, whereas perceived ineffectiveness, perceived uncontrollability, and perceived complexity increased discontinuance indirectly through boredom. The fsQCA results further identified four configurational pathways leading to intermittent discontinuance, indicating that learners' temporary withdrawal from AI-IDLE can result from different combinations of cognitive barriers and affective disengagement. These findings extend AI-IDLE research from adoption and continuance to post-adoption discontinuance and highlight the need to design AI-supported English learning experiences that are enjoyable, personalised, controllable, and cognitively manageable.

</details>


### [32] [From Elastic to Viscoelastic: An EEMD-Enhanced Pulse Transit Time Model for Robust Blood Pressure Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27500)
*Boyuan Gu,Yijin Yang,Shuaiqi Cheng,Xiaorong Ding*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理信息框架的无袖带血压估计方法，通过引入粘弹性补偿机制（利用EEMD分解PPG信号提取高频IMF定义“粘弹性速度度量”），在MIMIC-II数据库上实现了5.22/3.65 mmHg的RMSE和R>0.97的精度，显著优于传统弹性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Moens-Korteweg方程的PTT血压模型假设动脉壁完全弹性，忽略了血管固有粘弹性，导致在血流动力学快速波动时估计失效。

Method: 1) 使用Makima插值对原始PPG信号进行高保真重构；2) 采用鲁棒的相交切线法精确检测脉搏足点；3) 利用EEMD分解提取高频IMF分量，定义“粘弹性速度度量”以量化血管阻尼效应（η·έ）。

Result: 在MIMIC-II数据库364名受试者、28,525个心动周期上验证，SBP的RMSE为5.22 mmHg，DBP的RMSE为3.65 mmHg，Pearson相关系数R>0.97。

Conclusion: 融入粘弹性特征显著增强了模型对血管滞后的鲁棒性，实现了医疗级精度的无袖带血压估计。

Abstract: Cuffless blood pressure (BP) estimation based on Pulse Transit Time (PTT) has emerged as a promising solution for continuous health monitoring. However, conventional models relying on the Moens-Korteweg equation often fail during rapid hemodynamic fluctuations, as they assume arterial walls are purely elastic and neglect inherent viscoelasticity. To address this limitation, we propose a physics-informed framework introducing a viscoelastic compensation mechanism. First, raw photoplethysmogram (PPG) signals undergo high-fidelity reconstruction using Modified Akima (Makima) interpolation. Second, a robust Intersecting Tangent Method is applied for precise pulse foot localization. Crucially, we utilize Ensemble Empirical Mode Decomposition (EEMD) to isolate high-frequency Intrinsic Mode Functions (IMFs), defining a ``Viscoelastic Velocity Metric'' to quantify the vascular damping effect ($η\cdot \dotε$) typically ignored by elastic models. The framework was rigorously validated on a challenging subset of the MIMIC-II database (364 subjects, 28,525 cardiac cycles) characterized by a high prevalence of hypertension (23.4\%). Experimental results demonstrate medical-grade accuracy, yielding a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 5.22 mmHg for Systolic and 3.65 mmHg for Diastolic BP, with Pearson correlation coefficients ($R > 0.97$). These findings confirm that incorporating viscoelastic features significantly enhances robustness against vascular hysteresis.

</details>


### [33] [Examining discontinuance of AI-mediated informal digital learning of English (AI-IDLE) among university students: Evidence from SEM and fsQCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27506)
*Yiran Du,Huimin He*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于认知-情感-意动框架，通过746名中国大学生的问卷调查，采用SEM和fsQGA方法，探究了导致大学生放弃使用AI辅助非正式英语学习的因素。结果显示：不满意度（ dissatisfaction）和挫败感（frustration）正向预测放弃意图，其中挫败感影响更强；失调（disconfirmation）、感知复杂性和感知风险正向影响这两种情感反应；fsQGA揭示了导致高放弃意图的多种充分条件组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究大多关注AI辅助非正式语言学习的采用和持续使用，忽视了学习者的放弃行为。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探讨导致大学生放弃AI-IDLE（AI介导的非正式数字英语学习）的前因机制。

Method: 采用横截面问卷调查法，收集746名有AI工具使用经验的中国大学生数据。运用结构方程模型（SEM）检验变量间的线性关系，结合模糊集定性比较分析（fsQGA）识别导致高放弃意图的多种因果配置。

Result: SEM显示：不满意度和挫败感均正向预测放弃意图，且挫败感效应更强；失调、感知复杂性和感知风险正向影响不满意度和挫败感。fsQGA识别出多种足以导致高放弃意图的条件组合，表明放弃行为由因果复杂性和等效性（equifinality）塑造。

Conclusion: 研究将AI-IDLE研究从采用和参与拓展到采纳后脱离阶段，为减少学习者在AI支持的非正式英语学习中的不满意、挫败感、感知复杂性和风险提供了实践启示。

Abstract: This study examined university students' discontinuance intention towards AI-mediated informal digital learning of English (AI-IDLE). Drawing on the cognition-affect-conation framework, the study investigated how three cognitive factors, namely disconfirmation, perceived complexity, and perceived risk, influence two affective responses, namely dissatisfaction and frustration, and how these affective responses predict discontinuance intention. A cross-sectional survey was conducted with 746 Chinese university students who had experience using AI tools for informal English learning. Data were analysed using structural equation modelling (SEM) and fuzzy-set qualitative comparative analysis (fsQCA). The SEM results showed that dissatisfaction and frustration positively predicted discontinuance intention, with frustration showing the stronger effect. Disconfirmation, perceived complexity, and perceived risk also positively influenced dissatisfaction and frustration. The fsQCA results further identified multiple sufficient configurations leading to high AI-IDLE discontinuance intention, indicating that discontinuance is shaped by causal complexity and equifinality rather than by a single necessary condition. These findings extend AI-IDLE research from adoption and engagement to post-adoption disengagement and provide implications for reducing learners' dissatisfaction, frustration, perceived complexity, and risk in AI-supported informal English learning.

</details>


### [34] [I'm Fine, But My Voice Isn't: Cross-Modal Affective Dissonance Detection for Reflective Journaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27517)
*Sumin Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模态情感失调检测（CADD），用于识别数字日记中文本与语音之间的情感不一致性，包含Masking、Coping和Congruent三类。贡献包括：CADD-Journal数据集、DACM双编码器非对称注意力模型（macro-F1 0.711）、域差距量化分析，以及ReflectJournal iOS应用原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字日记存在“真实性鸿沟”：用户在将原始情感转化为文字时会不自觉地进行“净化”，即使在私密写作中也会掩盖真实情绪。现有研究缺乏对这种跨模态情感不一致性的形式化建模和检测方法。

Method: 形式化定义Cross-Modal Affective Dissonance Detection (CADD)三分类任务（Masking、Coping、Congruent），基于Gross情绪调节过程模型。构建CADD-Journal数据集（1,800样本，共享句子池设计以隔离声学信号与文本内容）。提出DACM双编码器模型，采用非对称跨模态注意力机制解决池化融合中的梯度退化问题。通过零样本评估量化TTS与真实语音之间的域差距。

Result: DACM模型在CADD任务上达到macro-F1 0.711。四步消融实验表明：非对称注意力是主导驱动因素（+0.242），而DIM仅在跨模态特征上有效（+0.033）。零样本评估显示TTS训练模型与真实语音之间存在显著域差距。

Conclusion: 本文形式化了数字日记中跨模态情感失调检测任务，提出了有效的建模方法与数据集，识别了TTS到真实语音的域差距问题，并为未来自然语料库构建提出了两个具体需求。ReflectJournal原型应用为自然数据收集提供了部署平台。

Abstract: Digital journaling creates an authenticity gap: users consciously translate raw emotions into text, often sanitizing narratives even in private writing. We formalize this as Cross-Modal Affective Dissonance Detection (CADD), a directional three-way classification distinguishing Masking (positive text, negative acoustics), Coping (negative text, positive acoustics), and Congruent utterances, grounded in Gross's process model of emotion regulation. We present three further contributions: (i) CADD-Journal, a 1,800-sample TTS dataset with a shared-sentence-pool design that provably isolates acoustic signal from textual content; (ii) DACM, a dual-encoder model with asymmetric cross-modal attention that re-solves a gradient degeneracy in pooled fusion, achieving macro-F1 0.711 - with a four-step ablation demonstrating that asymmetric attention is the dominant driver (+ 0.242) while the DIM is effective only on cross-modal features (+0.033); and (iii) a domain gap quantification: zero-shot evaluation across three naturalistic corpora reveals a substantial gap between TTS-trained models and real speech, and we identify two concrete requirements for future in-the-wild corpus construction. ReflectJournal, a proof-of-concept iOS application, operationalizes the framework and provides a deployment platform for naturalistic data collection.

</details>


### [35] [Knowledge Affordances for Hybrid Human-AI Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27539)
*Irene Celino*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"知识可供性"(Knowledge Affordance, KA)概念，用以系统化描述在混合人机信息生态中，智能体如何识别有意义的信息寻求机会。KA是声明性的、语义基础的知识源描述，阐明知识源能提供什么、适用于哪类问题以及具有哪些上下文属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前信息生态系统日益异构，人类和人工智智能体在面对"该向谁寻求知识及为什么"这一基本问题时缺乏系统性框架。作者受人类"察言观色"（read a room）直觉启发，希望弥合不同研究领域之间的鸿沟，为混合人机环境中的信息寻求提供统一的描述性概念。

Method: 概念性提案方法。作者将可供性理论、语义网服务、知识工程与查询以及互理解性等多个研究流联系起来，提出KA作为声明性、语义基础、关系性的描述框架，并勾勒了构建KA感知系统的未来研究方向。

Result: 提出KA概念的核心属性：(1) 声明性——对知识源能提供什么进行语义描述的说明；(2) 语义基础——形式化描述知识源可回答的问题类型；(3) 上下文属性——包含情境因素的描述；(4) 关系性——KA产生于智能体的任务、偏好和情境因素的交互。但本文尚未提供完整的框架实现或实证验证。

Conclusion: KA概念有望连接多个研究流，为构建更具透明度、适应性和共享理解的KA感知系统奠定基础。作者建议未来在此方向上进行更深入的研究探索。

Abstract: As information ecosystems grow more heterogeneous, both humans and artificial agents increasingly face a simple yet unresolved question: when seeking knowledge, whom should we ask, and why? Inspired by how people intuitively "read a room", this paper introduces the concept of knowledge affordance (KA) to systematize how agents identify meaningful opportunities for information seeking in hybrid human-AI environments.
  Rather than introducing a fully formed framework, we propose KAs as declarative, semantically grounded descriptions of what a knowledge source can offer, for which kinds of questions, and with which contextual properties. Additionally, we suggest that KAs are relational, possibly emerging from the interplay between the agent's task, preferences and situational factors.
  Our contribution is thus a conceptual proposal that connects different research streams, including affordances, semantic web services, knowledge engineering and querying, and mutual intelligibility. We sketch possible research directions to build KA-aware systems that navigate information spaces with greater transparency, adaptability and shared understanding.

</details>


### [36] [Users' Activity Logs: the Good, the Bad, the Misconception, and the Disastrous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27676)
*Eman Alashwali*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对30名沙特阿拉伯Google个人账户持有者的访谈数据二次分析，从用户视角揭示了活动日志的积极、消极、灾难性方面及用户误解，提供了平衡的用户感知图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究多聚焦于用户活动日志的负面方面（如用户对隐私控制缺乏意识、隐私担忧），缺乏对用户感知的全面平衡理解，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对30名沙特阿拉伯Google个人账户持有者的访谈数据进行二次分析，采用模板分析方法，从"好、坏、误解、灾难"四个主题视角进行编码与分析。

Result: 发现了新的主题和使用案例，呈现了用户对活动日志的平衡感知，包括积极价值、隐私担忧、常见误解以及潜在的灾难性后果。

Conclusion: 为服务提供商、隐私研究人员和用户提供了实用建议，为后续相关研究提供了有价值的参考来源。

Abstract: Most service providers, such as Google, save logs from data generated by users while using the service. Many service providers provide users with privacy controls to manage whether, how, and for how long the data is saved and used by the service provider. While most prior studies focused on the negative side of users' activity logs, such as users' lack of awareness about the logs' privacy controls and users' privacy concerns toward their data, this work aims to provide a balanced view of users' perceptions regarding activity logs by considering the positive, negative, and extremely negative (hence disastrous) sides, as well as the misconceptions of activity logs. In this work, we present a case study of Google's Activity controls by conducting a secondary analysis of interview data from 30 Google personal account holders in Saudi Arabia. Using template analysis, we analyzed the data from the lens of four main themes: the good, the bad, the misconception, and the disastrous aspects of users' activity logs from the users' perspective. Our findings uncover new themes and use cases, offering a balanced view of users' perceptions of activity logs, and provide a better understanding and a useful source for subsequent studies on related topics. We conclude with practical recommendations for service providers, privacy researchers and experts, and users alike.

</details>


### [37] [AgentEconomist: An End-to-end Agentic System Translating Economic Intuitions into Executable Computational Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27725)
*Jiaju Chen,Jinghua Piao,Xia Xu,Songwei Li,Tong Xia,Xiangnan He,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AgentEconomist是一个端到端交互系统，能将经济直觉转化为可执行的计算实验。系统基于超过13,000篇高质量论文的知识库，采用模块化多阶段架构（想法生成、实验设计、实验执行），并通过人机协作的迭代工作流，生成比通用大模型更具文献依据、新颖性和洞察力的研究想法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济学研究长期面临的挑战在于将直观洞见转化为可验证的研究，而非缺乏直觉本身。现有方法缺乏将抽象直觉系统性地转化为可执行计算实验的端到端工具。

Method: 构建了涵盖13,000+高质量学术论文的领域知识库，设计了三阶段模块化架构：Idea Development Stage（生成文献支撑的假设）、Experimental Design Stage（配置模拟器对齐的实验参数与协议）、Experimental Execution Stage（运行实验并返回结构化分析）。采用人机协作的迭代工作流。

Result: 通过人类专家评估和LLM作为评判者的实验，表明该系统生成的研究想法在文献依据、新颖性和洞察力方面均优于最先进的通用大语言模型。

Conclusion: AgentEconomist采用人机协作范式，使研究者能聚焦于高层次直觉，同时将翻译和计算执行等劳动密集型过程委托给智能体，有效弥合了经济学直觉与可验证研究之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: A long-standing challenge in economics lies not in the lack of intuition, but in the difficulty of translating intuitive insights into verifiable research. To address this challenge, we introduce AgentEconomist, an end-to-end interactive system designed to translate abstract intuitions into executable computational experiments. Grounded in a domain-specific knowledge base covering over 13,000 high-quality academic papers, the system employs a modular multi-stage architecture. Specifically, the Idea Development Stage generates literature-grounded hypotheses, the Experimental Design Stage configures simulator-aligned experimental parameters and protocols, and the Experimental Execution Stage runs experiments and returns structured analyses. Together, these stages form a human-in-the-loop, iterative workflow that translates economic intuitions into executable computational experiments. Through extensive experiments involving human expert evaluation and large language models (LLMs) as judges, we show that the system generates research ideas with stronger literature grounding and higher novelty and insight than state-of-the-art generic LLMs. Overall, AgentEconomist adopts a human-AI collaboration paradigm that enables researchers to focus on high-level intuitions, while delegating the labor-intensive processes of translation and computational execution to agents.

</details>


### [38] ["It depends on where AI is used": Players' attitude patterns and evaluative logics toward different AI applications in digital games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27812)
*Ting-Chen Hsu,Jiangxu Lin,Wenran Chen,Fei Qin,Zheyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于310份问卷的1856条开放式回答，研究发现玩家对游戏中八种AI应用（智能NPC、涌现叙事、动态平衡、推荐系统、审查治理、美术资源生成、协同创作、玩法进化）的接受取决于AI增强沉浸感/个性化/新颖性/效率（接受原因）还是威胁创造力/情感真实性/自主性/公平性/稳定性/作者权/问责制（拒绝原因），并归纳出六种评价逻辑：体验丰富、工具效率、系统可靠、能动控制、作者遵从、人类监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究玩家对游戏中不同AI应用场景的接受态度及其背后的评估逻辑，理解AI介入游戏的位置和方式如何影响玩家态度。

Method: 收集310份问卷的1856条有效开放式回答，进行主题分析，识别接受、拒绝和条件接受的原因，进而归纳评价逻辑。

Result: 玩家接受AI的原因包括增强沉浸感、个性化、新颖性、效率/便利性；拒绝的原因包括威胁创造力、情感真实性、自主性、公平性、系统稳定性、作者权、问责制；归纳出六种评价逻辑。

Conclusion: 玩家对游戏中AI的接受具有高度的上下文敏感性，AI的介入方式和场景决定了玩家是欢迎还是抵制。

Abstract: As AI becomes increasingly embedded in digital games, players' attitudes de-pend not only on whether AI is used, but also on where and how it intervenes in gameplay. This study examines players' evaluative patterns toward eight AI application contexts, including intelligent NPCs, emergent narrative, dynamic balancing, recommendation systems, review and governance, art asset generation, co-creation gameplay, and gameplay evolution. Based on 1,856 valid open-ended responses from 310 questionnaires, we conducted thematic analysis to identify reasons for acceptance, rejection, and conditional acceptance. Results show that players welcomed AI when it enhanced immersion, personalization, novelty, efficiency, or convenience, but resisted it when it threatened creativity, emotional authenticity, autonomy, fairness, system stability, authorship, or accountability. We further identify six evaluative logics: experiential enrichment, instrumental efficiency, system reliability, agency and control, authorship and compliance, and human oversight. These preliminary findings highlight the context-sensitive nature of AI acceptance in digital games.

</details>


### [39] [CoNewsReader: Supporting Comprehensive Understanding and Raising Critical Thoughts on Social Media News Through Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27905)
*Kangyu Yuan,Guanzheng Chen,Sizhe Liang,Hehai Lin,Qingyu Guo,Dingdong Liu,Xiaojuan Ma,Zhenhui Peng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoNewsReader，一种基于评论的交互式新闻批判性阅读工具，利用大语言模型帮助用户理解新闻主旨、筛选有用评论并生成批判性思考问题。用户实验表明，相比传统社交媒体阅读界面，CoNewsReader能提升新闻理解、批判性思维表现和阅读参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻批判性阅读（CNR）对普通用户具有挑战性，而新闻下方的评论可提供补充信息和多元批判性观点以辅助CNR，但目前缺乏如何利用评论支持CNR的系统研究。

Method: 通过文献分析和形成性研究（N=12）提取用户需求，开发基于大语言模型的评论驱动交互工具CoNewsReader，包含评论补充信息提取、有用评论筛选和基于评论的批判性问题生成三大功能，并通过组内实验（N=24）与基线社交媒体新闻阅读界面进行对比评估。

Result: 与基线界面相比，使用CoNewsReader的用户在新闻理解、批判性思考能力方面表现更优，阅读参与度也更高。

Conclusion: 基于评论的CNR工具可有效支持新闻批判性阅读，研究为利用用户生成内容和机器生成内容辅助阅读任务提供了设计启示。

Abstract: Critical news reading (CNR), which requires grasping the holistic ideas of and raising critical thoughts on the news, is beneficial yet challenging for general people who usually get information on daily social media. Comments under the news can aid CNR by providing complementary information and other readers' diverse and critical thoughts. However, it is under-investigated how to leverage these comments to support users in CNR. In this paper, we first derive user requirements for a comment-based CNR tool from literature and a formative study (N=12). Then, we develop CoNewsReader, a comment-based interactive CNR tool powered by a large language model. CoNewsReader supports users in grasping the news idea with complementary information from comments, filtering useful comments for CNR, and getting questions generated based on the comments to conduct critical thinking. Our within-subjects study with 24 university students indicates that compared to a baseline news reading interface in social media, participants with CoNewsReader have a more engaging CNR experience and perform better on comprehending the news and raising critical thoughts. We discuss design considerations for supporting reading tasks with user- and machine-generated content.

</details>


### [40] [Enhancing multimodal affect recognition in healthcare: the robustness of appraisal dimensions over labels within age groups and in cross-age generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27938)
*Hippolyte Fournier,Sina Alisamir,Safaa Azzakhnini,Isabella Zsoldos,Eléonore Trân,Gérard Bailly,Frédéric Elisei,Béatrice Bouchot,Brice Varini,Patrick Constant,Joan Fruitet,Franck Tarpin-Bernard,Solange Rossato,François Portet,Olivier Koenig,Hanna Chainay,Fabien Ringeval*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究比较了基于评价理论维度的多模态情感识别模型与基于分类标签的模型在不同年龄群体（老年人和年轻人）CCT情境下的表现，发现维度模型在准确性和跨年龄泛化能力上显著优于分类标签模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感识别在AI辅助医疗干预（如计算机化认知训练CCT）中仍面临重大挑战，尤其是面向老年人群体的多模态情感识别。现有研究缺乏对评价理论维度与分类标签在跨年龄群体中泛化能力的系统性比较。

Method: 在THERADIA-WoZ语料库基础上，扩展收集年轻成人数据集，构建跨年龄语料库。采用基于评价理论维度的多模态模型与基于分类标签的模型进行比较，通过语料库内、跨语料库和混合语料库三种评价范式进行训练和测试。

Result: 评价维度在所有条件下均优于分类标签，展现出更高的预测准确性和稳定性；分类标签在跨年龄语料库评估中性能降至随机水平，而评价维度仍保持高于随机水平的预测性能；混合训练未能提升泛化能力。

Conclusion: 评价维度在情感计算中具有理论和实践优势，尤其在跨年龄情感识别中表现出更强的鲁棒性；多模态融合和深度学习表示对情感建模至关重要；研究提供了面向时间连续情感预测的API工具。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into healthcare has advanced significantly, yet affect recognition remains a major challenge, particularly in AI-assisted interventions such as Computerized Cognitive Training (CCT). The THERADIA-WoZ corpus was developed to enable multimodal affect recognition in the context of AI-driven CCT, focusing on an older adult population. This study extends the corpus by introducing a dataset collected from young adults, allowing direct comparison of affect recognition models across age groups. Our objective was to assess whether multimodal models based on dimensions borrowed from appraisal theories outperform those based on categorical labels and to evaluate their generalisation power across age corpora. After comparing both corpora, models were trained and tested using within-corpus, cross-corpus, and mixed-corpus evaluation. Results revealed that appraisal dimensions consistently outperformed categorical labels across all conditions, demonstrating greater predictive accuracy and stability. Notably, categorical labels failed to generalise across age corpora, as performance dropped to chance levels in cross-corpus evaluation. In contrast, appraisal dimensions maintained predictive performance above chance, reinforcing their robustness for cross-age affect recognition. Furthermore, training on both corpora did not improve generalisation beyond within-corpus training. The findings support the theoretical and practical advantages of appraisal dimensions over categorical labels in affective computing. They also highlight the importance of multimodal fusion and deep learning representations for emotion modeling. To facilitate future research, we provide an API for researchers interested in time-continuous emotion prediction, offering valuable tools for behavioral sciences to enhance the measurement of emotional states in various experimental settings.

</details>


### [41] [MyoKin3X: A Myoelectric Framework for Full-Hand 3D Force Recording](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27949)
*Charlotte Rohleder,Raul Sîmpetru,Annika Wünsch,Alessandro Del Vecchio*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MyoKin3X是一个可定制的全手框架，能够同时进行最多五个手指的3D力测量，提供稳健的力重建、多轴交叉校准、自动坐标变换和多种反馈模式，校准验证表明其具有高稳定性、低串扰和高预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的手部力测量系统在手指覆盖范围、力测量维度和解剖适应性之间存在权衡，难以实现可靠的全手多方向力同步采集，多轴校准、手部尺寸调整和手指特异性力重建存在技术挑战。

Method: MyoKin3X集成了五个3D力传感器、可定制的解剖适应结构，以及用于同步肌电图和力采集的独立软件，支持原位交叉校准、单指和多指最大自主收缩记录、自动坐标变换，并提供四种反馈模式（1D斜坡、疲劳协议、2D任意目标斜坡和2D探索任务）。

Result: 校准验证显示轴特异性校准因子平均变异系数0.04%，50N下最大力误差±0.06N；平均串扰减少92.71%，多数轴对残余串扰低于0.02%；传感器R²>0.99。

Conclusion: MyoKin3X实现了标准化、可靠的全手力采集，支持灵活的协议控制和实时可视化，适用于手部运动控制、肌肉协同和人机接口的高保真研究。

Abstract: Simultaneous multi-directional force measurement across all five digits is essential for studying hand coordination, compensatory forces, and myoelectric control, yet existing systems trade off digit coverage, force dimensionality, and anatomical adaptability. Reliable full-hand acquisition remains challenging because multi-axis calibration, hand-size adjustment, and consistent digit-specific force reconstruction are technically demanding. We present MyoKin3X, a customizable full-hand framework for simultaneous 3D force measurement of up to five digits providing robust and validated force reconstruction. It combines an anatomically versatile structure with five integrated 3D force sensors and a standalone software for synchronized electromyography and force acquisition. MyoKin3X provides in-place cross-calibration of all five sensors, single- and multi-digit maximal voluntary contraction recording, and automated coordinate transformation to digit-specific coordinate systems for standardized analysis across subjects and tasks. Calibration validation demonstrates high stability of the axis-specific calibration factors, with a mean coefficient of variation of 0.04% and maximum force error of +- 0.06N at 50N. It also shows effective inter-axis decoupling (mean crosstalk reduction: 92.71%; residual crosstalk below 0.02% for most axis pairs) and high predictive accuracy (R2 > 0.99 across sensors). The software includes four feedback modes: 1D ramps, fatigue protocols, 2D arbitrary target ramps, and 2D exploratory tasks. MyoKin3X therefore enables standardized full-hand force acquisition with validated measurement reliability, flexible protocol control, and real-time visualization for high-fidelity studies of hand motor control, muscle synergies, and human-machine interfacing.

</details>


### [42] [When and How AI Should Assist Brainstorming for AI Impact Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27997)
*Jarod Govers,Sanja Šćepanović,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI如何在AI影响评估中支持团队头脑风暴，通过改进战略预见方法并设计AI干预措施，在15场工作坊（共82名参与者）中评估发现：AI能提升通用型AI用例（聊天机器人伴侣）的评估质量和头脑风暴感知，但对专业型用例（肾脏分配应用）效果不显著；最终提出AI辅助头脑风暴的设计指南：早期构思阶段AI应仅提供提示而非解决方案，仅在参与者陷入僵局或饱和时主动介入，在收敛阶段帮助结构化想法，利用专业知识优化想法，总体上AI应更多支持繁琐的流程任务而非团队珍视的创意生成本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助影响评估的工具未针对团队协作头脑风暴设计，也无法捕捉多样化团队中的不同观点，缺乏对AI如何支持团队头脑风暴的系统研究。

Method: 从战略预见中改编两种结构化方法并为它们共同设计AI干预措施，先在5场线下工作坊（28人）中进行迭代设计，再在10场线下工作坊（54人）中评估AI干预效果。

Result: AI提升了通用型AI用例（聊天机器人伴侣）的影响评估质量和头脑风暴感知，但对专业型用例（肾脏分配应用）无显著改善。

Conclusion: 提出AI辅助头脑风暴的设计指南：早期构思阶段仅提供提示而非解决方案，在参与者陷入僵局或饱和时主动介入，在收敛阶段帮助结构化想法，利用专业知识优化想法，AI应更多支持繁琐的流程任务而非团队珍视的创意生成。

Abstract: A key task in AI practice is to assess potential impacts to prevent harm. Current AI tools assisting AI impact assessment have not been designed or evaluated for collaborative team brainstorming, and they do not capture the range of views in diverse teams. We studied how AI can support team brainstorming during AI impact assessment and made three contributions. First, we adapted two structured methods from strategic foresight and co-designed AI interventions for them in five in-person workshops with 28 participants in total. Second, we evaluated the interventions in ten in-person workshops with 54 participants, finding that AI improved impact assessment quality and brainstorming perceptions for a general-purpose AI use (a chatbot companion) but not for a specialised one (a kidney allocation application). Third, our findings result in broader design guidance for AI assistance in brainstorming: AI should only offer hints and not solutions during early ideation, initiating interaction only when participants face fixation or saturation; it should facilitate structuring ideas during convergence; leverage expertise to refine ideas; and overall, it should serve more in support of tedious brainstorming process tasks, rather than ideation that teams value to do themselves.

</details>

### [43] [Fitting Horn DL Ontologies to ABox and Query Examples: A Tale of Simulation Quantifiers and Finite Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26976)
*Marvin Grosser,Carsten Lutz*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究在给定正负例（ABox和布尔查询）条件下，为Horn描述逻辑EL和ELI及其带底概念扩展构建本体的问题。对于原子查询（AQs），问题在PTime内可解；对于有根CQs和UCQs，EL上为Sigma_P^2-完全，ELI上为ExpTime-完全。添加底概念不改变复杂度。有趣的是，从ALC/ALCI转向EL/ELI反而引入了更多技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作研究了表达型DLs ALC和ALCI中基于正负例拟合本体的方法，但未充分探索Horn DLs（如EL和ELI）在该问题上的特性与复杂度边界。

Method: 基于仿真（simulations）刻画拟合本体存在性的条件，据此开发决策程序，并分析精确的计算复杂度（PTime、Sigma_P^2-完全、ExpTime-完全）。

Result: AQs在EL和ELI中均为PTime；有根CQs/UCQs在EL中为Sigma_P^2-完全，在ELI中为ExpTime-完全；底概念不影响复杂度。

Conclusion: 从ALC/ALCI转向EL/ELI并不会简化问题，反而带来额外的技术挑战，揭示了不同DL族在本体拟合问题上的复杂度层次差异。

Abstract: We study the problem of fitting a description logic (DL) ontology to a given set of positive and negative examples that take the form of an ABox and a Boolean query. While previous work has investigated this problem for the expressive DLs ALC and ALCI, we here focus on the Horn DLs EL and ELI, as well as their extensions with the bottom concept. As the query language, we consider atomic queries (AQs), conjunctive queries (rooted CQs), and unions thereof (rooted UCQs). We provide characterization of the existence of a fitting ontology based on simulations, use them to develop decision procedures, and clarify the exact computational complexity. For AQs, the problem is in PTime for both EL and ELI. For rooted CQs and UCQ, it is Sigma_P^2-complete for EL and ExpTime-complete for ELI. Adding the bottom concept does not change any of these complexities. Interestingly, moving from ALC and ALCI to EL and ELI introduces additional technical challenges rather than simplifying the matter.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Horty对现有可废止义务逻辑框架的质疑，解决早期方法在处理新冲突信息时无法撤销已推导义务的缺陷。

Method: 构建双偏好语义框架：采用理想性（ideality）和正常性（normality）两种世界排序，分别独立排名；在Hansson-Lewis偏好语义基础上嵌入非单调推理机制，实现义务的可废止性。

Result: 考虑了前提强化、包含和无淹没等非单调层公设，并与基于不同方法论的约束性输入/输出逻辑建立了理论关联。

Conclusion: 该双层偏好语义框架有效解决了可废止条件义务的建模问题，为道义逻辑中的非单调推理提供了新的理论基础，并与现有规范推理标准建立了联结。

Abstract: In response to a concern raised by Horty, this paper develops a two-tiered, preference-based semantic framework for modeling defeasible conditional obligations. The paper extends a Hansson-Lewis style preference semantics for dyadic deontic logic by incorporating a nonmonotonic reasoning mechanism that enables previously derived obligations to be withdrawn when new, potentially conflicting information comes in. The account is bi-preferential: two orderings--ideality and normality--on worlds are employed to address shortcomings in earlier approaches, with a separate ranking method for each. At the nonmonotonic layer, a number of postulates are considered, including antecedent strengthening, inclusion and no-drowning. A connection is established with so-called constrained input/output (I/O) logic--an existing standard for normative reasoning based on a different methodology.

</details>

*Wojciech Różowski,Robin Piedeleu,Alexandra Silva,Fabio Zanasi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文利用弦图（string diagrams）为Milner图表（一种具有可变输出的有限状态自动机模型）建立了行为距离的完备公理化系统，为加权转移系统等更具表达力的模型的定量分析奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为距离提供了一种定量比较过渡系统状态的方法，超越了传统的布尔等价概念。本文旨在为非确定性过程的行为距离建立完备的公理化系统，为更富表达力的模型（如加权转移系统）的定量分析奠定基础。

Method: 采用弦图（string diagrams）作为语法工具，利用其与Milner图表的图形结构紧密对应、支持归纳推理和组合语义、无需绑定子和替换机制等特点，对递归进行自然分解。

Result: 为基于Milner图表（一种通过引入可变输出来推广有限状态自动机的模型）的非确定性过程的行为距离建立了完备的公理化系统。

Conclusion: 该公理化研究为定量分析更具表达力的模型（如加权转移系统）奠定了基础，并推动了通过统一图式框架公理化自动机理论等价性的更广泛努力。

Abstract: Behavioural distances provide a quantitative approach to comparing the states of transition systems, moving beyond traditional Boolean notions of equivalence. In this paper, we develop a sound and complete axiomatisation of behavioural distance for nondeterministic processes using Milner's charts, a model that generalises finite-state automata by incorporating variable outputs. Charts provide a compelling setting for studying behavioural distances because they shift the focus from language equivalence to bisimilarity. Their axiomatic study lays the groundwork for quantitative analysis of more expressive models, such as weighted transition systems.
  To formalise this approach, we adopt string diagrams as our syntax of choice. String diagrams closely mirror the graphical structure of charts, while providing a rigorous formalism that supports inductive reasoning and compositional semantics. Unlike traditional algebraic syntaxes, which require additional mechanisms such as binders and substitution, string diagrams offer a variable-free representation where recursion naturally decomposes into simpler components. This makes them well-suited for reasoning about behavioural distances and aligns with broader efforts to axiomatise automata-theoretic equivalences through a unified diagrammatic framework.

</details>


### [46] [Order-invariant cluster first-order logic on graph classes of bounded degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27693)
*Fatemeh Ghasemi,Julien Grange*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“簇一阶逻辑”（cluster first-order logic）的新逻辑，用于研究序不变性。证明了虽然序不变的簇一阶逻辑在一般情况下可以定义超出纯一阶逻辑范围的性质，但在有界度类上，其表达能力包含于一阶逻辑之内。该结果通过显式构造线性序并利用保持相似性的局部到全局方法证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了研究序不变性（order invariance）——即公式中包含序关系但其真值与序的解释无关——并探索序不变公式的表达能力界限。

Method: 定义了“簇一阶逻辑”作为一阶逻辑的受限片段；通过显式构造线性序，使得相似的结构在扩展这些序后仍保持相似；采用保持相似性的局部到全局方法进行证明。

Result: 序不变的簇一阶逻辑在一般情况下可以表达超出纯一阶逻辑的性质；但在有界度类（classes of bounded degree）上，其表达能力包含于一阶逻辑之内。

Conclusion: 该工作可作为将此类技术应用于纯一阶逻辑以及解决序不变纯一阶逻辑表达能力问题的铺垫。

Abstract: We introduce a new logic, called \emph{cluster first-order logic}, a restricted fragment of first-order logic specifically designed to study order invariance. An order-invariant formula is one on a vocabulary that contains an order; however, whether a structure satisfies it or not is independent of the interpretation of the order.
  We show that while order-invariant cluster first-order logic can define properties outside the scope of plain first-order logic in general, its expressive power is included in that of first-order logic when it comes to classes of bounded degree.
  We establish this result by explicitly constructing linear orders such that similar structures remain similar when they are expanded with these orders. This similarity-preserving, local-to-global approach is technically involved and somewhat counterintuitive, since adding an order usually reveals distinctions that are otherwise hidden due to the locality of first-order logic. We believe that this work can be a stepping stone toward applying such techniques to plain first-order logic and toward settling the question of the expressive power of order-invariant plain first-order logic.

</details>


### [47] [A Logic of Inability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27917)
*Shanxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在经典 Coalition Logic 的基础上保守扩展了一个显式的“不能”算子（inability operator），将其定义为联盟能力的否定，并证明了该扩展系统的可靠性、完全性和保守性，系统分析了 inability 的模态行为特征，为多智能体系统中约束、负面能力与不可能性的推理提供了形式化基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Coalition Logic 主要关注联盟“能够”做什么，但对联盟“不能”做什么（即 inability）缺乏显式的形式化处理。在人工智能和安全关键的多智能体系统中，不仅需要指定智能体被指令或倾向不去做的事，更需要指定它们“无法”实现的事，这种不对称性需要被明确建模。

Method: 在标准 Coalition Logic 的基础上，通过定义 inability 算子（即 coalition ability 的否定），构建了一个保守扩展系统；证明了该系统的可靠性（soundness）、完全性（completeness）和对标准 Coalition Logic 的保守性（conservativity），并系统分析了 inability 的模态行为特征。

Result: 证明了 inability 算子的模态行为特征包括：关于联盟包含的反单调性、关于目标强度的逆变性质、与合取和析取的非对称交互、超加性失效、与对手能力不等价，以及大联盟 inability 与系统不可能性之间的联系。

Conclusion: 将 inability 作为可定义算子显式化，揭示了一个系统性的模态结构来刻画能动性的限制，为多智能体系统中关于约束、负面能力和不可能性的推理提供了形式化支持。

Abstract: Coalition Logic is primarily concerned with what coalitions can achieve, whereas what coalitions cannot achieve -- their \emph{inability} -- has received comparatively little explicit attention.
  This asymmetry matters in artificial intelligence and safety-critical multi-agent systems, where one often needs to specify not merely what agents are instructed or disposed not to do, but what they are \emph{unable} to bring about.
  We develop a conservative extension of Coalition Logic with an explicit inability operator, interpreted as the negation of coalition ability.
  This operator is introduced as a conservative and formally tractable starting point for studying inability as a modal concept in its own right.
  We prove soundness, completeness, and conservativity over standard Coalition Logic, and analyse the resulting modal behaviour: anti-monotonicity with respect to coalition inclusion, contravariance with respect to goal strength, asymmetric interaction with conjunction and disjunction, failure of superadditivity, non-equivalence with opponent ability, and the connection between grand-coalition inability and systemic impossibility.
  Making this definable operator explicit reveals a systematic modal structure governing the limits of agency, and supports reasoning about constraints, negative capabilities, and impossibility in multi-agent systems.

</details>

*Fabian Achammer,Stefan Hetzl,Renate A. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于饱和的SCAN算法，使其不仅能消除二阶量词，还能为二阶量词找到一阶公式的见证（witness），并提供了原型实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Second-order quantifier elimination is the problem of finding, given a formula with second-order quantifiers, a logically equivalent first-order formula. While such formulas are not computable in general, there are practical algorithms and subclasses with applications throughout computational logic. One of the most prominent algorithms for second-order quantifier elimination is the saturation-based SCAN algorithm. In this paper we show how the SCAN algorithm on clause sets can be extended to solve a more general problem: namely, finding a witness for the second-order quantifiers that results in a logically equivalent first-order formula. In addition, we provide a prototype implementation of the proposed method.

</details>


### [49] [On Higher-Order Probabilistic Verification via the Weighted Relational Model of Linear Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27986)
*Ugo Dal Lago,Guido Fiorillo,Paolo Pistone*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过线性逻辑的加权关系语义，将概率高阶递归程序（PHORS）的几乎必然终止问题转化为生成函数的计算，并证明了在带边界指数类型的程序中，生成函数总是代数的（即多项式方程的解），从而建立了该类程序几乎必然终止的可判定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率程序的几乎必然终止问题是不可判定的（实际上是Π⁰₂-完全的），因此需要寻找可判定（或半可判定）的程序子类。本文旨在扩展可判定程序类的边界。

Method: 利用线性逻辑的加权关系语义，将几乎必然终止问题转化为程序所解释的生成函数的计算问题。通过引入带边界指数的类型系统，扩展了Li等人的仿射PHORS类，并证明该类程序的生成函数总是代数的（即多项式方程的解）。

Result: 建立了带边界指数类型的PHORS程序几乎必然终止的可判定性，解决了该问题的有效判定方法。

Conclusion: 本文成功扩展了可判定的概率程序终止问题类，为概率高阶程序的验证提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: The problem of determining whether a probabilistic program terminates almost surely (i.e.~with probability one) is undecidable, and actually $Π^0_2$-complete. For this reason, a growing literature has explored classes of programs for which this and related problems can be shown (semi-)decidable. In this work we consider the termination problem for the language of Probabilistic Higher-Order Recursion Schemes (PHORS). Using the weighted relational semantics of linear logic, we translate this problem into the computation of suitable generating functions associated with the program interpreted. This way, we establish the decidability of almost sure termination for a class of programs that extends Li et al.'s affine PHORS via a type discipline with bounded exponentials. To achieve this, we show that the generating functions for such programs are always algebraic, that is, solutions of polynomial equations, yielding an effective method to answer the termination problem.

</details>


### [50] [Towards Neuro-symbolic Causal Rule Synthesis, Verification, and Evaluation Grounded in Legal and Safety Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28087)
*Zainab Rehan,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Sona Ghahremani,Holger Giese*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于元层的神经符号因果框架，通过结合大语言模型和形式化验证管道，从高层自然语言目标中自动合成并验证一阶逻辑规则，以缓解目标错配问题并支持可扩展的规则维护。在两个自动驾驶场景中验证了该方法能够生成最小必要且充分的规则集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的系统在安全关键领域存在可扩展性差、脆弱性高以及目标错配问题，AI系统易因优化狭隘目标而导致奖励破解和形式化验证失败。

Method: 在原有神经符号因果框架（集成一阶逻辑溯因树、结构因果模型、深度强化学习和MAPE-K循环）基础上，引入包含目标/规则合成器和规则验证引擎的元层。合成管道利用大语言模型将高层自然语言目标分解为候选原因、语义合并、转化为一阶规则候选、组合因果集合；验证管道执行语法模式验证、逻辑一致性分析、安全性及不变性检查。

Result: 在自动驾驶场景中的概念验证实验表明，给定人类指定的目标和原则，管道能够成功推导出最小必要且充分的规则集，并将其形式化为逻辑约束。

Conclusion: 该方法支持增量式、模块化、可追溯的规则合成，且基于既定法律和安全原则，有望缓解目标错配并提升规则系统的可维护性。

Abstract: Rule-based systems remain central in safety-critical domains but often struggle with scalability, brittleness, and goal misspecification. These limitations can lead to reward hacking and failures in formal verification, as AI systems tend to optimize for narrow objectives. In previous research, we developed a neuro-symbolic causal framework that integrates first-order logic abduction trees, structural causal models, and deep reinforcement learning within a MAPE-K loop to provide explainable adaptations under distribution shifts. In this paper, we extend that framework by introducing a meta-level layer designed to mitigate goal misspecification and support scalable rule maintenance. This layer consists of a Goal/Rule Synthesizer and a Rule Verification Engine, which iteratively refine a formal rule theory from high-level natural-language goals and principles provided by human experts. The synthesis pipeline employs large language models (LLMs) to: (1) decompose goals into candidate causes, (2) consolidate semantics to remove redundancies, (3) translate them into candidate first-order rules, and (4) compose necessary and sufficient causal sets. The verification pipeline then performs (1) syntax and schema validation, (2) logical consistency analysis, and (3) safety and invariant checks before integrating verified rules into the knowledge base. We evaluated our approach with a proof-of-concept implementation in two autonomous driving scenarios. Results indicate that, given human-specified goals and principles, the pipeline can successfully derive minimal necessary and sufficient rule sets and formalize them as logical constraints. These findings suggest that the pipeline supports incremental, modular, and traceable rule synthesis grounded in established legal and safety principles.

</details>


### [51] [Non-negative Rational Semantic Numeration Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28171)
*Alexander Chunikhin*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的语义计数系统——正有理语义计数系统，定义了基数语义算子中进位与余数的差异，阐述了其作为动力系统的性质，并初步尝试定义部分整数语义计数系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在扩展传统语义计数系统的范畴，将正有理数纳入语义计数框架，并探索部分整数语义计数系统的可能性。

Method: 通过引入正有理语义计数系统，定义基数语义算子中通用进位与余数的差异，结合动力系统理论与分析/数值示例进行验证。

Result: 成功构建了正有理语义计数系统的理论框架，通过分析和数值示例展示了其动力系统特性，并提出了部分整数语义计数系统的初步定义。

Conclusion: 正有理语义计数系统扩展了语义计数系统的理论边界，为后续研究部分整数语义计数系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: A new class of Semantic Numeration Systems, namely, positive rational Semantic Numeration Systems is introduced. For cardinal semantic operators, differences in the formation of carry (common carry) and remainders are defined. The properties of positive rational Semantic Numeration Systems as dynamical systems are formulated and illustrated through analytical and numerical examples. A first attempt at defining partial integer Semantic Numeration Systems is proposed.

</details>

### [52] [MAEO: Multiobjective Animorphic Ensemble Optimization for Scalable Large-scale Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26973)
*Omer F. Erdem,Dean Price,Paul Seurin,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出多目标拟态集成优化框架（MAEO），采用岛屿架构并行集成四种进化算法（NSGA-III、CTAEA、AGEMOEA2、SPEA2），通过无参数超体积指标评估岛屿性能、基于Pareto排序结合拥挤距离和纳底点邻近性的个体评分机制，在12个DTLZ/ZDT函数的36种维度设置下超越或匹敌领先算法，并成功应用于小型模块化核反应堆平衡循环优化（3目标、8离散变量、约40000次评估），降低了平准化电力成本和峰值硼浓度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化在收敛性、多样性和计算效率之间难以平衡，且No Free Lunch定理表明单一优化器存在固有局限性，亟需一种集成策略克服这些不足。

Method: 提出MAEO框架：岛屿架构并行集成NSGA-III、CTAEA、AGEMOEA2、SPEA2四种算法，采用无参数超体积指标评估岛屿性能，使用严格Pareto秩排序结合拥挤距离和纳底点邻近性的个体评分机制保持选择压力。

Result: 在12个DTLZ/ZDT函数的36种维度设置下，MAEO在超体积和反世代距离两个指标上通过Wilcoxon符号秩检验展现出平衡的收敛-多样性性能，优于或匹敌主流算法；应用于小型模块化核反应堆优化（3目标、8离散变量、约40000次评估），成功降低平准化电力成本和峰值硼浓度，同时满足安全约束。

Conclusion: MAEO框架通过多算法集成有效克服单一优化器的局限，在基准测试和实际工程问题中均表现出色，验证了岛屿架构集成策略在多目标优化中的可行性和实用性。

Abstract: Multiobjective optimization remains challenging for many scientific and engineering problems due to the need to balance convergence, diversity, and computational efficiency across high-dimensional objective landscapes. This work presents the Multiobjective Animorphic Ensemble Optimization (MAEO) framework, a parallelizable ensemble strategy that unifies state-of-the-art evolutionary algorithms within an island-based architecture, overcoming the limitations of relying on a single optimizer, as implied by the No Free Lunch theorem. MAEO uses a parameter-free hypervolume indicator for island performance assessment and a strict Pareto-rank-based individual scoring formulation that incorporates crowding distance and nadir-point proximity to ensure consistent selection pressure within each front. The framework is initiated using four algorithms (NSGA-III, CTAEA, AGEMOEA2, SPEA2) and evaluated through extensive benchmarking on 12 DTLZ/ZDT functions under 36 dimensionality settings using Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with both hypervolume and inverse generational distance metrics. Results show that MAEO achieves balanced convergence-diversity performance, outperforming or matching some of the leading multiobjective optimization algorithms across different benchmark problems. To demonstrate practical applicability, MAEO is applied to the equilibrium-cycle optimization of a small modular nuclear reactor. Eight discrete design variables (and three objectives (levelized cost of electricity, peak soluble boron concentration, fuel cycle length) are optimized under two safety constraints. The algorithm carried out roughly 40000 evaluations using computer simulations. MAEO identifies core designs that lower both the levelized cost of electricity and the peak boron concentration, while preserving fuel cycle length and meeting all safety constraints.

</details>

*Khoirul Faiq Muzakka,Sören Möller,Martin Finsterbusch*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出RCMAES，一种结合维度相关非线性种群规模缩减策略与自适应重启机制的CMA-ES变体，在CEC2017、CEC2020和CEC2022基准套件上取得竞争性且稳健的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升CMA-ES在CEC基准优化问题上的性能，通过引入新的种群规模缩减策略和自适应重启机制来克服现有方法的局限性。

Method: RCMAES在纯CMA-ES框架内集成了维度相关的非线性种群规模缩减策略和自适应重启机制。

Result: 在CEC2017、CEC2020和CEC2022三个基准套件上，RCMAES与state-of-the-art的DE算法及其近亲BIPOP-aCMAES相比，展现出竞争性和稳健的性能。

Conclusion: RCMAES是一种有效的CMA-ES变体，能够在多个CEC基准测试中实现稳健且具有竞争力的优化性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes RCMAES, a novel variant of the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES) for CEC benchmark optimization. RCMAES integrates a dimension-dependent nonlinear population-size reduction strategy with an adaptive restart mechanism within a pure CMA-ES framework. RCMAES is evaluated on three benchmark suites (CEC2017, CEC2020, and CEC2022) and compared with state-of-the-art DE algorithms as well as its closely related counterpart, BIPOP-aCMAES. Experimental results show that RCMAES achieves competitive and robust performance across all benchmarks.

</details>


### [54] [Attractor FCM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27947)
*Alexis Kafantaris*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度下降、物理约束的雅可比版吸引子模糊认知图（FCM），它使用残差记忆、时间反向传播和不动点锚定进行权重更新，并采用牛顿法结合自适应梯度下降的新学习算法，通过因果掩码融入物理先验知识，有效避免局部最优并高效降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FCM模型多基于Hebbian学习或Agentic方法，缺乏对系统不动点吸引子的精确求解能力和物理约束的显式建模，导致收敛不稳定或陷入局部最优。本文旨在构建一种物理约束、可精确求解吸引子的FCM框架。

Method: 1) 构建基于梯度下降的雅可比版FCM，非Hebbian/Agentic/混合方法；2) 引入残差记忆和时间反向传播（BPTT）进行递归权重更新，通过不动点锚定保证系统收敛；3) 采用牛顿法寻找系统不动点吸引子，配合自适应梯度下降动态调整损失景观，根据sigmoid饱和程度自适应调节权重；4) 使用因果掩码过滤更新，确保模型遵循初始专家知识和物理约束。

Result: 模型能够有效收敛到不动点吸引子，通过自适应梯度下降避免过早陷入局部最小值，因果掩码机制使模型尊重物理先验，训练误差高效降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的物理约束雅可比版吸引子FCM通过结合牛顿法、自适应梯度下降和因果掩码，实现了比传统FCM更稳定、准确的收敛性能，为物理感知的神经符号系统建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: In this paper an attractor FCM is created, tested, and analyzed. This FCM is neither a hebbian based nor agentic, nor a hybrid; it rather is a gradient descent based, physics constrained, Jacobian version of an FCM. Moreover, this model has several quirks; it uses residual memory, back propagation through time, and a fixed point anchor that is recursively implemented to update its weights. The residuals update the recursive part without losing the system memory. The model's anchor enables it to converge in a fixed point for which back propagation through time unrolls it and ensures that the error minimization is for an accurate gradient. Furthermore, a new learning algorithm is utilized. The Newton's method finds the system's fixed point attractor and then gradient descend is adaptively changing the landscape; an adaptive term is used to directly manipulate the weights through the attractor dynamics. As the adaptive term changes, the descent through the landscape is constantly adjusting according to sigmoid saturation, and that prevents premature convergence to a local minimum. Lastly, the updates are filtered by causal mask that informs the network about the physics, respecting the initial expert based opinions, for which model reduces the error to the target in an efficient way.

</details>

Method: 提出三层量子增强计算连续体架构：1) 物理层：共享光纤基础设施；2) 控制与编排层：用户管理；3) 应用层：自适应量子经典融合框架。采用从云耦合到低温逻辑的更紧系统集成策略。

Result: 通过更紧的系统集成（如低温逻辑）可减少能源浪费和热足迹，实现每解决一个问题的"绿色性能优势"。

Conclusion: 量子增强计算连续体通过异构集成和节能感知设计，有望在先进计算时代实现以能量效率为核心的绿色性能优势。

Abstract: We discuss a Quantum-Enhanced Computing Continuum, a heterogeneous, hybrid architecture that integrates quantum processing units (QPUs) within an Edge-Cloud-HPC fabric. Promote sustainability by shifting from performance to "energy-aware integration.' The architecture has three layers: a Physical Layer with shared fiber-optic infrastructure, a Control and Orchestration Layer managed by the user, and an Application Layer with an Adaptive Quantum Classical Fusion (AQCF) framework. Tighter system integration, like moving from cloud coupling to cryogenic logic, reduces energy waste and "thermal footprints.' The aim is a Green Performance Advantage: energy per problem solved in the era of Advanced Computing.

</details>
