  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学触觉传感器的仿真面临高可变形性和复杂光学特性的挑战，现有仿真器多采用简化模型，无法准确捕捉软体传感器的物理行为。

Method: (1) 使用物质点法(MPM)将软体传感器建模为弹性材料，精确模拟物理行为；(2) 提出基于残差学习的光学响应模拟方法，学习相对于真实世界空闲状态的残差图像；(3) 支持少量演示即可快速标定。

Result: (1) 准确复现DenseTact光学触觉传感器的物理动力学；(2) 在接触密集场景中生成逼真的光学输出；(3) 仿真训练的模型直接部署到真实世界，物体分类准确率85%，肿瘤检测准确率90%；(4) 仿真中训练的策略实现精确轨迹跟踪，平均误差<0.9mm。

Conclusion: DOT-Sim通过可微物理仿真和残差光学模拟，在物理和视觉真实性上显著优于现有基线，能够在几分钟内完成标定，支持大规模非线性形变，并成功实现零样本模拟到真实迁移。

Abstract: Simulating optical tactile sensors presents significant challenges due to their high deformability and intricate optical properties. To address these issues and enable a physically accurate simulation, we propose DOT-Sim: Differentiable Optical Tactile Simulation. Unlike prior simulators that rely on simplified models of deformable sensors, DOT-Sim accurately captures the physical behavior of soft sensors by modeling them as elastic materials using the Material Point Method (MPM). DOT-Sim enables rapid calibration of optical tactile sensor simulation using a small number of demonstrations within minutes, which is substantially faster than existing methods. Compared to current baselines, our approach supports much larger and non-linear deformations. To handle the optical aspect, we propose a novel approach to simulating optical responses by learning a residual image relative to the real-world idle state. We validate the physical and visual realism of our method through a series of zero-shot sim-to-real tasks. Our experiments show that DOT-Sim (1) accurately replicates the physical dynamics of a DenseTact optical tactile sensor in reality, (2) generates realistic optical outputs in contact-rich scenarios, (3) enables direct deployment of simulation-trained classifiers in the real world, achieving 85% classification accuracy on challenging objects and 90% accuracy in embedded tumor-type detection, and (4) allows precise trajectory following with a policy trained from demonstrations in simulation, with an average error of less than 0.9 mm.

</details>


### [92] [Flying by Inference: Active Inference World Models for Adaptive UAV Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27935)
*Kaleem Arshid,Ali Krayani,Lucio Marcenaro,David Martin Gomez,Carlo Regazzoni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专家引导的主动推理框架，用于自适应无人机蜂群轨迹规划。该方法将多无人机轨迹设计从重复组合优化问题转化为分层概率推理问题。离线阶段通过遗传算法规划器生成专家示范，并抽象为任务、航线和运动词典，用于学习概率世界模型。在线阶段，无人机蜂群通过后验信念和KL散度异常指标评估候选动作，实现任务分配、航线插入、运动适应和碰撞感知重规划。仿真和真实飞行数据验证了该方法在保持专家结构的同时，产生比改进Q-learning更平滑稳定的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多无人机蜂群轨迹规划中重复组合优化计算复杂度过高的问题，以及现有方法在动态环境中缺乏自适应能力的问题。

Method: 提出专家引导的主动推理框架，包括：(1) 离线阶段：GA-RF规划器生成专家示范，抽象为任务/航线/运动三级词典，学习概率世界模型；(2) 在线阶段：基于后验信念和KL散度异常指标进行任务分配、航线插入、运动适应和碰撞感知重规划；(3) 集成EKF和PF等贝叶斯状态估计器提高运动级轨迹修正能力。

Result: 仿真结果表明该框架保持专家级规划结构，产生比改进Q-learning更平滑稳定的行为。真实飞行数据验证了世界模型能在噪声和非平滑观测下修正符号预测。

Conclusion: 该框架有效将组合优化转化为分层概率推理，提高了无人机蜂群在动态环境中的自适应轨迹规划能力，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents an expert-guided active-inference-inspired framework for adaptive UAV swarm trajectory planning. The proposed method converts multi-UAV trajectory design from a repeated combinatorial optimization problem into a hierarchical probabilistic inference problem. In the offline phase, a genetic-algorithm planner with repulsive-force collision avoidance (GA--RF) generates expert demonstrations, which are abstracted into Mission, Route, and Motion dictionaries. These dictionaries are used to learn a probabilistic world model that captures how expert mission allocations induce route orders and how route orders induce motion-level behaviors. During online operation, the UAV swarm evaluates candidate actions by forming posterior beliefs over symbolic states and minimizing KL-divergence-based abnormality indicators with respect to expert-derived reference distributions. This enables mission allocation, route insertion, motion adaptation, and collision-aware replanning without rerunning the offline optimizer. Bayesian state estimators, including EKF and PF modules, are integrated at the motion level to improve trajectory correction under uncertainty. Simulation results show that the proposed framework preserves expert-like planning structure while producing smoother and more stable behavior than modified Q-learning. Additional validation using real-flight UAV trajectory data demonstrates that the learned world model can correct symbolic predictions under noisy and non-smooth observations, supporting its applicability to adaptive UAV swarm autonomy.

</details>


### [93] [RAY-TOLD: Ray-Based Latent Dynamics for Dense Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance with TDMPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27450)
*Seungho Han,Seokju Lee,Jeonguk Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RAY-TOLD，一种混合控制架构，将MPPI的短视物理滚动与强化学习的长期规划能力结合，通过激光雷达潜在动力学模型和策略混合采样，显著降低高密度动态人群中的碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集动态人群场景中，纯反应式规划方法（如MPPI）因预测视界有限而难以逃离局部最优，需要将长期规划能力与物理鲁棒性结合。

Method: 提出RAY-TOLD混合架构：(1) 基于激光雷达的潜在动力学模型将高维传感器数据编码为紧凑状态表示；(2) 学习终端值函数和策略先验；(3) 策略混合采样策略将强化学习策略生成的轨迹注入MPPI候选种群，引导规划器朝向目标同时保持运动学可行性。

Result: 在高密度动态障碍物的随机环境中，RAY-TOLD相比MPPI基线显著降低了碰撞率，验证了短视物理滚动与长期意图学习的混合策略有效提升了导航可靠性与安全性。

Conclusion: 将短视物理基础滚动与学习的长期意图相结合，能够显著提升自主导航在密集动态人群中的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Dense, dynamic crowds pose a persistent challenge for autonomous mobile robots. Purely reactive planning methods, such as Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control, often fail to escape local minima in complex scenarios due to their limited prediction horizon. To bridge this gap, we propose Ray-based Task-Oriented Latent Dynamics (RAY-TOLD), a hybrid control architecture that integrates obstacle information into latent dynamics and utilizes the robustness of physics-based MPPI with the long-horizon foresight of reinforcement learning. RAY-TOLD leverages a LiDAR-centric latent dynamics model to encode high-dimensional sensor data into a compact state representation, enabling the learning of a terminal value function and a policy prior. We introduce a policy mixture sampling strategy that augments the MPPI candidate population with trajectories derived from the learned policy, effectively guiding the planner towards the goal while maintaining kinematic feasibility. Extensive tests in a stochastic environment with high-density dynamic obstacles demonstrate that our method outperforms the MPPI baseline, reducing the collision rate. The results confirm that blending short-horizon physics-based rollouts with learned long-horizon intent significantly enhances navigation reliability and safety.

</details>


### [94] [Function-based Parametric Co-Design Optimization of Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27557)
*Mohammad Amin Mirzaee,Harsh Gupta,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的参数化框架，将手掌结构、手指运动学、指尖几何和精细表面曲率整合到单一设计空间中，用于机器人灵巧手的协同设计与抓取优化，并开源以支持快速迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手设计仍与任务驱动评估和控制脱节，缺乏系统优化；已有协同设计方法范围有限，仅优化少量设计参数。

Method: 构建了统一的参数化框架，包含手掌结构、手指运动学、指尖几何及通过参数化表面变形核引入的精细曲面特征，在仿真和真实动态场景中验证抓取稳定性任务上的设计优化。

Result: 框架能够生成可直接用于仿真和制造的手模型，并在抓取稳定性任务上进行了仿真和真实场景验证。

Conclusion: 该框架将开源，有望加速灵巧手协同设计优化、跨具身策略训练与控制方面的研究。

Abstract: Despite advances in dexterous hand manipulation, robotic hand design is still largely decoupled from task-driven evaluation and control, limiting systematic optimization. Existing robotic hand co-design approaches are often limited in scope, optimizing a small subset of design parameters. We introduce a comprehensive parametric framework for robotic hand generation that unifies palm structure, finger kinematics, fingertip geometry, and fine-scale surface curvatures within a single design space. Fine geometric features are introduced through parametric surface deformation kernels that directly influence contact interactions. We validate the framework on design optimization in grasp stability tasks in simulation and real-world dynamic scenarios. Our framework produces simulation- and fabrication-ready hand models and will be released as open-source to enable rapid design iteration for dexterous hand co-design optimization frameworks and cross-embodiment policy training and control research.

</details>


### [95] [Robot Learning from Human Videos: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27621)
*Junyi Ma,Erhang Zhang,Haoran Yang,Ditao Li,Chenyang Xu,Guangming Wang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了从人类视频数据中学习机器人操作技能的方法，涵盖人-机器人技能迁移和数据基础两大方面，提出了包含任务导向、观察导向和动作导向的分层分类法，并系统分析了不同数据配置与学习范式的耦合关系，最后总结了数据集发展规律、视频生成方案及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域面临数据扩展瓶颈，而人类活动视频数据丰富易得，利用这些视频源被动学习机器人技能有望实现可扩展的通用机器人系统。为此需要对该领域进行全面系统的综述以推动进展。

Method: 首先回顾机器人策略学习基础，然后描述融合人类视频的基本接口；提出分层分类法（任务导向、观察导向、动作导向三条路径）并结合不同数据配置与学习范式进行跨族系分析；最后调查人类视频数据集和视频生成方案，并统计数据集开发与使用趋势。

Result: 建立了系统化的机器人从人类视频学习技术分类体系，揭示了不同技能迁移路径与数据配置的耦合关系，提供了大规模数据集统计趋势。

Conclusion: 该领域仍面临诸多挑战和局限性，论文指明了未来研究方向，并公开了论文列表资源。

Abstract: A critical bottleneck hindering further advancement in embodied AI and robotics is the challenge of scaling robot data. To address this, the field of learning robot manipulation skills from human video data has attracted rapidly growing attention in recent years, driven by the abundance of human activity videos and advances in computer vision. This line of research promises to enable robots to acquire skills passively from the vast and readily available resource of human demonstrations, substantially favoring scalable learning for generalist robotic systems. Therefore, we present this survey to provide a comprehensive and up-to-date review of human-video-based learning techniques in robotics, focusing on both human-robot skill transfer and data foundations. We first review the policy learning foundations in robotics, and then describe the fundamental interfaces to incorporate human videos. Subsequently, we introduce a hierarchical taxonomy of transferring human videos to robot skills, covering task-, observation-, and action-oriented pathways, along with a cross-family analysis of their couplings with different data configurations and learning paradigms. In addition, we investigate the data foundations including widely-used human video datasets and video generation schemes, and provide large-scale statistical trends in dataset development and utilization. Ultimately, we emphasize the challenges and limitations intrinsic to this field, and delineate potential avenues for future research. The paper list of our survey is available at https://github.com/IRMVLab/awesome-robot-learning-from-human-videos.

</details>


### [96] [ExoActor: Exocentric Video Generation as Generalizable Interactive Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27711)
*Yanghao Zhou,Jingyu Ma,Yibo Peng,Zhenguo Sun,Yu Bai,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExoActor提出利用大规模视频生成模型的泛化能力，通过第三人称视频生成作为统一接口建模机器人、环境与物体间的交互行为，再将生成的视频通过人体运动估计和通用运动控制器转化为可执行的人形机器人行为序列，无需额外真实数据即可泛化到新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人控制中，对机器人、环境和任务相关物体之间流畅交互行为的建模，需要同时捕捉空间上下文、时间动态、机器人动作和任务意图，而传统监督方法难以有效应对这一挑战。

Method: 利用大规模视频生成模型的泛化能力，以第三人称视频生成作为统一接口隐式编码交互动态；通过任务指令和场景上下文合成执行过程视频，再经由人体运动估计和通用运动控制器将视频转化为可执行的人形机器人行为序列。

Result: 实现了端到端系统，验证了无需额外真实世界数据即可泛化到新场景的能力。

Conclusion: ExoActor提供了一种可扩展的交互丰富人形行为建模方法，为生成模型推动通用人形智能开辟了新途径，同时指出了当前实现的局限性和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Humanoid control systems have made significant progress in recent years, yet modeling fluent interaction-rich behavior between a robot, its surrounding environment, and task-relevant objects remains a fundamental challenge. This difficulty arises from the need to jointly capture spatial context, temporal dynamics, robot actions, and task intent at scale, which is a poor match to conventional supervision. We propose ExoActor, a novel framework that leverages the generalization capabilities of large-scale video generation models to address this problem. The key insight in ExoActor is to use third-person video generation as a unified interface for modeling interaction dynamics. Given a task instruction and scene context, ExoActor synthesizes plausible execution processes that implicitly encode coordinated interactions between robot, environment, and objects. Such video output is then transformed into executable humanoid behaviors through a pipeline that estimates human motion and executes it via a general motion controller, yielding a task-conditioned behavior sequence. To validate the proposed framework, we implement it as an end-to-end system and demonstrate its generalization to new scenarios without additional real-world data collection. Furthermore, we conclude by discussing limitations of the current implementation and outlining promising directions for future research, illustrating how ExoActor provides a scalable approach to modeling interaction-rich humanoid behaviors, potentially opening a new avenue for generative models to advance general-purpose humanoid intelligence.

</details>


### [97] [Connected Dependability Cage: Run-Time Function and Anomaly Monitoring for the Development and Operation of Safe Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27728)
*Iqra Aslam,Nour Habib,Abhishek Buragohain,Meng Zhang,Andreas Rausch,Vaibhav Tiwari,Mohamed Benchat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了连接式可信赖笼（Connected Dependability Cage）架构框架，通过功能监控器和异常监控器双重机制，实现AI感知系统的分层故障安全行为，支持优雅降级和最小风险机动策略，并通过实车测试验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶等级提升，AI感知系统在动态不可预测环境中面临复杂安全挑战，传统功能安全标准（如ISO 26262、SOTIF）难以应对未知场景和系统故障，亟需具备故障运行能力以确保系统在失效或退化条件下仍能安全运行。

Method: 提出Connected Dependability Cage架构，集成两种互补监控机制：功能监控器通过投票机制监督多个异构AI感知管线的一致性；异常监控器检测训练数据中未出现的未知/新颖物体以评估AI感知可靠性。安全标志触发时自动启动数据记录，支持系统迭代改进。

Result: 两种监控器均通过大量实车测试验证，证明了在真实应用场景中的实际有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该架构为AI感知系统提供了分层故障安全行为支持，在关键差异出现时实现优雅降级并过渡到最小风险机动策略，同时通过数据记录促进持续改进，为高级别自动驾驶的功能安全与SOTIF合规提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The advancement of automated vehicles introduces complex safety challenges, particularly in dynamic and unpredictable environments where AI-enabled perception systems must operate reliably. Ensuring compliance with safety standards such as ISO 26262 and ISO/PAS 21448 (SOTIF) is essential for addressing system malfunctions and mitigating unsafe behavior in unknown scenarios. However, as automation levels increase, vehicles must go beyond conventional functional safety by incorporating fail-operational capabilities that enable continued safe operation during system or component failures and the handling of unfamiliar or degraded operational conditions. To address these safety concerns, we propose the Connected Dependability Cage, an architectural framework designed to enable hierarchical fail-operational behavior in AI-enabled perception systems. This framework integrates two complementary monitoring mechanisms: a Function Monitor that oversees multiple heterogeneous AI-based perception pipelines and detects inconsistencies through a voting mechanism, and an Anomaly Monitor that evaluates the reliability of AI perception by detecting unknown or novel objects in scenes that may be excluded from the training dataset. In the presence of critical discrepancies, the system supports graceful degradation, ultimately enabling a transition to a minimal-risk maneuver strategy. Furthermore, whenever either monitor raises a safety flag, an automated data recording process is initiated to facilitate iterative system development and continuous improvement. Both monitors have been implemented and validated through extensive vehicle testing, demonstrating their practical effectiveness in real-world applications.

</details>


### [98] [MotuBrain: An Advanced World Action Model for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27792)
*MotuBrain Team,Chendong Xiang,Fan Bao,Haitian Liu,Hengkai Tan,Hongzhe Bi,James Li,Jiabao Liu,Jingrui Pang,Kiro Jing,Louis Liu,Mengchen Cai,Rongxu Cui,Ruowen Zhao,Runqing Wang,Shuhe Huang,Yao Feng,Yinze Rong,Zeyuan Wang,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotuBrain是一个统一的生成式多模态模型，采用UniDiffuser框架和三流Mixture-of-Transformers架构，联合建模视频和动作，支持策略学习、世界建模、视频生成、反动力学和联合视频-动作预测等多种推理模式，并在真实部署中实现50倍以上的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在语义泛化上表现良好，但缺乏对世界动力学的细粒度建模；虽然已有工作探索将视频生成模型作为世界模型基础，但仍需要统一的模型同时建模视觉动态和动作。

Method: 采用UniDiffuser统一扩散框架，设计三流Mixture-of-Transformers架构联合建模视频和动作，引入统一多视图表示、显式语言-动作耦合和高效推理栈。

Result: 支持多种推理模式（策略学习、世界建模、视频生成、反动力学、联合预测），能处理异构多模态数据（纯视频、跨本体机器人数据），实现50倍以上实时部署加速。

Conclusion: MotuBrain通过统一框架实现了视频与动作的联合建模，在多种推理任务上展现了能力，并大幅提升了实际部署效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong semantic generalization but often lack fine-grained modeling of world dynamics. Recent work explores video generation models as a foundation for world modeling, leading to unified World Action Models (WAMs) that jointly model visual dynamics and actions. We present MotuBrain, a unified multimodal generative model that jointly models video and action under a UniDiffuser formulation with a three-stream Mixture-of-Transformers architecture. A single model supports multiple inference modes, including policy learning, world modeling, video generation, inverse dynamics, and joint video-action prediction, while scaling to heterogeneous multimodal data such as video-only and cross-embodiment robot data. To improve real-world applicability, MotuBrain introduces a unified multiview representation, explicit language-action coupling, and an efficient inference stack, achieving over 50x speedup for real-time deployment.

</details>


### [99] [Learning-Based Hierarchical Scene Graph Matching for Robot Localization Leveraging Prior Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27821)
*Nimrod Millenium Ndulue,Jose Andres Millan-Romera,Matteo Giorgi,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端可微学习的场景图匹配方法，利用层次化语义边缘类型同时匹配从房间概念到墙面的多层级结构，在BIM辅助的机器人定位中实现零样本泛化，比组合基线方法快一个数量级且F1更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内自主机器人需要精确定位，场景图可从传感器数据和建筑信息模型（BIM）两种互补途径构建，匹配二者可校正SLAM漂移。然而现有组合方法在大规模场景下计算成本过高，而现有学习方法仅处理平面图匹配，忽略了双手段场景图中的多层次语义结构。

Method: 提出一种端到端可微的管道，为两种图增强语义驱动的边缘类型（编码层内和层间关系），显式利用层级结构实现从高层房间概念到低层墙面的联合匹配，仅在平面图上训练。

Result: 在真实LiDAR环境上，该方法在F1指标上超越组合基线方法，同时运行速度快一个数量级，展示了在BIM辅助机器人定位中可行的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 利用层次化语义信息进行图匹配能有效提升匹配精度和效率，且仅通过平面图训练即可泛化到真实传感器数据，为BIM辅助的机器人定位提供了可行的学习方案。

Abstract: Accurate localization is a fundamental requirement for autonomous robots operating in indoor environments. Scene graphs encode the spatial structure of an environment as a hierarchy of semantic entities and their relationships, and can be constructed both online from robot sensor data and offline from architectural priors such as Building Information Models (BIM). Matching these two complementary representations enables drift correction in SLAM by grounding robot observations against a known structural prior. However, establishing reliable node-to-node correspondences between them remains an open challenge: existing combinatorial methods are prohibitively expensive at scale, and prior learned approaches address only flat graph matching, ignoring the multi-level semantic structure present in both representations. Here we present a learned, end-to-end differentiable pipeline that augments both graphs with semantically motivated edge types encoding intra- and inter- level relationships, explicitly exploiting this hierarchy to enable simultaneous matching from high-level room concepts down to low-level wall surfaces. Trained exclusively on floor plans, the proposed method outperforms the combinatorial baseline in F1 on real LiDAR environments while running an order of magnitude faster, demonstrating viable zero-shot generalization for BIM-assisted robot localization.

</details>


### [100] [GSDrive: Reinforcing Driving Policies by Multi-mode Trajectory Probing with 3D Gaussian Splatting Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28111)
*Ziang Guo,Min Chen,Xuefeng Zhang,Yixiao Zhou,Zufeng Zhang,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GSDrive 利用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）构建可微分的物理仿真器，为端到端自动驾驶提供稠密奖励信号，通过流匹配轨迹预测器实现多模态轨迹探索，弥补了传统RL仅依赖稀疏事件奖励的缺陷，在闭环实验中优于现有基于仿真的RL驾驶方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶中，模仿学习与强化学习的结合受限于：1）标注成本高昂及时序数据质量退化；2）传统RL仅依赖碰撞等稀疏事件奖励，导致策略过早收敛到次优行为。

Method: 1) 基于3DGS构建可微分物理仿真器，提供稠密、可微分的奖励信号；2) 在3DGS仿真器内集成流匹配轨迹预测器，实现多模态轨迹探测（rollout候选轨迹以评估预期奖励）；3) 建立IL与RL间的双向知识交换，奖励函数基于物理仿真交互信号。

Result: 在重建的nuScenes数据集上的闭环实验中，GSDrive优于现有的基于仿真的RL驾驶方法。

Conclusion: GSDrive通过3DGS可微仿真器为RL提供即刻稠密反馈，解决了稀疏灾难事件奖励的局限，验证了物理仿真交互信号在端到端驾驶策略提升中的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving presents a promising approach for translating perceptual inputs directly into driving actions. However, prohibitive annotation costs and temporal data quality degradation hinder long-term real-world deployment. While combining imitation learning (IL) and reinforcement learning (RL) is a common strategy for policy improvement, conventional RL training relies on delayed, event-based rewards-policies learn only from catastrophic outcomes such as collisions, leading to premature convergence to suboptimal behaviors. To address these limitations, we introduce GSDrive, a framework that exploits 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for differentiable, physics-based reward shaping in E2E driving policy improvement. Our method incorporates a flow matching-based trajectory predictor within the 3DGS simulator, enabling multi-mode trajectory probing where candidate trajectories are rolled out to assess prospective rewards. This establishes a bidirectional knowledge exchange between IL and RL by grounding reward functions in physically simulated interaction signals, offering immediate dense feedback instead of sparse catastrophic events. Evaluated on the reconstructed nuScenes dataset, our method surpasses existing simulation-based RL driving approaches in closed-loop experiments. Code is available at https://github.com/ZionGo6/GSDrive.

</details>


### [101] [FreeOcc: Training-Free Embodied Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28115)
*Zeyu Jiang,Changqing Zhou,Xingxing Zuo,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreeOcc是一种无需训练的开词汇占用预测框架，通过SLAM、3D高斯映射、开放词汇语义关联和高斯到占用投影四层流水线，在无需3D标注、真值位姿和任何学习阶段的情况下，实现比自监督方法IoU和mIoU提升2倍以上的性能，并能零样本迁移到新环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的占用预测方法依赖大规模3D标注，且跨环境泛化能力差。为解决这一问题，作者提出无需任何训练和标注的FreeOcc框架。

Method: FreeOcc采用四层流水线：(1) SLAM骨干估计位姿和稀疏几何；(2) 几何一致的高斯更新构建稠密3D高斯地图；(3) 利用现成的视觉-语言模型关联开放词汇语义与高斯原语；(4) 概率高斯到占用投影产生稠密体素占用。整个框架无需训练、无需3D标注和真值位姿。

Result: 在EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet上，相比自监督方法IoU和mIoU提升2倍以上；在ReplicaOcc基准上零样本迁移，性能显著优于有监督和自监督基线。

Conclusion: FreeOcc证明了无需任何训练数据和标注即可实现高质量的开词汇占用预测，且具有良好的跨环境零样本泛化能力，为占用预测领域提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Existing learning-based occupancy prediction methods rely on large-scale 3D annotations and generalize poorly across environments. We present FreeOcc, a training-free framework for open-vocabulary occupancy prediction from monocular or RGB-D sequences. Unlike prior approaches that require voxel-level supervision and ground-truth camera poses, FreeOcc operates without 3D annotations, pose ground truth, or any learning stage. FreeOcc incrementally builds a globally consistent occupancy map via a four-layer pipeline: a SLAM backbone estimates poses and sparse geometry; a geometrically consistent Gaussian update constructs dense 3D Gaussian maps; open-vocabulary semantics from off-the-shelf vision-language models are associated with Gaussian primitives; and a probabilistic Gaussian-to-occupancy projection produces dense voxel occupancy. Despite being entirely training-free and pose-agnostic, FreeOcc achieves over $2\times$ improvements in IoU and mIoU on EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet compared to prior self-supervised methods. We further introduce ReplicaOcc, a benchmark for indoor open-vocabulary occupancy prediction, and show that FreeOcc transfers zero-shot to novel environments, substantially outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines. Project page: https://the-masses.github.io/freeocc-web/.

</details>


### [102] [Design and Characteristics of a Thin-Film ThermoMesh for the Efficient Embedded Sensing of a Spatio-Temporally Sparse Heat Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28148)
*Sajjad Boorghan Farahan,Ahmed Alajlouni,Jingzhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ThermoMesh，一种基于热电薄膜网格的无源热传感器，通过传导热成像检测空间-时间稀疏热源。该器件集成热电节点与线性/非线性层间电阻元件，实现传感与压缩同步。实验表明非线性NTC陶瓷层在973-1273K范围内将最小灵敏度提升约14500倍，VO₂层在298-373K范围内提升约24倍。VO₂方案在40dB信噪比下定位精度达98%，平均温度误差0.23K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统红外热成像在恶劣环境（如高温、熔滴检测）中受限，需要一种无源、能效高、可在恶劣条件下工作的热传感方案，实现稀疏热源的高效检测与定位。

Method: 设计热电薄膜网格传感器，集成热电节点与线性或非线性（NTC陶瓷、VO₂）层间电阻元件，实现同步传感与压缩。定义四项性能指标（范围、效率、灵敏度、精度），通过数值建模分析不同电阻层对灵敏度分布的影响，并用合成1-稀疏数据集验证性能。

Result: 线性电阻层使16×16网格的最小灵敏度提升约10倍；非线性NTC陶瓷层在200×200网格中最小灵敏度比线性设计高约14500倍；VO₂层提升约24倍。VO₂方案定位精度98%，平均温度误差0.23K，NET为0.07K；NTC方案无定位误差，平均温度误差1.83K，NET为1.49K。

Conclusion: ThermoMesh能实现高能效嵌入式热传感，适用于传统红外成像受限的场景，如恶劣环境中的熔滴检测或热点监测。

Abstract: This work presents ThermoMesh, a passive thin-film thermoelectric mesh sensor designed to detect and characterize spatio-temporally sparse heat sources through conduction-based thermal imaging. The device integrates thermoelectric junctions with linear or nonlinear interlayer resistive elements to perform simultaneous sensing and in-sensor compression. We focus on the single-event (1-sparse) operation and define four performance metrics: range, efficiency, sensitivity, and accuracy. Numerical modeling shows that a linear resistive interlayer flattens the sensitivity distribution and improves minimum sensitivity by approximately tenfold for a $16\times16$ mesh. Nonlinear temperature-dependent interlayers further enhance minimum sensitivity at scale: a ceramic negative-temperature-coefficient (NTC) layer over 973--1273~K yields a $\sim14{,}500\times$ higher minimum sensitivity than the linear design at a $200\times200$ mesh, while a VO$_2$ interlayer modeled across its metal--insulator transition (MIT) over 298--373~K yields a $\sim24\times$ improvement. Using synthetic 1-sparse datasets with white boundary-channel noise at a signal-to-noise ratio of 40~dB, the VO$_2$ case achieved $98\%$ localization accuracy, a mean absolute temperature error of $0.23$~K, and a noise-equivalent temperature (NET) of $0.07$~K. For the ceramic-NTC case no localization errors were observed under the tested conditions, with a mean absolute temperature error of $1.83$~K and a NET of $1.49$~K. These results indicate that ThermoMesh could enable energy-efficient embedded thermal sensing in scenarios where conventional infrared imaging is limited, such as molten-droplet detection or hot-spot monitoring in harsh environments.

</details>


### [103] [FlexiTac: A Low-Cost, Open-Source, Scalable Tactile Sensing Solution for Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28156)
*Binghao Huang,Yunzhu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlexiTac是一种低成本、开源、可扩展的压阻式触觉传感解决方案，由柔性传感垫和紧凑型多通道读取板组成，支持实时控制和大规模数据采集，适用于多种机器人末端执行器，并兼容现代触觉学习流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感器成本高、制造重复性差、难以集成到不同机器人平台，且缺乏对现代触觉学习流程的良好支持。

Method: 采用三层层压结构（FPC-Velostat-FPC），将电极图案直接集成到柔性印刷电路中；使用低成本、广泛可得的元件设计紧凑型多通道读取板，通过串口以100Hz流式传输触觉信号。

Result: 实现了高制造效率和重复性，兼容刚性和软体夹爪，支持多种配置（指尖垫、触觉垫），并支持3D视觉-触觉融合、跨本体技能迁移及真实-仿真-真实的微调等触觉学习流程。

Conclusion: FlexiTac为机器人触觉传感提供了一种实用、可扩展且低成本的解决方案，有效连接了触觉硬件与数据驱动的触觉学习算法。

Abstract: We present FlexiTac, a low-cost, open-source, and scalable piezoresistive tactile sensing solution designed for robotic end-effectors. FlexiTac is a practical "plug-in" module consisting of (i) thin, flexible tactile sensor pads that provide dense tactile signals and (ii) a compact multi-channel readout board that streams synchronized measurements for real-time control and large-scale data collection. FlexiTac pads adopt a sealed three-layer laminate stack (FPC-Velostat-FPC) with electrode patterns directly integrated into flexible printed circuits, substantially improving fabrication throughput and repeatability while maintaining mechanical compliance for deployment on both rigid and soft grippers. The readout electronics use widely available, low-cost components and stream tactile signals to a host computer at 100 Hz via serial communication. Across multiple configurations, including fingertip pads and larger tactile mats, FlexiTac can be mounted on diverse platforms without major mechanical redesign. We further show that FlexiTac supports modern tactile learning pipelines, including 3D visuo-tactile fusion for contact-aware decision making, cross-embodiment skill transfer, and real-to-sim-to-real fine-tuning with GPU-parallel tactile simulation. Our project page is available at https://flexitac.github.io/.

</details>


### [104] [RopeDreamer: A Kinematic Recurrent State Space Model for Dynamics of Flexible Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28161)
*Tim Missal,Lucas Domingues,Berk Guler,Simon Manschitz,Jan Peters,Paula Dornhofer Paro Costa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合循环状态空间模型与四元数运动链表示的潜在动力学框架，用于可变形线性物体的鲁棒长期状态预测，在自交接触场景下相比基线方法实现了40.52%的预测误差降低和31.17%的推理时间减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体的机器人操作面临高维非线性动力学与拓扑完整性维持的挑战，现有数据驱动方法（如RNN、GNN）在自交和非物理变形（缠绕、链接拉伸）场景下表现不佳。

Method: 提出潜在动力学框架：1）将DLO编码为相对旋转的四元数序列而非独立笛卡尔位置，从几何上约束模型至保持链接长度恒定的物理有效流形；2）结合循环状态空间模型进行状态推断；3）引入双解码器架构，将状态重构与未来状态预测解耦，迫使潜在空间捕捉底层物理变形。

Result: 在包含自交的复杂拾放轨迹大规模模拟数据集上，50步开环预测误差降低40.52%，推理时间减少31.17%，在多交叉场景中保持优越的拓扑一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为长时域操作规划的合成基元，证明了其在大变形和自交场景下的有效性。

Abstract: The robotic manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is a fundamental challenge due to the high-dimensional, non-linear dynamics of flexible structures and the complexity of maintaining topological integrity during contact-rich tasks. While recent data-driven methods have utilized Recurrent and Graph Neural Networks for dynamics modeling, they often struggle with self-intersections and non-physical deformations, such as tangling and link stretching. In this paper, we propose a latent dynamics framework that combines a Recurrent State Space Model with a Quaternionic Kinematic Chain representation to enable robust, long-term forecasting of DLO states. By encoding the DLO as a sequence of relative rotations (quaternions) rather than independent Cartesian positions, we inherently constrain the model to a physically valid manifold that preserves link-length constancy. Furthermore, we introduce a dual-decoder architecture that decouples state reconstruction from future-state prediction, forcing the latent space to capture the underlying physics of deformation. We evaluate our approach on a large-scale simulated dataset of complex pick-and-place trajectories involving self-intersections. Our results demonstrate that the proposed model achieves a 40.52% reduction in open-loop prediction error over 50-step horizons compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, while reducing inference time by 31.17%. Our model further maintains superior topological consistency in scenarios with multiple crossings, proving its efficacy as a compositional primitive for long-horizon manipulation planning.

</details>


### [105] [LaST-R1: Reinforcing Action via Adaptive Physical Latent Reasoning for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28192)
*Hao Chen,Jiaming Liu,Zhonghao Yan,Nuowei Han,Renrui Zhang,Chenyang Gu,Jialin Gao,Ziyu Guo,Siyuan Qian,Yinxi Wang,Peng Jia,Chi-Wing Fu,Shanghang Zhang,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LaST-R1框架，将潜在思维链推理与强化学习后训练相结合，通过LAPO算法联合优化推理与动作生成，在LIBERO基准上达到99.8%成功率，真实部署中相比初始策略提升44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型要么使用显式语言推理（存在延迟和离散化问题），要么使用连续潜在推理，但都局限于静态模仿学习，限制了适应性和泛化能力。虽然RL已被引入VLA，但现有方法仅优化动作空间，忽略了底层的物理推理过程。

Method: 提出LaST-R1统一框架：(1) LAPO算法，联合优化潜在推理过程与动作生成，桥接推理与控制；(2) 自适应潜在CoT机制，允许策略根据环境复杂度动态调整推理深度。

Result: LIBERO基准上99.8%平均成功率（仅需一次监督预热），显著优于现有方法；真实部署中LAPO后训练带来最高44%的性能提升（涵盖单臂和双臂设置）；在仿真和真实环境中均展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将潜在推理与强化学习结合，LaST-R1有效提升了VLA模型的物理世界建模表示能力和交互环境鲁棒性，实现了高效、可泛化的机器人操作。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have increasingly incorporated reasoning mechanisms for complex robotic manipulation. However, existing approaches share a critical limitation: whether employing explicit linguistic reasoning that suffers from latency and discretization, or utilizing more expressive continuous latent reasoning, they are predominantly confined to static imitation learning that limits adaptability and generalization. While online reinforcement learning (RL) has been introduced to VLAs to enable trial-and-error exploration, current methods exclusively optimize the vanilla action space, bypassing the underlying physical reasoning process. In this paper, we present \textbf{LaST-R1}, a unified VLA framework that integrates latent Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning over physical dynamics prior to action execution, along with a tailored RL post-training paradigm. Specifically, we propose \textbf{Latent-to-Action Policy Optimization (LAPO)}, a novel RL algorithm that jointly optimizes the latent reasoning process and the action generation. By bridging reasoning and control, LAPO improves the representation of physical world modeling and enhances robustness in interactive environments. Furthermore, an \textbf{adaptive latent CoT mechanism} is introduced to allow the policy to dynamically adjust its reasoning horizon based on environment complexity. Extensive experiments show that LaST-R1 achieves a near-perfect 99.8\% average success rate on the LIBERO benchmark with only one-shot supervised warm-up, significantly improving convergence speed and performance over prior state-of-the-art methods. In real-world deployments, LAPO post-training yields up to a 44\% improvement over the initial warm-up policy across four complex tasks, including both single-arm and dual-arm settings. Finally, LaST-R1 demonstrates strong generalization across simulated and real-world environments.

</details>


### [106] [OmniRobotHome: A Multi-Camera Platform for Real-Time Multiadic Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28197)
*Junyoung Lee,Sookwan Han,Jeonghwan Kim,Inhee Lee,Mingi Choi,Jisoo Kim,Wonjung Woo,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniRobotHome是首个支持多主体协作研究的室内级平台，通过48个同步RGB相机实现实时、抗遮挡的3D人体与物体感知，并协调多机器人操作，使多人在多机器人共享空间中的紧密协作研究变得实验可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要局限于双人或顺序协作场景，而真实家庭环境需要多主体协作，即多个人与多个机器人在共享空间中并行执行交错子任务，存在紧密的空间与时间耦合。这一领域未得到充分探索，因为人与机器人、物体之间的近距离交互会产生持续遮挡和快速状态变化，使可靠的实时3D跟踪成为核心瓶颈，且缺乏相应的实验平台。

Method: 在一个真实家庭环境中部署48个硬件同步的RGB相机，实现无标记、抗遮挡的多人体与物体实时3D跟踪；将这些感知数据与两台Franka机械臂的操作在共享世界坐标系中时间对齐，形成统一的感知-驱动闭环。

Result: 该平台使多主体协作实验变得可行，重点验证了两个核心问题：共享人-机环境中的安全性，以及具有人类预期能力的机器人辅助。实验表明实时感知和累积行为记忆在这两个任务上均带来了可衡量的性能提升。

Conclusion: OmniRobotHome通过统一的大范围实时3D感知与协调多机器人驱动，突破了多主体协作研究的实验瓶颈，证明了实时感知与长期行为记忆在人机安全协作和预期性辅助中的关键价值。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration has been studied primarily in dyadic or sequential settings. However, real homes require multiadic collaboration, where multiple humans and robots share a workspace, acting concurrently on interleaved subtasks with tight spatial and temporal coupling. This regime remains underexplored because close-proximity interaction between humans, robots, and objects creates persistent occlusion and rapid state changes, making reliable real-time 3D tracking the central bottleneck. No existing platform provides the real-time, occlusion-robust, room-scale perception needed to make this regime experimentally tractable. We present OmniRobotHome, the first room-scale residential platform that unifies wide-area real-time 3D human and object perception with coordinated multi-robot actuation in a shared world frame. The system instruments a natural home environment with 48 hardware-synchronized RGB cameras for markerless, occlusion-robust tracking of multiple humans and objects, temporally aligned with two Franka arms that act on live scene state. Continuous capture within this consistent frame further supports long-horizon human behavior modeling from accumulated trajectories. The platform makes the multiadic collaboration regime experimentally tractable. We focus on two central problems: safety in shared human-robot environments and human-anticipatory robotic assistance, and show that real-time perception and accumulated behavior memory each yield measurable gains in both.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [A High-Throughput Compute-Efficient POMDP Hide-And-Seek-Engine (HASE) for Multi-Agent Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27162)
*Timothy Flavin,Sandip Sen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种原生C++实现的Dec-POMDP引擎Hide-And-Seek-Engine，通过数据导向设计、缓存行对齐、零拷贝PyTorch内存桥等技术，在单智能体1024环境中实现3300万步/秒的吞吐量，比基线NumPy实现快约3500倍，并能在几分钟内完成PPO、DQN、SAC等多智能体策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL算法在Dec-POMDP场景下样本效率低，现有并行框架（如SampleFactory、EnvPool）虽加速了环境执行，但主要针对经典MDP环境。作者旨在优化人机联合决策的决策层，需要一种计算高效的Dec-POMDP专用引擎。

Method: 原生C++架构的Dec-POMDP引擎，采用数据导向设计（DOD）原则、显式64字节缓存行对齐消除伪共享、使用锁页内存和DMA实现零拷贝PyTorch内存桥。

Result: 单智能体1024环境中达到3300万步/秒吞吐量；10智能体时降至700万步/秒（随机动作生成占1/3运行时间）；比基线单线程向量化NumPy实现快约3500倍；支持PPO、DQN、SAC算法训练。

Conclusion: 该引擎在Dec-POMDP环境下实现了极高的计算效率和吞吐量，验证了其在多智能体协同策略训练中的性能和通用性，可在数分钟内完成训练。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms exhibit high sample complexity, particularly when applied to Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (Dec-POMDPs). As a response, projects such as SampleFactory, EnvPool, Brax, and IsaacLab migrate parallel execution of classic environments such as MuJoCo and Atari into C++ thread pools or the GPU to decrease the computational cost of environment steps. We are interested in optimizing the decision-level of human-AI joint operations, so we introduce a compute-efficient Dec-POMDP engine natively architected in C++ called Hide-And-Seek-Engine. By employing Data-Oriented Design (DOD) principles, explicit 64-byte cache-line alignment to remove false sharing, and a zero-copy PyTorch memory bridge using pinned memory and Direct Memory Access (DMA), our engine sustains throughput of up to 33,000,000 steps per second (SPS) in a single-agent, 1024-environment, decentralized observations on an AMD Ryzen 9950X (16 cores). Ten agents reduces FPS to 7M SPS with generating random actions contributing 1/3rd the total runtime for reference. The engine achieves a throughput increase of approximately 3,500$\times$ over the baseline single threaded vectorized NumPy implementation and successfully trains cooperative multi-agent policies via PPO, DQN, and SAC in minutes, validating both its performance and generality.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Monitoring Neural Training with Topology: A Footprint-Predictable Collapse Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26984)
*Alexander Kalinowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种拓扑感知的在线监控方法（MMHM+CI），用于检测神经表示中的表征崩溃，在LLM微调和时序知识图谱嵌入训练中提供早期预警信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征崩溃（表示变得各向异性并失去多尺度结构）会在性能指标反应之前就侵蚀下游性能，需要一种能够在训练中早期检测该现象的监控方法。

Method: 将模块化莫尔斯同调维护（MMHM）与复合崩溃指数（CI）相结合，通过稀疏编辑和离散莫尔斯匹配实现快速增量更新，避免每个epoch重建复形。

Result: 在LLM微调和时序知识图谱嵌入训练中，CI提供了低延迟的早期预警信号，适合在训练中进行干预。

Conclusion: 提出的拓扑感知在线监控器能够有效检测表征崩溃，为训练中的及时干预提供支持。

Abstract: Representational collapse, where embeddings become anisotropic and lose multi-scale structure, can erode downstream performance long before performance metrics react. We propose an online, topology-aware monitor for evolving neural representations that couples Modular Morse Homology Maintenance (MMHM) with a composite Collapse Index (CI). Instead of rebuilding complexes each epoch, we apply sparse edits at a fixed scale and maintain a discrete Morse matching, yielding fast, incremental updates. Across LLM fine-tuning and temporal KGE training, CI provides a low-latency early-warning signal suitable for in-training interventions. Code and experimental scripts will be released publicly

</details>


### [109] [Simple Self-Conditioning Adaptation for Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26985)
*Michael Cardei,Huu Binh Ta,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自条件掩码扩散模型（SCMDM），一种后训练适配方法，通过让每个去噪步骤基于模型自身之前的干净状态预测进行条件化，从而提升了标准掩码扩散模型在跨步细化上的能力。该方法无需从头训练、无需辅助模型、不增加采样时的去噪器评估次数，在多个领域（文本、图像、分子、基因组）中显著优于基线模型，生成困惑度降低近50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码扩散模型在反向更新中，若某位置仍为掩码状态，模型会丢弃该位置之前的干净状态预测，导致仍需从掩码标记出发反复推断。这种设计制约了跨步细化能力，限制了生成质量。

Method: 提出SCMDM，对已训练好的掩码扩散模型进行后训练适配：每个去噪步骤中，模型以自身前一步的干净状态预测作为额外条件输入。该方法引入极小的架构改动，不依赖循环隐状态路径或辅助参考模型，且不增加采样时的去噪器评估次数。论文还发现，后训练场景下局部自条件的50% dropout策略是次优的，而当模型自身生成的干净估计足够可信时，专注于细化优于混合条件与无条件目标。

Result: 在多个领域取得一致提升：OWT训练模型上生成困惑度从42.89降至23.72（降低近50%）；在离散图像合成、小分子生成、基因组分布建模的保真度上均有显著改进。

Conclusion: SCMDM是一种简单有效的后训练适配方法，通过利用模型自身的先前干净预测进行条件化，大幅提升了掩码扩散模型的生成质量，且无需从头训练或增加采样计算开销。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate discrete sequences by iterative denoising under an absorbing masking process. In standard masked diffusion, if a token remains masked after a reverse update, the model discards its clean-state prediction for that position. Thus, still-masked positions must be repeatedly inferred from the mask token alone. This design choice limits cross-step refinement. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a simple, yet effective, post-training adaptation for MDMs that conditions each denoising step on the model's own previous clean-state predictions. The resulting method, called Self-Conditioned Masked Diffusion Models (SCMDM), requires minimal architectural change, does not introduce a recurrent latent-state pathway, does not rely on an auxiliary reference model, and adds no extra denoiser evaluations during sampling. This is an important departure from partial self-conditioning approaches which requires expensive model training from scratch. In particular, the paper shows that partial self-conditioning, including the commonly used 50% dropout strategy for training self-conditioned models from scratch, is suboptimal in the post-training regime. Instead, once the model's self-generated clean-state estimates become informative, the specialization to refinement is preferable to mixing conditional and unconditional objectives. SCMDM is evaluated across multiple domains, demonstrating consistent improvement over vanilla MDM baselines, achieving nearly a 50% reduction in generative perplexity on OWT-trained models (42.89 to 23.72), alongside strong improvements in discretized image synthesis quality, small molecular generation, and enhanced fidelity in genomic distribution modeling.

</details>


### [110] [People-Centred Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26991)
*Zheng Zhang,Milad Masroor,Cuong Nguyen,Tahir Hassan,Yuanhong Chen,David Rosewarne,Kevin Wells,Thanh-Toan Do,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有数据驱动的医疗AI在诊断准确性上表现出色，但临床采纳率低。本文提出People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan)框架，通过动态门控机制在医生工作负载约束下联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流效率，并引入FairHAI基准评估三者权衡。实验表明PecMan优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的医疗AI在准确性上已取得显著进展，但未被广泛临床采纳。作者推测原因在于：(1) 未充分优化跨不同患者群体的公平性能（性能偏差导致监管障碍）；(2) 工作流集成不足（自动化干扰临床常规、降低人机协作质量、削弱临床医生采纳意愿）。现有研究（如L2D、L2C和AI公平性）通常孤立处理这些问题，忽视了它们的相互依赖性和临床环境的实际约束（如医生可用性受限）。

Method: 提出People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan)框架，这是一个在医生工作量约束下，通过动态门控机制将病例分配给AI、医生或两者联合处理的人机协作框架，联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流效率。同时引入FairHAI基准，用于评估准确性、公平性和医生工作量之间的权衡。

Result: 在FairHAI基准上的实验表明，PecMan在所有评估维度上 consistently 优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PecMan通过联合优化公平性、准确性和工作流集成，为更可信和临床可行的AI系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-centric medical AI have produced highly accurate diagnostic systems, but the emphasis on data curation and performance metrics has not translated into widespread clinical adoption. We conjecture that this limited uptake stems from insufficient attention dedicated to the optimisation of fair performance across diverse patient populations and to workflow integration: performance biases can create regulatory barriers, and poorly integrated automation can disrupt clinical routines, degrade the quality of human-AI collaboration, and reduce clinicians' willingness to adopt AI tools. Prior work on workflow integration (e.g., Learning to Defer (L2D) and Learning to Complement (L2C)) and AI fairness has typically examined these challenges in isolation, overlooking their natural interdependence and the practical constraints of clinical environments, such as restricted clinician availability. We propose People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan), a human-AI framework that jointly optimises fairness, diagnostic accuracy, and workflow effectiveness through a dynamic gating mechanism that assigns cases to AI, clinicians, or both under clinician workload constraints. We also introduce the Fairness and Human-Centred AI (FairHAI) benchmark for evaluating trade-offs between accuracy, fairness, and clinician workload. Experiments using this benchmark show that PecMan consistently outperforms existing methods, paving the way for more trustworthy and clinically viable AI systems. Code will be available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [111] [Automatic Causal Fairness Analysis with LLM-Generated Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27011)
*Alessia Berarducci,Eric Rossetto,Alessandro Antonucci,Marco Zaffalon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍FairMind原型系统，基于因果反事实推理自动评估数据集层面的公平性，利用LLM生成报告，并提出序数保护变量和连续目标的新分解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoML框架大多未考虑训练数据和预测中可能存在的公平性缺失问题，需要自动化的公平性分析工具来弥补这一空白。

Method: 基于Plečko和Bareinboim提出的标准公平模型，利用反事实查询计算因果效应，通过封闭形式计算实现公平性评估，并利用LLM在零样本设置下生成公平性报告。

Result: 通过示例展示了相比LLM直接分析的优势，并扩展至序数保护变量和连续目标场景，提出新的分解结果。

Conclusion: FairMind能够以因果推断为基础，自动化地评估数据集层面的公平性，借助LLM增强可解释性，为AutoML引入公平性保障提供了可行路径。

Abstract: AutoML, intended as the process of automating the application of machine learning to real-world problems, is a key step for AI popularisation. Most AutoML frameworks are not accounting for the potential lack of fairness in the training data and in the corresponding predictions. We introduce \textsc{FairMind}, a software prototype aiming to automatise fairness analysis at the dataset level. We achieve that by resorting to the assumptions of the \emph{standard fairness model}, recently proposed by Plečko and Bareinboim. This allows for a sound fairness evaluation in terms of causal effects, based on \emph{counterfactual} queries involving the target, possibly confounders and mediators, and the different values of an input feature we regard as \emph{protected}. After the necessary data preprocessing, the tool implements a closed-form computation of the effects. LLMs are consequently exploited to generate accurate reports on the fairness levels detected in the training dataset. We achieve that in a zero-shot setup and show by examples the expected advantages with respect to a direct analysis performed by the LLM. To favour applications, extensions to ordinal protected variable and continuous targets and novel decomposition results are also discussed.

</details>


### [112] [Fidelity, Diversity, and Privacy: A Multi-Dimensional LLM Evaluation for Clinical Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27014)
*Guillermo Iglesias,Gema Bello-Orgaz,María Navas-Loro,Cristian Ramirez-Atencia,Mercè Salvador Robert,Enrique Baca-Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5等大语言模型根据ICD-10编码生成合成精神健康评估报告，并建立包含语义保真度、词汇多样性、隐私/抄袭的三维评估框架，结果表明该方法能生成临床连贯、多样且隐私安全的合成数据，有效扩充临床NLP训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量标注医疗数据（尤其是精神健康领域）稀缺，隐私法规限制数据共享，亟需一种既能生成合成数据又不泄露患者隐私的替代方案。

Method: 使用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5三种LLM，以特定ICD-10编码为条件生成合成精神健康评估报告；同时建立三维评估框架（语义保真度、词汇多样性、隐私/抄袭检测）以避免模式坍塌或隐私泄露。

Result: 所有模型均能生成临床连贯、词汇多样且隐私安全的合成诊断报告，有效扩充临床NLP训练数据且不损害患者机密性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的合成数据生成结合多维评估框架，有望解决精神健康领域医疗数据稀缺与隐私保护之间的矛盾，为临床NLP任务提供可行的数据扩充方案。

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated medical data, particularly in mental health, poses a significant bottleneck for training robust machine learning models. Privacy regulations restrict data sharing, making synthetic data generation a promising alternative. The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in a data augmentation pipeline could be leveraged as an alternative in this field. In the proposed methodology, DeepSeek-R1, OpenBioLLM-Llama3 and Qwen 3.5 are used to generate synthetic mental health evaluation reports conditioned on specific International Classification of Diseases, Tenth Revision (ICD-10) codes. Because naive text generation can lead to mode collapse or privacy breaches (memorization), a comprehensive evaluation framework is introduced. The generated diagnostic texts are assessed across three dimensions: semantic fidelity, lexical diversity, and privacy/plagiarism. The results demonstrate that all models can generate clinically coherent, diverse, and privacy-safe synthetic reports, significantly expanding the available training data for clinical natural language processing tasks without compromising patient confidentiality.

</details>


### [113] [ANCORA: Learning to Question via Manifold-Anchored Self-Play for Verifiable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27644)
*Chengcao Yang,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ANCORA框架，通过交替生成规范与求解验证的自改进循环，使语言模型无需人类监督即可自我提升，在形式验证任务中将pass@1从26.6%提升至81.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型能否从"学习回答"转向"学习提问"——自主生成可验证问题、求解问题并利用反馈实现无人类监督的自我改进。

Method: ANCORA包含三个核心机制：1) 双层组间相对更新，耦合Proposer跨规范的奖励优势与Solver跨求解尝试的优势；2) 迭代自蒸馏SFT，先投影到有效输出流形再进行RL；3) UCB引导的课程DAG，仅通过严格筛选的新颖、可验证规范进行增长。

Result: 在Dafny2Verus任务上，pass@1从SFT基线26.6%提升至81.5%，超过PSV自对弈基线15.8个百分点（PSV使用1-shot推理）；在迁移设置下，MBPP和HumanEval分别达36.2%和17.2% pass@1。

Conclusion: 稀疏验证器反馈会导致Proposer崩溃，而所提出的稳定器机制是必要的；ANCORA框架成功实现了无监督自我改进，在形式验证和代码生成任务上均取得了显著提升。

Abstract: We propose a paradigm shift from learning to answer to learning to question: can a language model generate verifiable problems, solve them, and turn the resulting feedback into self-improvement without human supervision? We introduce ANCORA, an anchored-curriculum framework in which a unified policy alternates between a Proposer that synthesizes novel specifications and a Solver that produces verified solutions. ANCORA rests on three load-bearing mechanisms: a two-level group-relative update that couples Proposer advantages across specifications with Solver advantages across solution attempts; iterative self-distilled SFT that projects the base model onto its valid-output manifold before RL; and a UCB-guided Curriculum DAG that grows only through strictly filtered, novel, Solver-verified specifications. These stabilizers are necessary because sparse verifier feedback otherwise drives Proposer collapse even under MLRL-aligned rewards. Instantiated in Verus, ANCORA lifts Dafny2Verus pass@1 from a 26.6% SFT baseline to 81.5% in the test-time-training setting under 0-shot evaluation, outperforming the PSV self-play baseline by 15.8 points despite PSV using 1-shot inference; in a separate transfer setting, training from Dafny2Verus seeds yields 36.2% and 17.2% pass@1 on held-out MBPP and HumanEval.

</details>


### [114] [Cross-Subject Generalization for EEG Decoding: A Survey of Deep Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27033)
*Taida Li,Yujun Yan,Fei Dou,Wenzhan Song,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了针对跨被试EEG解码中个体间差异导致的领域偏移问题，将跨被试设定形式化为多源域问题，并建立了严格评估协议。核心贡献在于将现有深度学习方法归纳为特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习四大方法族，最后探讨了理论局限性、被试身份的结构性价值以及EEG基础模型三个关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在跨被试EEG解码中面临高个体间变异性带来的严重领域偏移问题，这阻碍了模型在未见被试上的泛化能力。

Method: 将跨被试设定形式化为多源域问题，建立严格的被试独立评估协议，并基于特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习四个方法族对现有文献进行系统分类。

Result: 提出了一个系统的分类学框架，将跨被试EEG解码的深度学习方法归纳为四大方法族。

Conclusion: 指出了当前方法的理论局限性、被试身份的结构性价值以及EEG基础模型的emergence是推动鲁棒真实世界解码的三个关键要素。

Abstract: Deep learning for cross-subject EEG decoding is hindered by high inter-subject variability, which introduces a severe domain shift between training and unseen test subjects. This survey presents a comprehensive review of deep learning methodologies specifically engineered to address this cross-subject generalization challenge. To ground this analysis, we formalize the cross-subject setting as a multi-source domain problem and delineate the rigorous, subject-independent evaluation protocols required for valid assessment. Central to this survey is a systematic taxonomy of the current literature into discrete methodological families, including feature alignment, adversarial learning, feature disentanglement, and contrastive learning. We conclude by examining three critical elements for advancing robust, real-world decoding: the theoretical limitations of current methodologies, the structural value of subject identity, and the emergence of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [115] [Detecting Clinical Discrepancies in Health Coaching Agents: A Dual-Stream Memory and Reconciliation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27045)
*Samuel L Pugh,Eric Yang,Alexander Muir Sutherland,Alessandra Breschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双流记忆架构（Dual-Stream Memory Architecture），将患者自述与结构化临床记录（FHIR）严格分离，并通过 reconciliation engine 对两类记忆进行一致性评估。在675次纵向健康辅导会话中，引擎检测到84.4%的临床差异，安全关键召回率86.7%，并量化了13.6%的误差级联现象——主要源于从非结构化对话中提取记忆时的临床细节丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体从单次对话工具转向管理长期医疗健康的持久系统时，其记忆架构面临两难：患者自述虽实时但存在回忆偏差，电子健康记录（EHR）虽经医学验证但常过时。通用记忆系统倾向于用用户最新陈述覆盖旧事实，这在临床场景中可能导致安全风险。

Method: 提出双流记忆架构：将患者叙事与结构化临床记录（FHIR）严格分离，并引入 Reconciliation Engine，对每条提取的记忆与患者FHIR档案进行比对，按类型、严重程度及涉及的FHIR资源分类差异。使用26名患者、675次纵向健康辅导会话的混合数据集（真实医患对话+合成FHIR临床场景）进行评估。

Result: 隔离测试中检测到84.4%的设计临床差异，安全关键召回率达86.7%。将提取与一致性评估耦合后，直接量化出13.6%的误差级联，追溯发现退化主要源于从非结构化对话中提取记忆时的临床细节丢失，而非下游分类错误。

Conclusion: 验证了将患者报告的记忆与临床记录进行验证既是可行的，也是纵向健康智能体安全部署的必要条件。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents transition from single-session tools to persistent systems managing longitudinal healthcare journeys, their memory architectures face a critical challenge: reconciling two imperfect sources of truth. The patient's evolving self-report is current but prone to recall bias, while the Electronic Health Record (EHR) is medically validated but frequently stale. General-purpose agent memory systems optimize for coherence by overwriting older facts with the user's latest statement, a pattern that risks safety failures when applied to clinical data. We introduce a Dual-Stream Memory Architecture that strictly separates the patient narrative from the structured clinical record (FHIR), governed by a dedicated Reconciliation Engine that evaluates every extracted memory against the patient's FHIR profile and classifies discrepancies by type, severity, and the specific FHIR resources involved. We evaluate this architecture on 26 patients across 675 longitudinal wellness coaching sessions, using a hybrid dataset that interleaves real provider-patient transcripts with synthetic, FHIR-grounded clinical scenarios. In isolated testing, the engine detects 84.4% of designed clinical discrepancies with 86.7% safety-critical recall. By coupling extraction and reconciliation evaluation on the same data, we directly quantify a 13.6% error cascade, tracing the degradation to clinical details lost during memory extraction from unstructured conversation rather than to downstream classification errors. These findings establish that validating patient-reported memories against clinical records is both feasible and necessary for safe deployment of longitudinal health agents.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Rate Transfer in Normalized Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27077)
*Boris Shigida,Boris Hanin,Andrey Gromov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出νGPT参数化方法，解决了nGPT（归一化Transformer）在学习率迁移方面的缺陷，实现了跨宽度、深度和token序列长度的超参数迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nGPT虽然实现了显著的训练加速且无需权重衰减或学习率预热，但其超参数无法在不同模型规模和token序列长度之间有效迁移，缺乏学习率迁移能力。

Method: 结合数值实验与对齐指数（alignment exponents）的理论指导，重新审视并修改了μP的超参数迁移方法，提出了新的nGPT参数化方案——νGPT。

Result: νGPT在宽度、深度和token序列长度三个维度上均实现了学习率的有效迁移，通过了广泛的经验验证。

Conclusion: νGPT成功解决了nGPT在学习率迁移上的局限性，为归一化Transformer架构提供了一种更具可扩展性的参数化方案。

Abstract: The Normalized Transformer, or nGPT (arXiv:2410.01131) achieves impressive training speedups and does not require weight decay or learning rate warmup. However, despite having hyperparameters that explicitly scale with model size, we observe that nGPT does not exhibit learning rate transfer across model dimension and token horizon. To rectify this, we combine numerical experiments with a principled use of alignment exponents (arXiv:2407.05872) to revisit and modify the $μ$P approach to hyperparameter transfer (arXiv:2011.14522). The result is a novel nGPT parameterization we call $ν$GPT. Through extensive empirical validation, we find $ν$GPT exhibits learning rate transfer across width, depth, and token horizon.

</details>


### [117] [Co-Evolving Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27083)
*Naibin Gu,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Chuanyu Qin,Dingyu Yao,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoPD方法，通过并行训练专家并在RLVR训练过程中引入双向在线策略蒸馏，实现多模态能力的一体化整合，显著优于混合RLVR和MOPD等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范式（混合RLVR和分阶段OPD）在整合多专家能力时存在能力损失问题：混合RLVR受限于跨能力发散代价，而先训练专家再进行OPD的流程因师生行为模式差距大而无法充分吸收教师能力。

Method: 提出Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD)：并行训练各领域专家，在每个专家进行RLVR训练的过程中（而非完成后）引入在线策略蒸馏（OPD），专家之间互为教师（双向蒸馏），实现协同演化。

Result: 实验表明CoPD成功实现了文本、图像和视频推理能力的一体化整合，显著优于混合RLVR和MOPD等强基线方法，甚至超越领域专用专家模型。

Conclusion: CoPD的模型并行训练范式可能启发新的训练缩放范式，有效解决了多能力整合中的行为模式不一致与知识互补性之间的矛盾。

Abstract: RLVR and OPD have become standard paradigms for post-training. We provide a unified analysis of these two paradigms in consolidating multiple expert capabilities into a single model, identifying capability loss in different ways: mixed RLVR suffers from inter-capability divergence cost, while the pipeline of first training experts and then performing OPD, though avoiding divergence, fails to fully absorb teacher capabilities due to large behavioral pattern gaps between teacher and student. We propose Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD), which encourages parallel training of experts and introduces OPD during each expert's ongoing RLVR training rather than after complete expert training, with experts serving as mutual teachers (making OPD bidirectional) to co-evolve. This enables more consistent behavioral patterns among experts while maintaining sufficient complementary knowledge throughout. Experiments validate that CoPD achieves all-in-one integration of text, image, and video reasoning capabilities, significantly outperforming strong baselines such as mixed RLVR and MOPD, and even surpassing domain-specific experts. The model parallel training pattern offered by CoPD may inspire a novel training scaling paradigm.

</details>


### [118] [AutoSP: Unlocking Long-Context LLM Training Via Compiler-Based Sequence Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27089)
*Ahan Gupta,Zhihao Wang,Neel Dani,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSP是首个自动优化LLM长上下文训练的方案，通过自动序列并行和长上下文感知的激活检查点技术，在NVIDIA和AMD硬件上分别实现2.7倍和2.5倍的训练上下文长度提升，且运行时性能开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练库缺乏针对长上下文训练的易用抽象，主要关注大参数模型的优化（如ZeRO-3/FSDP、张量和流水线并行），用户需要重写训练库来集成序列并行等复杂长上下文优化，这需要深入专业知识且降低开发效率。

Method: AutoSP采用自动化编译方法，主要包含两项核心优化：1）自动序列并行（automated sequence parallelism）；2）长上下文感知的激活检查点（long-context aware activation-checkpointing）。

Result: 在NVIDIA硬件上训练上下文长度提升达2.7倍，在AMD硬件上提升达2.5倍，且相比手工优化的基线方案，运行时性能开销极小。

Conclusion: AutoSP作为首个自动化长上下文优化方案，能够显著提升LLM的可训练性，同时兼容多种硬件平台，降低了长上下文训练的技术门槛和开发成本。

Abstract: Large-language-models (LLMs) demonstrate enormous utility in long-context tasks which require processing prompts that consist of tens to hundreds of thousands of tokens. However, existing LLM training libraries do not provide easy to use abstractions to optimize for long-context training, instead focusing on optimizations for models with large parameter counts through ZeRO-3/FSDP, Tensor and Pipeline parallelism. This forces users to rewrite LLM training libraries to incorporate compositions of various complex long-context optimizations, such as sequence-parallelism, to training pipelines; a process that requires in-depth expertise, reducing developer productivity. To tackle these challenges, we introduce AutoSP: the first automated solution to automatically optimize LLM training for longer-contexts. AutoSP compiles models and applies a targeted set of optimizations: automated sequence parallelism, and long-context aware activation-checkpointing, to drastically enhance LLM trainability at negligible cost to throughput. Our evaluation demonstrates AutoSP's capability on both NVIDIA and AMD hardware, increasing training contexts by upto 2.7$\times$ and 2.5$\times$ respectively over competitive hand-written baseline at negligible cost to runtime performance.

</details>


### [119] [Anomaly Detection in Soil Heavy Metal Contamination Using Unsupervised Learning for Environmental Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27102)
*Isaac Tettey Adjokatse,Samuel Senyo Koranteng,George Yamoah Afrifa,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Marcellin Atemkeng,Joseph Bremang Tandoh,Kow Ahor Essel-Yorke,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究应用无监督机器学习框架（Isolation Forest、PCA重构误差、DBSCAN及共识方法）检测加纳中部地区12个垃圾场及对照住宅区土壤中八种重金属（As、Cd、Cr、Cu、Hg、Ni、Pb、Zn）的异常污染模式。共识方法识别出6个稳健异常样本（7.7%），均集中于S3站点，其平均HI值比正常样本高70-80%且超过阈值HI=1，PCA重构误差与HI呈强正相关（r≈0.8），揭示了三种异常类型：S3的极端Cu富集、S4/S5的异常低Ni、S9-S12的中等多金属（Pb-Zn）共升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加纳快速城市化地区（尤其是非法垃圾场）的重金属土壤污染对环境和公共健康构成持续威胁，传统聚合健康指数（如HI、ILCR）虽能评估风险但缺乏对异常污染模式的精细检测能力，亟需更客观、精细的异常识别方法以实现靶向优先干预。

Method: 采集78份土壤样本（12个垃圾场+对照住宅区），检测As、Cd、Cr、Cu、Hg、Ni、Pb、Zn八种金属浓度并计算HI和ILCR；使用Isolation Forest、PCA重构误差、DBSCAN三种无监督方法单独检测异常，再通过共识方法（取三者交集）筛选稳健异常样本。

Result: Isolation Forest和PCA重构误差各识别12个异常（15.4%），DBSCAN未检测到密度孤立点；共识法识别6个稳健异常（7.7%），全部位于S3站点。异常样本平均HI值较正常样本高70-80%，所有共识异常HI>1。PCA重构误差与HI呈强正相关（r≈0.8）。识别出三种异常类型：S3极端Cu富集、S4/S5异常低Ni、S9-S12中等Pb-Zn共升。

Conclusion: 无监督机器学习能够从聚合健康指数之外提供更精细、客观的异常污染检测结果，有助于实现靶向站点优先排序和风险知情环境管理。

Abstract: Soil contamination by heavy metals poses a persistent environmental and public health concern in rapidly urbanising regions of Ghana, particularly at unregulated waste disposal sites. This study applies an unsupervised machine learning framework to detect and characterise anomalous heavy metal contamination patterns in soils from twelve waste sites and residential controls in the Central Region, of Ghana. Concentrations of eight metals (As, Cd, Cr, Cu, Hg, Ni, Pb, Zn) were analysed alongside standard health risk indices, including the Hazard Index (HI) and Incremental Lifetime Cancer Risk (ILCR). Isolation Forest and PCA reconstruction error each identified $12$ anomalous samples ($15.4\%$ of $78$ samples), while DBSCAN detected no density-isolated noise points. A consensus approach isolated six robust anomalies ($7.7\%)$, all spatially concentrated at a single site (S3). Anomalies exhibited approximately $70$--$80\%$ higher mean HI values than normal samples, with all consensus anomalies exceeding the HI$=1$ threshold. PCA reconstruction error showed a strong positive association with HI ($r \approx 0.8$), indicating consistency between multivariate deviation and health risk. Three distinct anomaly types were identified: extreme Cu enrichment at S3, anomalously low Ni at S4/S5, and moderate multi-metal (Pb--Zn) co-elevation at S9--S12. The results demonstrate that unsupervised machine learning provides granular, objective insight beyond aggregate indices, enabling targeted site prioritisation and risk-informed environmental management.

</details>


### [120] [Better Models, Faster Training: Sigmoid Attention for single-cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27124)
*Vijay Sadashivaiah,Georgios Dasoulas,Judith Mueller,Soumya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在生物基础模型中使用sigmoid注意力机制替代softmax注意力，在六个单细胞数据集上实现更优的表征学习（细胞类型分离提升25%）、更快的训练速度（最高提升10%）和更稳定的训练过程。sigmoid注意力具有全局有界导数（≤0.25）和对角雅可比结构，从根本上消除了softmax的不稳定性。在1.6亿参数模型上，softmax梯度爆炸四个数量级而sigmoid保持稳定。同时开源了TritonSigmoid高效GPU内核，在H100上达到515 TFLOPS，优于FlashAttention-2和FlashSigmoid。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练稳定的生物基础模型需要重新思考注意力机制，因为softmax注意力存在固有的训练不稳定问题，如梯度爆炸和密集耦合，这限制了生物基础模型的可扩展性和训练效率。

Method: 提出使用sigmoid注意力作为softmax注意力的即插即用替代方案。从理论上分析了sigmoid注意力的全局有界导数（≤0.25）和对角雅可比结构，并实现了TritonSigmoid高效GPU内核（支持原生padding，在H100上达到515 TFLOPS）。

Result: 在六个单细胞数据集上，sigmoid注意力实现25%更高的细胞类型分离、更好的细胞类型凝聚指标和更低的验证损失；训练速度提升最高10%；在1.6亿参数双向注意力模型无梯度裁剪的8K序列压力测试中，softmax梯度爆炸四个数量级，sigmoid保持稳定。

Conclusion: Sigmoid注意力在理论和经验上均优于softmax注意力，特别适用于生物基础模型，能提供更稳定、更高效的训练和更好的表征学习。

Abstract: Training stable biological foundation models requires rethinking attention mechanisms: we find that using sigmoid attention as a drop in replacement for softmax attention a) produces better learned representations: on six diverse single-cell datasets, sigmoid achieves 25% higher cell-type separation, better cell-type cohesion metrics, and lower validation loss, b) faster training, models with sigmoid attention train up to 10% faster than their softmax counterparts, and c) more stable training by eliminating inherent sources of instability in softmax attention. We establish that sigmoid attention has globally bounded derivatives ($\leq 0.25$) as opposed to softmax, and a diagonal Jacobian structure in contrast with softmax's dense coupling, which together help alleviate training instabilities. In stress tests on 160M-parameter bidirectional attention models trained without gradient clipping on 8K-token sequences, softmax diverges catastrophically, with gradients exploding by four orders of magnitude, while sigmoid remains stable. Finally, we implement and open-source TritonSigmoid, an efficient GPU kernel that achieves 515 TFLOPS on H100 GPUs, outperforming both FlashAttention-2 and FlashSigmoid, with native padding support, which is essential for biological sequences. Our results establish sigmoid attention as both theoretically grounded and empirically superior for biological foundation models. Code is available at https://github.com/MSDLLCpapers/triton-sigmoid

</details>


### [121] [How to Guide Your Flow: Few-Step Alignment via Flow Map Reward Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27147)
*Jerry Y. Huang,Justin Lin,Sheel Shah,Kartik Nair,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Map Reward Guidance (FMRG)，将引导问题重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，利用流图实现无需训练、单轨迹的生成引导，在3次NFE下匹配或超越基线，实现数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导方法要么需要昂贵的多粒子多步方案，要么依赖理解不足的近似，缺乏高效且理论清晰的方案。

Method: 将引导重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，推导出算法层次结构；利用流图（flow map）同时进行积分和引导，提出FMRG框架，无需训练、单轨迹运行。

Result: 在文本到图像尺度上，FMRG在逆向问题、风格迁移、人类偏好和VLM奖励等任务中，以仅3次NFE匹配或超越基线，相比先前最优方法实现至少一个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: FMRG通过最优控制视角统一了现有方法，并利用流图实现了高效、训练免费的生成引导，显著提升速度。

Abstract: In generative modeling, we often wish to produce samples that maximize a user-specified reward such as aesthetic quality or alignment with human preferences, a problem known as guidance. Despite their widespread use, existing guidance methods either require expensive multi-particle, many-step schemes or rely on poorly understood approximations. We reformulate guidance as a deterministic optimal control problem, yielding a hierarchy of algorithms that subsumes existing approaches at the coarsest level. We show that the flow map, an object of significant recent interest for its role in fast inference, arises naturally in the optimal solution. Based on this observation, we propose Flow Map Reward Guidance (FMRG): a training-free, single-trajectory framework that uses the flow map to both integrate and guide the flow. At text-to-image scale, FMRG matches or surpasses baselines across inverse problems, style transfer, human preferences, and VLM rewards with as few as 3 NFEs, giving at least an order-of-magnitude speedup in comparison to prior state of the art.

</details>


### [122] [Generalizing the Geometry of Model Merging Through Frechet Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27155)
*Marvin F. da Silva,Mohammed Adnan,Felix Dangel,Sageev Oore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模型合并应使用Fréchet平均（在流形上最小化测地距离和），以实现对称性感知的合并，并针对LoRA的商流形几何给出了实用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数空间平均方法容易受到架构对称性的影响，因为其几何结构未考虑这些对称性。本文指出不仅几何结构需要对称性感知，平均过程本身也必须满足对称不变性。

Method: 将模型合并视为Fréchet平均问题，即在合适的流形上选择参数最小化测地距离之和。核心设计选择是整体几何（度量、流形、距离近似）的定义。在简化假设下，该方法包含了Fisher合并。针对LoRA的特殊情况，考虑其商流形几何，提出了实用算法。

Result: 理论证明了Fréchet平均在假设简化下能包含Fisher合并；针对LoRA对称性导致的商流形几何，指出了当前合并方法的局限性，并给出了对比实验。

Conclusion: 模型合并必须同时考虑几何结构和平均过程的对称不变性；Fréchet平均框架提供了一个通用解法，且对LoRA这种具有内在对称性的架构尤为重要。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine multiple models into one without additional training. Naïve parameter-space averaging can be fragile under architectural symmetries, as their geometry does not take them into account. In this work we show that not only the geometry, but also the averaging procedure itself, must be symmetry-invariant to achieve symmetry-aware merges. Consequently, we propose a general solution: merging as Fréchet averaging, i.e., selecting parameters that minimize a sum of geodesic distances on an appropriate manifold. In this view, the key design choice is the overall geometry, i.e., the choice of metric, manifold, and distance approximation, that determines what it means for two models to be "close". We show that Fréchet averaging, combined with simplifying assumptions, contains Fisher merging. Building on this, we examine the particular case of low-rank adapters (LoRA), whose symmetries induce a distinct geometry: that of a quotient manifold. We outline the limitations of current LoRA merging methods, propose a practical algorithm for this setting, and show how they compare with other commonly used approaches.

</details>


### [123] [Distributional Alignment Games for Answer-Level Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27166)
*Mehryar Mohri,Jon Schneider,Yifan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于博弈论的分布对齐框架，将答案级微调（ALFT）这一难以直接优化的边际化问题转化为可求解的投影问题，并证明其纳什均衡等价于原优化问题的解。该框架统一了多样性和自改进方法，并推出了与GRPO兼容的高效算法Coherence-GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型微调通常依赖完整推理轨迹进行优化，但实际场景中往往只关心最终答案的正确性而非推理过程。直接优化答案级目标需要对海量潜在推理路径进行边际化求和，计算上不可行。

Method: 提出一种博弈论框架，将ALFT形式化为一个两玩家（策略生成器与目标辅助分布）之间的分布对齐博弈，通过变分视角将边际化问题转化为投影问题，并推导出与GRPO兼容的高效算法Coherence-GRPO。

Result: 该框架统一了多样性和自改进（连贯性）等近期方法，Coherence-GRPO在数学推理任务上取得了显著的复杂度降低。

Conclusion: 博弈论视角下的分布对齐框架能够有效解决答案级微调中的计算瓶颈，为语言模型在推理任务上的高效优化提供了新的理论支撑和实用算法。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of \emph{Answer-Level Fine-Tuning} (ALFT), where the goal is to optimize a language model based on the correctness or properties of its final answers, rather than the specific reasoning traces used to produce them. Directly optimizing answer-level objectives is computationally intractable due to the need to marginalize over the vast space of latent reasoning paths. To overcome this, we propose a general game-theoretical framework that lifts the problem to a \emph{Distributional Alignment Game}. We formulate ALFT as a two-player game between a Policy (the generator) and a Target (an auxiliary distribution). We prove that the Nash Equilibrium of this game corresponds exactly to the solution of the original answer-level optimization problem. This variational perspective transforms the intractable marginalization problem into a tractable projection problem. We demonstrate that this framework unifies recent approaches to diversity and self-improvement (coherence) and provide efficient algorithms compatible with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), such as Coherence-GRPO, yielding significant complexity gains in mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [124] [Preserving Temporal Dynamics in Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27182)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于MCMC的模型无关框架，通过强制保持相邻时间点之间的经验转移统计一致性，来缓解条件生成模型在多变量时间序列生成中的分布偏移和时间漂移问题，实验证明该方法在多个指标上优于现有GAN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GAN的时间序列生成方法主要关注边缘数据分布的匹配，忽视了原始多元时间序列中自然存在的时间动态特性，导致生成序列出现分布偏移和时间漂移，降低了合成序列的保真度。

Method: 提出一个模型无关的MCMC框架，通过强制生成序列与原始数据在相邻时间点之间的经验转移统计保持一致，来校正条件生成模型在序列生成过程中累积的偏差，并提供了理论分析。

Result: 在Lorenz、Licor、ETTh和ILI数据集上使用五种GAN方法（RCGAN、GCWGAN、TimeGAN、SigCWGAN、AECGAN）的实验表明，MCMC框架在自相关对齐、偏度误差、峰度误差、R²、判别分数和预测分数上均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 合成时间序列需要显式地保持转移规律而非仅依赖对抗性分布匹配，这为改进时间序列数据的生成建模提供了一个有原则性的方向。

Abstract: Time-series data augmentation plays a crucial role in regression-oriented forecasting tasks, where limited data restricts the performance of deep learning models. While Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) have shown promise in synthetic time-series generation, existing approaches primarily focus on matching marginal data distributions and often overlook the temporal dynamics that naturally exist in the original multivariate time series. When generating multivariate time series, this mismatch leads to distribution shift and temporal drift, thereby degrading the fidelity of the synthetic sequences.
  In this work, we propose a model-agnostic Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC)-based framework to mitigate distribution shift and preserve temporal dynamics in synthetic time series. We provide a theoretical analysis of how conditional generative models accumulate deviations under sequential generation and demonstrate that the MCMC algorithm can correct these discrepancies by enforcing consistency with empirical transition statistics between neighboring time points.
  Extensive experiments on the Lorenz, Licor, ETTh, and ILI datasets using RCGAN, GCWGAN, TimeGAN, SigCWGAN, and AECGAN demonstrate that the proposed MCMC framework consistently improves autocorrelation alignment, skewness error, kurtosis error, R$^2$, discriminative score, and predictive score. These results suggest that synthetic time series consistent with the original data require explicit preservation of transition laws rather than solely relying on adversarial distribution matching, thereby offering a principled direction for improving generative modeling of time-series data.

</details>


### [125] [Analytical Correction for Subsampling Bias in Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27239)
*Jiaru Zhang,Zeyun Deng,Juanwu Lu,Ziran Wang,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对漂移模型（Drifting Models）中minibatch质心估计存在O(1/n)偏差的问题，提出了解析偏差校正（ABC）方法——一种闭式插件调整，将偏差降至O(1/n²)，且不增加一阶总方差、保持凸包包含性。实验验证了理论缩放关系，并在CIFAR-10上以极小代码开销提升了FID和训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移模型中使用的minibatch软最大加权质心是目标质心的有偏估计，存在O(1/n)的点态偏差，该偏差源于softmax自归一化。纠正此偏差需要对完整分布求期望，这是不可行的，因此需要近似方法。

Method: 从批内统计量近似主导偏差项，提出解析偏差校正（ABC）——一种闭式插件调整方法。理论上证明了ABC将偏差从O(1/n)降至O(1/n²)，且不会引入一阶总方差增加，并保持校正后质心的凸包包含性。

Result: Toy实验验证了O(1/n)和O(1/n²)的理论缩放关系。在CIFAR-10上，ABC降低了FID并加速了训练，在小n（偏差最显著时）收益最大。

Conclusion: ABC提供了一种简单高效（仅需额外两行代码、编译执行下时间开销可忽略）的偏差校正方案，在保持理论保证的同时显著提升了实际性能。

Abstract: Drifting models are capable one-step generative models trained to follow a drifting field. The field combines attractive and repulsive softmax-weighted centroids over the data and current-generator distributions. In practice, only a minibatch of $n$ samples from each distribution is available, and each centroid is approximated by an empirical estimate. In this paper, we begin by showing that the minibatch centroid is in general a biased estimator of the target centroid, with a pointwise $O(1/n)$ bias arising from softmax self-normalization. Correcting this bias requires the expectation over the full distribution, which is intractable. We instead approximate the leading bias term from in-batch statistics and propose Analytical Bias Correction (ABC), a closed-form plug-in adjustment. We prove that ABC reduces the bias from $O(1/n)$ to $O(1/n^2)$, introduces no first-order increase in total variance, and preserves convex-hull containment of the corrected centroid. In practice, ABC requires only two additional lines of code and has negligible wall-time overhead under compiled execution. Toy experiments confirm the theoretical $O(1/n)$ and $O(1/n^2)$ scaling. On CIFAR-10, ABC reduces FID and trains faster, with the largest gains at small $n$, where the bias is most significant.

</details>


### [126] [AutoREC: A software platform for developing reinforcement learning agents for equivalent circuit model generation from electrochemical impedance spectroscopy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27266)
*Ali Jaberi,Yonatan Kurniawan,Robert Black,Shayan Mousavi M.,Kabir Verma,Zoya Sadighi,Santiago Miret,Jason Hattrick-Simpers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoREC是一个开源Python工具包，利用深度强化学习（Double DQN with PER）从电化学阻抗谱数据中自动生成等效电路模型，在合成数据上成功率超过99.6%，并能泛化到多种实际电化学系统（电池、腐蚀、OER、CO₂还原）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等效电路模型构建依赖人工试错，需要领域专家且难以规模化，无法满足自主实验管线（如自驱动实验室）对自动化和可扩展性的需求。

Method: 将ECM构建建模为马尔可夫决策过程，采用Double Deep Q-Network结合优先经验回放（Prioritized Experience Replay）进行强化学习，并设计了专用的死循环缓解策略来应对复杂动作空间中的搜索问题。

Result: 在合成数据集上成功率超过99.6%；对未见过的真实实验EIS数据（电池、腐蚀、析氧反应、CO₂还原系统）展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: AutoREC是一个有前景的自适应数据驱动ECM生成平台，有望集成到自动化电化学工作流中。

Abstract: This paper introduces AutoREC, an open-source Python package for developing reinforcement learning (RL) agents to automatically generate equivalent circuit models (ECMs) from electrochemical impedance spectroscopy (EIS) data. While ECMs are a standard framework for interpreting EIS data, traditional identification is typically based on manual trial-and-error, which requires domain experts and limits scalability, particularly in autonomous experimental pipelines such as self-driving laboratories. AutoREC addresses this challenge by formulating ECM construction as a sequential decision-making problem within a Markov Decision Process framework. It implements a Double Deep Q-Network with prioritized experience replay, along with a dedicated dead-loop mitigation strategy, to efficiently explore a complex action space for circuit generation. To demonstrate the capabilities of the platform, we trained an RL agent using AutoREC and evaluated its strengths and limitations across diverse datasets, while also discussing possible strategies to mitigate these limitations in future agent designs. The trained agent achieved a success rate exceeding $99.6\%$ on synthetic datasets and demonstrated strong generalization to unseen experimental EIS data from batteries, corrosion, oxygen evolution reaction, and CO$_2$ reduction systems. These results position AutoREC as a promising platform for adaptive and data-driven ECM generation, with potential for integration into automated electrochemical workflows.

</details>


### [127] [Predicting Covariate-Driven Spatial Deformation for Nonstationary Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27280)
*Minghao Gu,Weizhi Lin,Qiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于协变量驱动的空间形变方法，用于非平稳高斯过程的外推预测。通过将形变映射为李代数中的速度场，并证明可截断多协变量的高阶交互作用，建立了简洁的形变函数形式，并开发了高效的估计-推断算法。在制造和地统计学领域的模拟与案例研究中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间形变方法是静态的，无法利用协变量信息来预测新协变量条件下的非平稳高斯过程，限制了其在变化环境中的泛化能力。本文旨在解决这一预测建模问题。

Method: 将微分同胚形变空间与欧几里得协变量空间通过李代数中的速度场连接；证明在适度物理假设下可截断多协变量的高阶交互作用，从而建立简洁的形变函数形式；开发适用于有限协变量-形变样本对的估计-推断算法。

Result: 通过模拟研究和两个案例研究（制造领域和地统计学领域）验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提出的协变量驱动形变方法能够有效实现非平稳高斯过程在协变量变化条件下的外推预测，理论截断保证了方法的可操作性。

Abstract: Nonstationary Gaussian processes (GPs) are essential for modeling complex, locally heterogeneous spatial data. A common modeling approach is the spatial deformation method that warps the domain to recover isotropy. However, this static method does not account for changes in spatial correlation induced by covariates, limiting its ability to predict nonstationary GPs under new covariate conditions.
  To enable predictive modeling of the deformation method, we propose to model the spatial deformation as a function of covariates. The spaces of diffeomorphic deformations and Euclidean covariate vectors are connected by characterizing deformations as generated by velocity fields living in a Lie algebra. To overcome the estimation instability caused by high-order interactions between multiple covariates in a general Lie algebra, we prove that those interactions can be truncated with a moderate physical assumption.
